ippeṟṟiyavām patikaṅkaḷē irukkuvētam muḻuvatum nirampik kiṭaṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉōraṉṉa patikaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa vaḻipāṭukaḷ iṉṟum mika manta pakkuvamuṭiya mākkaḷ nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṭṭēri, iruḷaṉ, kaṟuppaṉ mutaliya ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu varum muṟikaḷōṭu ottiruk kiṉṟaṉavē yallatu vēṟeṉṉi? iṉṉum āriyar cōmaracapāṉattāṉ vēḷvikaḷ vēṭṭataṉṟiyum veḷḷāṭukaḷ, cemmaṟiyāṭakaḷ, pacumāṭukaḷ, erumi māṭukaḷ, kutirikaḷ, maṉitar mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷik koṉṟuvēṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉip pucittu vantārkaḷ. maṉitaṉ maṉitaṉik koṉṟu pucikkuñ ceyki evvaḷavu koṭumiyāṉa ceyal! nakkavāram mutaliya tīvakaṅkaḷil vacikkum mikka anākarika cātiyārē iṉṟum maṉita ūṉ pucittu varukiṉṟaṉar. uyirkaḷil maṉitarkkup palavakiyālum payaṉpaṭutaluṭiya pacumāṭu, kutiri mutaliyavaṟṟic ciṟitum irakkamiṉṟik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉivirumpi vēṇṭiyamaṭṭum pucittu vantaṉarāyiṉ, paṇṭik kāla āriyacātiyār evvaḷavu koṭiyavarka ḷeṉṟum evvaḷavu aṟiyāmi yuṭiyavarkaḷeṉṟum eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu! ivvaḷavu koṭuñ ceyaluñ ceytu koṇṭu, ikkoṭuñceyaluk kutaviyākat tām pāṭi vittuk koṇṭa iḻinta vētappāṭṭukkiḷit tammiṉ vēṟāṉa tamiḻmakkaḷum oruppaṭṭut taḻuvic ciṟappikkumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttu varum avar ceyal niṉiyuṅkā lellāñ cālavum vañcaṉiyuṭitteṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ittakiya koṭuñ ceykiyuñ cuvarkkattit tarumeṉṟa avaratu pōlip poyyuriyi maṟuttaṟkaṉṟē nammutu kuravarāṉa teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
  uyircekut tuṇṇāmi naṉṟu.
eṉṟu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiṉār. cāṅkiyam eṉṉum ariya tattuvanūl vakutteḻutiya pēraṟivāḷarāṉa kapilarum kaṇakkillāp pirāṇikaḷik kolvittu avaṟṟiṉ irattatti āṟāyp perukac ceyvittaliṉ vētaṅkaḷ acuttamuṭiyaṉa.1 eṉṟu ōtiṉār. atu niṟka.
iṉi, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu irukkuvētam mutaliyaṉa cāmāṉiya putika vaḻipāṭṭil tali niṉṟaṉavāyiṉum, avvēta muṭipāṉa upaniṭataṅkaḷum cāṅkiyam vētānta cūttiram mutaliyaṉavumellām uyarnta tattuva nuṇporuḷ pōtikkak kāṇṭaliṉ āriyamakkaḷ piṟṟiñāṉṟu pēraṟivuṭiyarāyiṉā reṉṉāmō veṉiṉ;- eṉṉām. upaniṭata mutalāṉa ñāṉanūlkaḷ āriyamoḻiyil eḻutap paṭṭiruttalpaṟṟi avi āriyamakkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟal vaḻuvām.
paṇṭināḷil tamiḻmakkaḷ vaṭakkē imayamalic cāralvariyilum, mēṟkē āpkāṉitāṉam variyilum paraviyiruntaṉar. itaṟku aṟikuṟiyāka iṉṟum āpkāṉi tāṉam pelucitāṉam eṉṉum nāṭukaḷil tamiḻōṭu mikka toṭarpuṭiya pirāki eṉṉum moḻi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. imayamalic cāralil vāḻum kuṉṟavar kaḷellārum tamiḻiṉ ṟiripāṉa oru moḻi vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvē yaṉṟi vaṭanāṭṭiṟ paḻiya pala ūrkaḷellān tamiḻp peyarpeṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻūk eṉṉum ūr vaṭanāṭṭiliruttal yārumaṟinta toṉṟēyām. iññāṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭi liruppōriṟ perumpālār paṇṭikkālat tamiḻ marapiṉarēyāvar. moḻi kālavēṟupāṭṭāl vēṟu paṭiṉum makkaḷ cātip piṟappu yāṇṭum orutaṉmiyākavē yirukkum. muṉnāḷiṟ ṟamiḻar mikap pukaḻ peṟṟu vāḻntapōtu vaṭāciyāviliruntu cintu natip pakkamāy āriyamakkaḷ intiyāviṉuṭ puka, uḷḷirunta tamiḻariyaṟkiyilē viruntu varuvāri ōmpippōṟṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyarātalāl tam nāṭu tēṭip piḻikkavanta āriyamakkaḷi nalvaravāka ēṟṟu avarkku vēṇṭuvaṉa tantu ōmpiṉār. āriyar varuṅkālattut tamiḻar mikka nākarika vāḻvuṟṟiruntaṉa reṉpati irukkuvēta vurikoṇṭu muṉṉarē iṉitu viḷakkiṉām. iṉi, aṅṅaṉam pōnta āriya makkaḷōṭu tamiḻar peritum aḷavaḷāviṉārakaliṉ, avarkkuriya āriya moḻiyiṉiyun tāṅkaṟṟu ataṉiyum paṇpaṭuttuvantaṉar. āriyamakkaḷ ceyyum vēḷvi viḻākkaḷukkut tāmum poruḷutavi ceytu avaṟṟi naṭipeṟuvittu vantaṉarāyiṉum, avvēḷvi vēṭṭal ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāmi aṟivittu avarkkut tattuva nuṇporuḷkaḷiyun tamiḻar āṅkāṅku uṇarttu vārāyiṉar. putiyarāy vantōr tām vaḻakkamāka mēṟkoṇṭu ceyyum oru karumattic caṭutiyiṟ ceyyavēṇṭāmeṉṟu niṟuttiṉāl avarmika maṉam varuntuvarākaliṉ, avarkku mutalil utaviyāḷarāy niṉṟu avar vēṇṭiya vēḷvik kaṉmaṅkaḷiyum naṭāttivanta tamiḻar piṉ ciṟitu ciṟitē avarkkut tattuva nuṇporuḷaṟivu koḷutti vēḷvi vēṭṭal payaṉpaṭāmiyē yaṉṟit tīviṉiyuṭit tātaluṅ kāṭṭuvar. iṅṅaṉan tamiḻāciriyar kāṭṭiya aṟivumoḻikaḷē upaniṭataṅkaḷeṉṟu piṟṟiñāṉṟu peyar peṟalāyiṉa. upaniṭataṅkaḷellām oru mukamāy niṉṟu vēḷvivēṭṭa lākāteṉṟum, makkaḷellārun tattuvañāṉan talippaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. vētaṅkaḷiṟ colliya kaṉmaṅkaḷukku muṟṟum māṟupāṭāy niṉṟu avi tammip paḻittut tattuva ñāṉa mēmpāṭu pulappaṭukkum peṟṟiyaṉavām. avi vētāntameṉavum peyarpeṟukiṉṟaṉa. vētamum vētāntamum tammiṟ peritum muraṇi niṟpaṉavām. vētam kaṉmaṅkaḷi mikkeṭuttuk kūṟuvaṉa; vētāntam avaṟṟip paḻittu ñāṉatti mikkeṭuttuk kūṟuvaṉa; vētam āriya makkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; vētāntam tamiḻmakkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavām. vētamaṭṭum aṟinta āriyar vētāntamaṟinta tamiḻarpāl ataṉik kaṟṟaṟivā rāyiṉar. paṇṭik kālat tamiḻarkkuṭ pukaḻpeṟṟa vēntarā yirunta caṉakaṉ civali, acātacatturu mutaliya maṉṉar maṉṉar āriyarkku vētāntam uṇarttiya varalāṟu catapātapirāmaṇam, upaniṭatam mutaliya nūlkaḷāṟ ṟeṟṟeṉa viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻamaṉṉarum ammaṉṉar kuṭiyārum āriyarāl araciṉiyār eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭavāṟu muṉ iṉitu viḷakkip pōntām. appeṟṟiyarām rājanyar ‘innilattiṉiṉṟum makkaḷ eṅku eḻukiṉṟaṉar nī aṟiviyā?’ eṉṟu vēntaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ ataṟku avar ‘aiyā, nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār ‘tirumpa avar eṅku celkiṉṟār aṟiviyā?’ eṉa viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟkavar ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉa iṟuttār; tēvayāṉattiṟkum, pitiryāṉat tiṟkum eṅkē pētamuṇṭākiṉṟatu aṟiviyā?’ eṉa viṉaviṉāṉ; ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār.(2)
‘maṟumiyulakam ēṉ niṟikkap paṭuvatilli aṟiviyā?’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟku avar ‘aṟiyēṉ aiyā’ eṉṟār; ‘pañcamākutiyil tiravapporuḷkaḷ ēṉ puruṣacaptameytukiṉṟaṉa aṟiviyā?’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟkavar ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār.(3)

appollāta aracaṉ eṉṉi aintu viṉākkaḷ viṉāviṉāṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟkēṉum eṉṉāl viṭi yiṟukka muṭiyavilliyē eṉṟāṉ. atu kēṭṭa tanti ‘nī coṉṉa ak kēḷvikaḷ aintaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkēṉum eṉakkē viṭi teriyavilliyē; avi eṉakkut terintaṉavāyiṉ niṉakkēṉ yāṉ collāmaliruppēṉ? eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ(5).
kutamakōttirattārāṉa avar tanti avvaracaṉ araṇmaṉikkup pōyiṉār. avar varukiyik kaṇṭa aracaṉ viruntiṉarkkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭukaḷellām āṟṟiṉāṉ. maṟunāṭ kāliyil avar aracaṉ avik kaḷattē ceṉṟār. aracaṉ avari nōkki, ō kutamarē!  ulakattup poruḷkaḷiṟ ciṟantatetuveṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟīrō ataṉik kēḷum eṉṟāṉ. ataṟkavar ulakiyaṟporuḷkaḷ eṉakku vēṇṭām avi niṉpālē yirukkaṭṭum. ō vēntaṉē! nī eṉ putalvaṉik kēṭṭa aintu viṉākkaḷiyum eṉakku viḷakkic collutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār (6)
atukēṭṭaracaṉ vicaṉamuṭiyāṉay, eṉṉuṭaṉ cilanāḷ irukkakkaṭavīr eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. atu kaḻintapiṉ aracaṉ avari nōkki, nīr ivvāṟu vicāraṇi ceyyap pukuntamiyāṉum, itaṟku muṉ pirāmaṇaṉ evaṉum ivvirakaciyatti aṟiyāṉākalāṉum, ivvulakattiluḷḷa ellā makkaḷuḷḷum rājanyarkku māttiramē itaṉi upatēcikkum urimiyuṇṭākalāṉum yām itaṉi umakku aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟām eṉṟu upatēcikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭatu.
iṉṉum ivipōla upaniṭataṅkaḷil āṇṭāṇṭu varuvaṉavellām īṇṭuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ itu mikaviriyumeṉa vañci viṭukkiṉṟām. ivi tammāl tēṟappaṭum muṭipuri yāteṉiṉ, rājanyar allatu araciṉiyār eṉṟu āriyarāl aḻikkappapeṟṟa paṇṭikkālat tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē āriyap pirāmaṇarkkut tattuva ñāṉōpatēcañ ceytu vantaṉa reṉpatēyām eṉka. ākavē, upaniṭatam tamiḻāciriyarāl āriyamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa veṉṉumuṇmi teḷiporuḷāy niliniṟuttap paṭṭatu. vētānta cūttiram, cāṅkiyam, niyāyikam, vicēṭikam, mutaliyaṉavellām vakuttu nuṇporuḷ potuḷa eḻutiṉār tamiḻāciriyarēyā meṉpatūum, puttacamayam viḷakkiya kutamacākkiyarum tamiḻāciriyarēyā meṉpatūum mullippāṭṭārāycci, tirukkuṟaḷārāycciyil naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉām. āṇṭuk kāṇka. ivvāṟu tattuvañāṉaṅkaḷum avi tirunta viḷakkiya upaniṭatam civākama mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻarkkē uriyaṉavāyp piṉ āriyarāṟ ṟamiḻariṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭa āriya iravaṟporuṇūlkaḷāy nilippavum, iññāṉṟi āriyar avi tamakkē uriyaṉaveṉavum, avi tammit tamiḻar ōtappeṟāreṉavuṅ kūṟutal ciṟumakārāṉum eḷḷi nakiyāṭaṟpālatām. iññāṉṟi āriyar tamiḻar ceyta naṉṟiyic ciṟitum ōrātu tīṭṭiṉa marattiṟ kūrpārttal oppat tamakku aṟivukoḷuttiya paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ marapiṉarām nammaṉōrpāṟ ceytupōtarum paṭiṟṟoḻukkam mikapperitu! avar paṭiṟṟoḻukka viyalpu iṉit teṉṟaṟiyāta nammaṉōrum avarmayakkavuriyil viḻuntu avamēyākiṉṟār. iṉiyēṉum tam peruñciṟap puṇarntu ākkameytuvārāka.

piṉ iṇippu
1. uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam

in nilulakattil uyir uḷ poruḷkaḷum uyiril poruḷkaḷum ākiya iruvakiyum oruṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, uyiruḷ poruḷkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭumukattāl uyiril poruḷkaḷ ārāyntaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉavēyaṉṟi, ivi muṉṉiyavaṟṟiṉ toṭarpiṉṟi aṅṅaṉam aṟiyap paṭutalik kāṇkilēm. ākavē, uyirp poruḷkaḷip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa aṟivu peṟṟāl, avvaṟivu peṟuvārk kellām aḵtu aṟivum iṉpamum aḷittu, avaratu maṇṇulaka vāḻkkiyi viṇṇulaka vāḻkki yōṭoppac ceyyun tiṟattatām eṉpataṉi aṉivarum niṉiviṟ patittal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam iṉṟiyamiyātu ārāyntu aṟiyaṟ pālaṉavākiya uyirkaḷō eṇṇiṟanta kōṭiyaṉa; eṉṟālum, avitammiṟ pulaṉākum uṇarviṉ aṟiviṉ perumi ciṟumi kaṇṭu, avitammi āṟu tokutikaḷākap pakuttup, paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār aḷavu paṭutti yirukkiṉṟār. toṭṭāluṇarum uṇarccimaṭṭum uṭiya ōr aṟivuyirkaḷum, toṭutaluṭaṉ nāviṉāṟ cuvittuṇarum īr aṟivuyirkaḷum, toṭutal cuvittaluṭaṉ mōntuṇarum mūkkuṇarvum vāynta mūvaṟivuyirkaḷum, im mūṉṟaṉuṭaṉ kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭuṇaruṅ kaṇṇuṇarvum vāynta nālaṟivuyirkaḷum, innāṉkaṉuṭaṉ ceviyāṟ kēṭṭuṇaruñ ceviyuṇarvum vāynta aiyaṟivuyirkaḷum, iṉi ivvavim pulaṉkaḷāl aṟintavaṟṟi, nallatitu tīyatitu, iḵtu itaṉiyoppatu, iḵtu itaṉiṉ vēṟāvatu eṉappakuttuk kāṇum maṉavaṟivum oruṅkukeḻumiya āṟaṟivuyirkaḷum, eṉa āṟu perumpakuppil ellā uyirkaḷiyum aṭakki, avaṟṟiṟku ovvoru pakuppiliruntum orucila uyirkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉ aṟiviyakkattiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉaṟār āciriyar.1 ivvāṟāka uyirkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum, avvavaṟṟiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum aṟiviṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu kaṇṭu, āṟu vakiyil aṭakkikkāṭṭiya mikap paḻiya nūl tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa tolkāppiyattit tavira, vēṟentap paḻiya moḻiyilum entap paḻiya nūlum illāmi karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. itukoṇṭu, paḻiya tamiḻāciriyariṉ nuṭpa ārāycciyaṟiviṉ māṭci teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam pakukkappaṭṭa aṟuvaki uyirkaḷil, maṉavaṟivuṭaṉ āṟaṟivukūṭiya makkaḷē uyarnta piṟappiṉar. ataṉāl, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār makkaḷi uyartiṇi eṉṟum, makkaḷ allāta ēṉi aintaṟivukkuṭpaṭṭa uyirkaḷi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉār.2
iṉit, toṭutaluṇarcci oṉṟēyuṭiya pullum maraṉumē mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya uyirkaḷākum. innilavulakattiṉ mēṟparappellāṅ kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākap paṉippāṟikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ap paṉippāṟikaḷ pakalavaṉ veppattāl urukik kariyak kariya, nīrperukip paḷḷattākkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil nirampikkoṇṭu parampavē, āṟum ēriyum ciṟukaṭal peruṅkaṭa lkaḷumākiya nīrnilikaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa.
piṉṉar, in nīrnilikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ōraṟivuyir kaḷiṟ kaṭippaṭi yiṉavākiya nīrppūṇṭukaḷum, īraṟivuyir kaḷākiya natti, caṅku, ippi, kiḷiñcil mutaliyaṉavum, mūvaṟiviṉatākiya aṭṭiyum, nāṉ kaṟiviṉatākiya naṇṭum, aiyaṟiviṉavākiya mīṉkaḷum piṟ piṟkālaṅkaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟuva vāyiṉa.
nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya nirppūṇṭukaḷ kālañ cellac cella nilattiṉmēl ivarntu vaḷara, avaṟṟiyaṭuttu vāṉaḷāviya pariya pariya maraṅkaḷ tōṉṟalum, avi aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ pala āṅkāṅku immaṇmicip polivavāyiṉa. ittakiya kāṭukaḷiṟ pala pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukkuppiṉ maṭintu nilattiṉkaṭ putintu pōkavē, paṭṭuppōṉa avikaḷiṉ maraṅkaḷ pala nūṟṇṭukaḷukkup piṉ karikaḷāka urumāṟiṉa; aṅṅaṉam māṟiya karikaḷē, ippōtu nilatti akaḻnta curaṅkaṅkaḷiliruntum mikutiyāy eṭukkappaṭṭu, nīrāvi vaṇṭikaḷiyum, nīrāvik kappalkaḷiyum virivākac celuttutaṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, nilattiṉkaṇ iyaṅkum uyirkaḷil iruppaṉa, ūrvaṉa, naṭappaṉa, paṟappaṉa, nīntuvaṉavākiya ellā  uyirkaḷum mutaṉmutal nīriṉ kaṭṭōṉṟip piṟkālaṅ kaḷiṟṟām nilattiṉmēl ēṟivantu uyirvāḻappeṟṟaṉa. itaṟkuc cāṉṟāka nīrkkōḻiyiyum nīrāmiyiyum uṟṟup pārmiṉkaḷ! nīrkkōḻi nīruḷ amiḻnti vāḻavum vallatu, nilattiṉ mēl iyaṅkavum vallatu, vāṉiṟ paṟantu cellavum vallatu. maṟṟu, nīrāmi nīril nīnti uyirkāvaḻtaṟkut tuṇiceyyum ceṭṭikaḷ vāyntatāyiruttal pōlavē, nila āmi nilattiṉmēl naṭamāṭutaṟ kēṟṟa aṭikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya kālkaḷ vāyttiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatu. iṉṉum nilattiṉkaṇ ulavum nāy, kutiri, yāṉa, kuraṅku mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷō ṭoppa, nīrnāy, nīrkkutiri, nīryāṉi, nīrkkuraṅku, mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ kaṭaliṉkaṇ ulavā niṟṟalik, kaṇkāṭci niliyaṅkaḷil avitammiṟ cila koṇarntu vikkappaṭṭiruttalik kaṇṭu naṉkaṟiyalām.
iṉṉum, nilattiṉmēl uḷḷa uyirkaḷik kāṭṭilum ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku periya uruvam vāynta uyirkaḷ, makkaḷ tōṉṟāta annāḷiṟ kaṭal nīrilum maṇalveḷikaḷilum iyaṅkiṉa. makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉṉāḷilum avviraṇṭiluṅ kāṇappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.1 nilattiṉmēl uḷḷa tēḷi viṭa mika mikap periya uruviṉavākiya kaṭaṟṟēḷkaḷ oṉpataṭi nīḷam uḷḷaṉavāy annāḷil tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. aṅṅaṉamē, mikap pariyavāṉa cila vilaṅkukaḷ eṇpattu nāṉkaṭi nīḷamum, maṟṟuñ cila nūṟaṭi nīḷamum uḷḷaṉavāyiruntaṉa. iññāṉṟuḷḷa yāṉiyiviṭap pattup patiṉintumaṭaṅku periya uruviṉavāṉa pēriyāṉikaḷum aññāṉṟiruntaṉa. āṉālum, ivvilaṅkukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa makkaḷ tōṉṟu taṟku muṉṉamē māṇṭaṭoḻintaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, uyirmāṇṭu paṉippāṟikaḷiṉ ūṭē kiṭantu putintupōṉavi piṉnāḷil ap paṉippāṟikaḷ katiravaṉ veppattāl urukik karintuviṭavē, ettaṉiyō āyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ māṇṭu pōṉa avaṟṟiṉ muḻuvuṭampum puttap putiyaṉavāyk kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. paṉippāṟikaḷ illāta iṭaṅkaḷil māyntavikaḷō aṅkuḷḷa maṇ kaṟpāṟikaḷāy iṟukiviṭa avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē kiṭantu taci kaḻintu maṭkippōka, veṟum eṟpuk kūṭukaḷāy veṭṭi veḷiyē eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam, iruppaṉa, ūrvaṉa, naṭappaṉa, paṟappaṉa, nīntuvaṉavākiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ mutaṉmutal nīriṟ ṟōṉṟi iyaṅkip, piṉṉar avaṟṟiṟ pala nilattiṉ mēl ivarntu, varavarap palki vāḻalāyiṉāṟ pōlavē, makkaḷ yākki yiṉiyuṭiya uyirkaḷum mutalil nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟip, piṉṉar nilattiṉ mēl vantu uyirvāḻntu perukalā yiṉamiyum oru cila nikaḻccikaḷāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. oru kāl mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷāṟ celuttappaṭṭuc ceṉṟa oru marakkalam oru kaṭaṟpakutiyiṟ celluṅkāl, aṅkuḷḷa kaṭalnīriṉiṉṟum makkaḷ vaṭiviṉatākiya ōr āṇ uyir, am marakkalattiṉmēl ēṟivantu, ataṉ muṉ aṇiyattil niṟuttappaṭṭirunta oru peṇpāvi yuruviṉic ciṟitunēram uṟṟunōkkik koṇṭiruntu, akkappal mīkāmaṉum piṟarun taṉṉip piṭikka muyaṉṟapōtu, akkaṭalnīriṟ kutittu amiḻntippōṉa uṇmi varalāṟṟiṉi ematu `ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ eṉṉum nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēm. ittakiya nikaḻcciyiliruntu, kaṭalnīrilum makkaḷ vaṭiviṉavākiya uyirkaḷ iyaṅkutal aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? ivvāṟu ellā uyirkaḷum ivvūṉ uṭampiṟ ṟēṉṟutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta iṭamākiya kaṭalnīril mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiyē, paṉṉeṭuṅ kālaṅkaḷukkup piṟaku makkaḷum nīrvāḻkkiyi viṭṭu nilavāḻkkiyik kaṭippiṭikkalā yiṉareṉṟu uṇartal vēṇṭum.
aḵtuṇmiyēyāyiṉum, uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟi yamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum ellāk karuvikaḷum oruṅku vāykkappeṟṟa innilattiṉmici uyirkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟāmal, nīriṉkaṇ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟalāṉatu eṉṉiyeṉiṟ, kūṟutum: paṭippiṉ tuvakkattilēyē im maṇṇulakam uyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkkikku ēṟṟatāy iruntāl, puṉmutal makkaḷ īṟāṉa ellā uyirkaḷum im maṭpāṅkar miciyē tōṉṟiyirukkum. āṉāl, innilavulakiṉ iyalpō pallāyiraṅkōṭi āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ irunta nilivēṟu, iṅñāṉṟuḷḷa nili muḻuvatūum vēṟu.
veyyavaṉ maṇṭaṭilam oru perunerupput tiraḷiyāy aṉṟum iṉṟum viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu. itu niṉittaṟkum iyalāk kaṭuvirivuṭaṉ cuḻalā niṟkiyil innilamum, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, caṉi mutalāṉa kōḷkaḷum avveyyavaṉākiya tīppiḻampiṉiṉṟun teṟittuppōnta neruppup poṟikaḷēyākum. ciṟiya poruḷkaḷiyē aṟintu nukar taṟpāṉmiyavāṉa kaṇ mutalāṉa ciṟupoṟikaḷ maṭṭumē amiyappeṟṟa ciṟṟaṟiviṉarākiya nammaṉōr kaṇkaḷukku nām vāḻum immaṇṇulakam mikap periyatupōṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, uṇmiyāṉ ārāyntu kāṇpārkkuk katiravaṉ maṇṭilamē innilavulakattip pārkkilum munnūṟṟu muppatiṉāyira maṭaṅku periyatātal viḷaṅkumeṉṟu iññāṉṟi vāṉnūlāciriyar kaṇakkuc ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar.1 ataṉāl, immaṇṇulakaṅ katiravaṉ maṇṭilattiṉiṉṟun teṟittup pōnta oru ciṟu neruppup poṟiyēyallāl vēṟaṉṟātal tērntuṇaraṟpāṟṟu. aṅṅaṉam oru neruppuppoṟiyāy mutaṉ mutal uṇṭāṉa innilavulaku ettaṉiyō nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy vemmi kuṟintu āṟi iṟukivarukiṉṟatu. iṉṟuṅkūṭa itaṉ uṭpakuti aḷaviṭappaṭāta veppam vāynta tīkkuḻampu uṭiyatāyk kotittuk koṇṭiruk kiṉṟatu. innilattiṉ miyamāṉa iṭattiṉūṟu āṅkāṅkuḷḷa erimalikaḷiṉ puḻivaḻiyē ittīkkuḻampu orōvorukāṟ kotittup poṅki mēleḻuntu vaḻintuviṭutal uṇṭu; ivvāṟu vaḻitalāl erimalikaḷiṉ aṭivārattilum aṇmiyilum uḷḷa ūrkaḷum nakarkaḷum erintu cāmpalāyviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi 501 - ām āṇṭil vecuviyacu eṉṉum erimaliyiṉiṉṟum poṅki vaḻinta tīkkuḻampāl ataṉi aṭuttirunta periya nakaraṅkaḷ erintu cāmparāyiṉa; patiṉeṇṇāyiram makkaḷum maṭintupōyiṉar!
iṅṅaṉam, uyir vāḻkkikkup pēriṭar payappatāṉa tīvaṭiviṉatāy mutaṟkaṇ iruntamiyāl, innilavulakattiṉ mēṟparappiṟ palakōṭi āṇṭukaḷ variyiṟ puṟpūṇṭukaḷ mutal makkaḷ īṟāṉa ettakiya uyirun tōṉṟāvāyiṉa. piṟaku, paṉṉūrāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka innilavulakiṉ vemmi āṟi varavara, itaṉ mēṟparappu iṟuka, iṟukiya kaṟpāṟiyiṉ paḷḷattākkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil maḻinīr nirampi, ēri, ciṟukaṭal peruṅkaṭal mutalāṉa nīrnilikaḷit tōṟṟuvikkalāyiṟṟu.
aṅṅaṉam uṇṭāṉa nīrnilikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil nīrppūṇṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉpiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittup, perukkak kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟi nam kaṭpulaṉkaḷukkuc ciṟitum pulaṉākāta mika nuṇṇiya uyirkaḷun tōṉṟuva vāyiṉa. avaṟṟiṉpiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu naṅ kaṭpulaṉkaḷukkup pulaṉākum īraṟivuyirkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ mūvaṟivu, nālaṟivu aiyaṟivuṭiya uyirkaḷum, iṟutiyāka āṟaṟivuyirkaḷākiya makkaḷun tōṉṟalāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya uyirkaḷiṟ pala nilattiṉmēl ivarntu vantu uyirvāḻtaṟku ēṟṟapaṭiyāka, nīr nilikaḷi aṭutta nilappakutikaḷiṟ ciṟiyavum periyavumāṉa puṟpūṇṭukaḷum, marañceṭi koṭikaḷum uṇṭāki iliyum pūvaṅ, kāyuṅ kaṉiyuṅ, kiḻaṅkukaḷum vittukkaḷum ākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷi mikutiyāka aḷittaṉa. uṇavup poruḷkaḷ mikuntu varavē, avaṟṟi ayilum uyirkaḷum oṉṟu, pattu, nūṟu, āyiram, patiṉāyiram, nūṟāyiramāyp palkalāyiṉa.
ivvāṟāka, nilattiṉ mēṟparappil nīrttōṟm, kaṟpoṭittōṟṟam, puṟpūṇṭuttōṟṟam, ciṟṟuyirttōṟṟam mutalāṉa nikaḻccikaḷellām paṭippaṭiyē yuṇṭākip, palakōṭi āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟa piṉṉarētāṉ makkaḷuyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam uḷatāyiṟṟu. vilaṅkiṉ yākkiyil niṉṟa uyir palakōṭi yāṇṭukaḷāka avviyākkikaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭuk kaṭippaṭiyāka makkaḷ yākkiyil varalāṉatu iṟṟikku ainnūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyaṉṟi, ataṟku muṉ makkaḷuṭampuṭiya ettakiya uyirumē illiyeṉṟu ittuṟiyiṟ pēruḻippeṭuttup peritārāynta nila nūl uyir nūlvalla mēlnāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ muṭivukaṭṭi yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ākavē, im maṇmicit tōṉṟiya uyirttokikaḷiṟ, kaṭimuṟiyākat tōṉṟiyavi makkaḷuṭampu vāynta āṟaṟivuyir kaḷēyeṉṟum, ivi tōṉṟiya kālam ainnūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷukkumēṟ ceṉṟatākāteṉṟum niṉiviṟ patittal vēṇṭum.
2. makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya iṭam
iṉi, innilavulakiṉ entap pakutiyil mutaṉ mutal ellā uyirkaḷum vāḻat tuvaṅkiṉa eṉpatu ārāyaṟpāṟṟu. nīriviṭṭu nilattiṉmēṟ pōnta piṉ uyirkaḷ paciyāl vāṭi maṭiyāmal nilippaṭutaṟku vēṇṭiyavi uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷ allavō? ākavē, uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaṉmutal enta iṭattil mikutiyāyt tōṉṟik kiḷarntaṉavō, anta iṭamē uyirkaḷ naṉku uyirvāḻntu palkutaṟku ēṟṟa peruvaḷaṉ uṭiyatātal vēṇṭumeṉpati evarumē aṟivar. innilavulakiṉ vaṭamuṉip pāṅkarum, itaṉ teṉmuṉip pāṅkarum aṉṟum iṉṟum paṉippāṟikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, aṅkē puṟpūṇṭukaḷum marañceṭi koṭikaḷum uṇṭāvatilli. avi yilliyākavē uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyā uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷum appakutikaḷiṟ kiṭippatilli. ākavē, vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉikaḷi neruṅkiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟi vāḻntiruttal iyalātu.
piṉṉar, av vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉikaḷukku mika vilaki, innilattiṟku naṭuvaṇatāṉa kumari nāṭē, pakal irāppoḻutukaḷum veppataṭpaṅkaḷum otta niliyil iyaṟkiyē poruntappeṟṟu, marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarntu vaḷarntu, nel, kōtumi, palvakip payaṟukaḷ, tīñcuvik kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutalāṉa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, ītoṉṟē makkaḷum piṟa uyirkaḷum mutaṉmutaṟṟōṉṟi uyirvāḻntu, palkip palamukamāyp paravutaṟku iṭañceytateṉpatu āḻnta ārāycciyāl naṉku viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu.
ivviyalpiṉatāṉa kumarināṭē makkaṭṭōṟṟattiṟku mutaṟṟāyakamāy irunta uṇmiyiṉi mēṉāṭṭā ciriyarkaḷ palamukamāyp parakka ārāyantu muṭipu kaṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	naṉimikap paḻaṅkālattu makkaḷ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟi vāḻnta iṭam teṉṟamiḻk kumarināṭē eṉa valiyuṟuttappaṭuvatāl tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻ makkaḷum, tamiḻar nākarikamumē muṉṉuḷḷaṉa eṉpataṉi valiyuṟutti innūli viritteḻuta aṭikaḷār toṭaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl ataṉip piṉ iṇippāka īṇṭuc cērttuḷḷām.

paṇṭikkālat tamiḻarum āriyarum
- muṟṟum -


Preface to the Second Edition
This Tamil work on ‘Caste and Its Evil’ first appeared in the form of a somewhat long essay in the sixth volume of my Tamil magazine Jnanasagaram in October, 1911. The occasion which called it into existence was in this wise. In response to the invitation of the Saiva Siddhanta Sabha at Tuticorin, I had to go there in December, 1910, in order to preside at its grand annual gathering and so to conduct it as to make it meet the needs of the time. I took an active part in its proceedings, as it was ably managed by learned, intelligent and well-meaning Saivites and was also attended by a large number of men and women of enlightenment, earnestness and understanding. Everything went on smoothly and delightfully on the first day, except in one important respect. Able lecturers filled with enthusiasm for introducing reforms into religious and social matters, came from distant parts of the country, but some of them were not treated with that respect which learning, good intentions and good manners clim, but were treated, as appeared to my mind, rather badly, simply becuse they happened to belong to non-vegetarian castes. Whether learned or unlearned, good or bad, religious or irreligious only those who were known to be Saivites, that is, those only whose lineage was known to have originated with the vegetarian velala parents, were invited to sit in one hall and dine together, while others, whose adherence to Saiva religion and clean vegetarian mode of living could not be questioned but whose only fault was what was occasioned by the mere accident of birth, were made to sit aloof in a separate place and served meals rather lately. This I observed on the first day and, on the next, I questioned some of the prominent members of the society why they were treated thus. One amongst them replied me that they belonged to non-vegtetarin castes, and, therefore, could not be admitted into the dining - hall of the Saivites. But I sid that though they were no born vegetarians, they too were Saivites in so far as they followed the principles of Saiva religion and adopted the vegetarian mode of living, and that, therefore, they ought to be encouraged, so that other like them might also come up in the hope of mingling with a higher class people and lead a purer and a truer form of religious life. I added that, if their object of conducting such religious meetings were sincere, they should spread the beneficial, teachings of the Saiva religion not in brotherly love and affection towards each other as all are the children of a single Heavenly Father Lord Siva and by prompting those of them who are morally and intellectually stronger to uplift the weaker and make them their own, and that such merciful, tender and benevolent kind of service by drawing together the choice people from every class and community, would not only tend to increase the strength of the Saivite community but would also heighten the value of their service to humanity at large. To this the man curtly replied that it was not the custom to do so. Agin, I argued with him at some length pointing out to him that what he called ‘the custom’ was not really so but the reverse of it was the true one as is evident in the lives of the Saints and Sages who founded and spread the Saiva religion. But unfortunately the man was not amenable to reason, since his false and arrongant notion of the superiority of his caste blinded his whole mental vision. Thereupon I made up my mind to dine rather with the excluded party than with such self - conceited Saivites. And accordingly I did dine with the forbidden brethren. But so much did this action of mine enrage the Saivite brethren that, on the next day, they forbade me entering their dining hall during meal-times. This put me on my mettle and I told them point blank that, as the way to my room lay through their dining - hall, I could not go but through it as usual; and sid also that I should not take the meals cooked by their men under their supervision, since i thought the meals prepared in the midst of such inhuman people get themselves contaminated. On that day, the sitting of the Conference was to commence at 3 p.m. with me in the chir; it was nearly 2 p.m., still I had not touched my meals; having seen me persevering in my determination, all the Saivite members, except one or two, had the kindness to come to me and apologize for what the had done and expressed their willingness to treat henceforth the lecturers and other with equality and without minding any caste - distinctions. Of course, there were one or two dissenting elements but these were eliminated from our group. We then sat together, prayed to God for blessing us with that bond of unity, and partook of our meals with one joyous heart. On that third sitting day of the conference, the lectures had been very illuminating and the proceedings were brought to a successful close by my concluding speech which impressed on the minds of the audience the urgent necessity of cutting at the root the evils of caste - distinctions.
Nevertheless, a few mischievous elements with whom caste was everything, while virtue, learning, intelligence. piety, religiousness and such other great qualities count for nothing, could not be silenced either by reasoning or by any regard to social unity, but they bestirred themselves most actively and attacked me in a magazine - article which, with much abusive matter, asserted the superiority of their caste on false and most erroneous grounds. I might have treated it with indifference, for it contined no argument worth contending for, but for the harmful influence it was likely to have on the minds of the unenlightened. I had, therefore, to take up the cudgels on behalf of the down - trodden and in the interest also of the true Saiva community whose progress consisted not in its exclusiveness but in absorbing into it and assimilating with itself persons of higher qualities turned out from lower orders and having an eager expectation to be lifted up from above; and the result was the  first edition of this treatise.
The first edition was merely a booklet of 22 pages, which dealt chiefly with the practical aspect of the caste question, while its theoretical part it touched but rather too briefly. How the barriers rised between one caste and another are mere fictitious ones existing only in the imagination of certin class of people in whom it was bred by their own arrogance and ignorance, how a mixture of castes results as a product of natural human needs and takes place both openly and covertly from the very beginning of the human family, and how easily a fusion of the good and cultured people picked up from every caste and community can be effected for the immense benefit of all, were clearly shown in it. That the tenets of Saiva religion do not stand aginst but lend a free, unstinted, undisguised support to such a whole-some fusion of the select, was also shown by quotations taken direct from the writings of the founders and exponents of tha religion. How useful the booklet proved to those who looked forward with eagerness to such a reform of castes, thought they themselves had not the courage to express the need of it might be seen from the rapid sale the thousan copies of it had in a few months.
Inevitably the book had been long out of print, though demands for it increased day after day. The elaborte research - work unided I had to do, in order to redeem the literary and religious history of the land from the thick gloom of myth and uncertinty created and cast over it by selfish and selfinterested persons, the constantanduremitting study I pursued of works on ‘New psychology’, stimulated by a strong desire to introduce its subjects into Tamil, left me little leisure to devote to the preparation of its second edition earlier. But, now that I was enabled to undertake the work, years of study and research - work facilitating it for me, I set myself to enlarging the briefly - touched - subject on ‘The origin and growth of the institution of caste,’ since I thought that, for healing its present corruption and infusing new life into it, only a thorough knowledge of its past history could afford a proper and effective remedy. And accordingly the subject has been amplified and treated fully and extensively in the first five chapters of this edition.
In the first chapter, it is shown that the present day castes cannot be identified with those of the past since the callings of the former have undergone so radical a change as is opposed to the ordinances lid down in the Sanscrit Vedas and Dharma Sastras. Modern castes have become hardened into unworkable masses by losing completely their pliancy of meaning and original good quality. When the composition of the first nine mandalas of the Rig Veda took place, the four castes were quite unknown to the Aryans. But, by the time when the tenth was added, the institution of four castes had become almost an established fact. Still it should be borne in mind that this division of the Indian people into four castes was based merely upon division of labour and was introduces as a convenient theoretic classification intended for the purposes of social and economical sciences. Practically there was no any hard and fast rule that one caste shouldnot adopt the profession of another nor should the one mix with another. Many Dravidian kings, merchants and even slaves took part in the composition of the Vedic hymns and followed professions that suited best their taste and capacity.
In the second chapter, the same subject is continued and discussed how, even at the time of the twelve oldest upanishads and the two epics the Mahabharata and the Ramayana when attempts were made by brahmins to make castes hereditary and exalt themselves at the expense of others, only merit and not birth was regarded as constituting a high caste.
In the third chapter are shown how, at the time when a series of Sanscrit works called Dharma Sastras came to be written, the hereditary clims of castes were just beginning to tighten their hold on the people, how the brahmins were attempting to demand exclusive privileges for themselves and devise the cruellest and the most oppressive measures for the labouring classes, how, even then, importance was attached not to birth but to merit, and how the brahmins and others who failed, in the smallest degree, to perform the prescribed duties and functions of their caste lost their caste and became low-born. Here, for the first time, the mischief is pointed out of including the Tamilians in the Sudra or slave caste. The institution of caste-system originated not with the Aryans as is erroneously supposed by oriental scholars, but with the statesmen of the ancient Tamilians themselves who had attined to a high degree of civilisation long before the Aryans entered India.
In the fourth chapter, a critical inquiry of the ‘Tolkappiam’, a very ancient Tamil work which is older then even some portions of the Rig-Veda, is taken up - especially of its sections dealing with the social classifications of the Tamil people. How the Tamil people reached the agricultural stage many centuries before when the Aryans were still in the pastoral stage leading the life of nomads and how the life of an agricultural people necessitated the classification of its men and women into different classes according to their occupations, are clearly proved by the aid of its study. Though this division of the Tamil people was rendered necessary by the conditions of their civilized life, there was no prohibition in any Tamil classics of old of any caste - intermixture. On the other hand there are clear references in them to free intermingling of castes.
In ther fifth chapter in which the later day Saiva and Vaishnava religious literatures are taken up for solving this intricate and difficult problem of caste, are given numerous instances and clear quotations from the lives and writings of the Saints of the two great religious, in proof that it was only Love towards God and his servants and not caste was regarded and respected in them.
In the sixth chapter the whole of the first edition which dealt with the practical aspect of the castequestion is included with necessary alterations.
In  the seventh and th concluding chapter, easy and practical means for wiping away caste-distinctions and consolidating the refined elements coming above from every grade of society, are set forth.
Another prominent feature of this edition is its pure Tamils style entirely free from Sanscrit words.
In fine it is my great pleasure to express my thanks and heart-felt gratitude to my esteemed friend Mr. C. Ariya Nayagam, Proctor, at Matale (Ceylon), for the extreme kindness with which he promptly sent me a charitable contribution of Rs 250 towards the printing expenses of the book. Words are inadequate to express sufficiently my deep debt of gratitude to this generous-hearted gentleman for similar contributions which he was making frequently for the past ten years and more towards the printing expenses of my writings. But for his munificent help, I would have been much embarassed in carrying out my literary and religious work successfully. By the grace of God may he live long in all glory!

The Sacred Order of Love	VEDACHALAM
Pallavaram,
1st June, 1926.

tirunāvukkaracu cuvāmikaḷ
iyal - 1
paḻiya vētattil cāti illi
civacamayamāṉatu aruḷ oḻukkattiyum, civapirāṉ māṭṭum avaṉ aṭiyār māṭṭum ēṉiyellā uyirkaḷ māṭṭum aṉpupūṇṭoḻukum oḻukkattiyum aṟivuṟut tuvateṉṟu uṇarum nalviṉi illāta pōlic civar cilar cātivēṟṟumiyē tām civacamayattāṟ peṟum meyyuṇarvākum eṉṟu mayaṅki uṇarntu, am mayakka vuṇarcciyit tammiṭattum tammō ṭottavariṭattum vittu makiḻvatōṭu amitipeṟāmal, ataṉit tamakku icinta pattirikikaḷiṉum eḻuti veḷiyiṭuvittuc, civa vuṇmikaḷi uḷḷavāṟē eṭuttu viḷakkivarum tava voḻukkamuṭiya periyāriyum tamakkuḷḷa poṟāmi yālum iḻintataṉmiyālum tamakkut tōṉṟiyavā ṟellām ikaḻntu pēcutaluñ ceykiṉṟār. oruvaruṭiya uyarvun tāḻvum aṟivāṉ mikka cāṉṟōr matikkaṟ pālarē yallāmal, aṟivillāk kuṟumpar matikkaṟpāla rallar; ākavē, aṟivillār kūṟum ikaḻurikaḷiyum poym moḻikaḷiyum aṟivuṭiyār oru poruṭṭāka eṇṇārākaliṉ avar kūṟum paḻic coṟkaḷi viṭuttu, avar kūṟum maṟṟap pakutikaḷil uḷḷa kuṟṟaṅkaḷi maṭṭum oru ciṟitu eṭuttukkāṭṭi maṟuttal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu ārāycci yuṇarvillātār mayaṅkāmip poruṭṭēyām.
cātivēṟṟumi paḻiyakālantoṭṭē vētam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatākaliṉ, annūlkaḷi oppukkoṇṭavarkaḷ avvēṟṟumiyiṉik kippaṟṟi yoḻukutalē ceyaṟpāla reṉpatu orucārār koḷki.
cātivēṟṟumi paḻamiyāka uḷḷateṉṟē koṇ ṭālum, ataṉit taḻuviyoḻukal vēṇṭumeṉpatu pakuttaṟivillār kūṟṟām. paḻiyaṉavellām nallaṉa vātalum illi, putiyaṉavellām tīyaṉavātalum illi; paḻiyaṉavaṟṟil tīyaṉavum uṇṭu putiyaṉavaṟṟil nallaṉavum uṇṭu. ivvuṇmiyic civacittānta āciriyaruḷ oruvarāṉa umāpati civācāriya aṭikaḷ toṉmiyavām eṉum eviyum naṉṟākā iṉṟu tōṉṟiyanūl eṉum eviyum tītākā eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttaruḷiṉamiyuṅ kāṇka.
iñ ñāṉṟuḷḷa palvaki makkaṭpiriviṉarum tattamakkup paṇṭutoṭṭē uriya kaṭamikaḷilāvatu piṟaḻāmal vāḻkiṉṟaṉarō veṉṟāl atuvumilli. pārppaṉariṟ perumpālār tamakku uriya nūl ōtutal, vēḷvivēṭṭal, piṟarkoṭukkum poruḷi yēṟṟu vayiṟuvaḷarttal mutalāṉa toḻilkaḷi aṟavē kiviṭṭu āṅkila nūlkaḷik kaṟṟut, tammāl milēccarākak karutappaṭṭa veḷḷikkārariṉ kīḻ aluvalkaḷil amarntu, avarkaḷ ēviya paṟpala toḻilkaḷiyum ceytuvarukiṟārkaḷ allarō? iṅṅaṉamākat tamakkuriya toḻilkaḷik kiviṭṭu, aracariṉkīḻ aluval pārppōr pārppaṉar ākamāṭṭār eṉṟu maṉumiruti (3, 4) vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟiyum, aracariṉkīḻ ūḻiyam ceyvatē ippōtu avarkaṭku iyaṟkiyāy viṭṭataṉṟō? kaṭalkaṭantu vēṟṟunāṭu kaḷukkup pōtal pārppaṉar kaḷukku vilakkappaṭṭiruntum (maṉu, 3, 158), avarkaḷiṟ palar kaṭalkaṭantu ceṉṟu āṅkilaṅ kaṟṟum, poruḷ tēṭiyun tirumpivantu tammiṉattavarōṭu uṟavukalantu pārppaṉariṟ ciṟantōrākak karutappaṭutali nām kāṇkiṉṟaṉam allamō? uṇavu ciṟṟuṇṭiviṟṟalālum, icipāṭu talālum, cōtiṭañ collutalālum, campaḷam peṟṟuk koṇṭu piṟarkkuk kalvi kaṟpittalālum uyir piḻip pōr pārppaṉar ākamāṭṭār, avar mika iḻintōrē yāvar eṉṟu maṉamiruti (3, 152, 155, 156, 162) naṉku valiyuṟuttic colliyum, ippōtu ittakiya toḻil kaḷic ceytu vayiṟu vaḷarttalē avarkaṭku iyaṟkiyāy vantu viṭṭataṉṟō? iṉṉum āviṉ (pacumāṭṭiṉ) iṟicciyit tiṉṟu uyirvāḻtal paṇṭikkālattirunta pārppaṉarkku vaḻakka māyirunta teṉṟu `tittirīyacaṅkiti, 7, 1, 4 viḷakkamākac colliyum, ataṟku oppa iṉṟu kāṟum vaṭanāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa pārppaṉarkaḷ mīṉ maṟi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ūṉi veḷippaṭiyākattiṉṟu uyir vāḻāniṟpavum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiya pārppaṉar maṭṭum iṅkuḷḷa civa vēḷāḷarkaḷiṉ pulāl uṇṇā aruḷoḻukkattik kaṇṭu tāmum tamatu paḻiya pulittoḻilik kiviṭṭup, pulāl uṇṇāp putiya naṭiyik kikkoṇṭu oḻukutal ellārum aṟinta toṉṟaṉṟō? ivvāṟu pārppaṉarkaḷ tamakku uriya paḻiya vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷik kiviṭṭu, ikkālattukku ēṟṟa vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum naṭiyuṭi cāyalkaḷiyum kippaṟṟi uyir vāḻtal pōlavē, maṟṟi vakuppiṉarun tattamakkuriya paḻiya toḻilkaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyuṅ ki viṭṭup putiya toḻil kaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum kippaṟṟi naṭantu varukiṟārkaḷ. uḻutuṇṇum vēḷāḷariṟ palar avvuḻutoḻiliviṭuttup, paṟpala kittoḻilkaḷiṟ pukuntirukkiṟārkaḷ. uḻuvit tuṇṇum vēḷāḷariṟ palar tēvāra tiruvācakam ōtal, tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kaṟṟal kaṟpittal, civapirāṉukku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟal, tirukkōyiṟṟoṇṭu purital, aracariṉ kīḻ amiccarāyum piṟarāyum aluval pārttal, ayalnāṭukaḷilum tam nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuviṟṟal, kaṟṟōrkkum iravalarkkum vēṇṭiya utavikaḷic ceytal, tamiḻiyum civattiyum vaḷarttal mutalāṉa tamakkuriya viḻumiya ceyalkaḷik kinnekiḻaviṭṭuk, kalviyaṟivum civanēyamum ilarāy, aviyillāmiyāl aracarkkum ulakiṉarkkum payaṉ paṭārāyt, tam vayiṟu vaḷarttaṟku ettuṇi iḻitoḻi lāyiṉum ataṉip puritaṟkup piṉṉiṭi yārāyt, tammi yōmputalilum tammi vīṇē uyartti iṟumāttaliluṅ karuttu mikuntu piṟaratu naṉmiyip poruṭpaṭuttāmaṟ, kālam vāyttapōtu piṟar uṭimikaḷik kavarntu avarkkut tīmiceyvatilum, aracamaṉṟaṅkaḷil aḻivaḻakkāṭu vatilum muṉittu niṟkiṉṟaṉarallarō? eṉṟālum, ivarkaḷuṭ cilar ikkālattiṟku vēṇṭum payaṉuḷḷa muṟikaḷiyum oḻukalāṟukaḷiyun taḻuvi, āṅkilattōṭu tamiḻkaṟṟum, āṅkāṅku araciyaṟ ṟoḻili mēṟkoṇṭum, cāti vēṟṟumikaḷi oḻittum, tiri kaṭalōṭic celvattiṉit tokuttum varukiṉṟaṉa reṉpatūum maṟukkaṟpālataṉṟu. āyiṉum, ivvuḻuvit tuṇṇum vēḷāḷar tamakkuriya paṇṭi naṟperuṅ kaṭami kaḷi muṟṟuñ celuttukiṉṟilar; avaṟṟiṟ perumpālum vaḻuviyē niṟkiṉṟaṉar. mēlum, innāṭṭavar kaḷuḷ āṇpālār tamakku vaḻakkamāyuḷḷapaṭiyē uṭiyuṭuttātu, mēlnāṭṭavarippōṟ kāṟcaṭṭi mēṟcaṭṭi yiṭṭum, taliyiṟ kullā toppi kavittum, aṭikaḷukkuc cappāttu māṭṭiyum palavakiyāl tamatu paḻiya muṟiyi māṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉarallarō? peṇpālār tam kātukaḷit toḷittu avaṟṟi mūkkuccaḷipōl toṅkavaḷarttu avaṟṟiṟ cumi mikunta poṉṉurukkaḷi yiṭum paḻiya aḻakaṟṟa muṟiyi māṟṟi, avaṟṟiṟ ciṟutuḷiyiṭṭuc civappu viram mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ camitta aḻakiya tōṭukaḷi aḻakuṟap porutti eḻilmikuntu ulavakkāṇkiṉṟaṉam allamō? peṇpālār tamatu mārpi mūṭāmal tiṟappāka viṭutalum, mārpiṉmēl tuṇiyiṭuvārkūṭa iravikki yiṭātu ulavutalumē naṉmuṟiyeṉṟu collivantavarka ḷellām, pārkka aruvaruppāṉa anta muṟiyi yoḻittu mārpip puṭiviyāl naṉku maṟittum iravikki yaṇintum varak kāṇkiṉṟōmē. iṉṉum, pattāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kāliyiṟ paḻiya cōṟum mōrum uṇṭu naṉku vaḷarntavar kaḷellām, ippōtu viṭiyum nērattilēyē kāppit taṇṇīrum  uroṭṭiyum uṇṇak kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉarē. iṅṅaṉa mellām namatu nāṭṭavar vāḻkkiyiṟ putitu pukuntirukkum māṟutalkaḷiyellām eṭutturikkap pukuntāl, ivvēṭu iṭaṅkoḷḷātu. immāṟutalkaḷil nallaṉavum uṇṭu, tīyaṉavum uṇṭu, iviyellām paḻiyakālattil iruntavikaḷ alla; putitu vantavikaḷē.
ivvāṟu makkaḷuṭiya nākarika vaḷarccikkum aṟiviṉ mēṉmikkum ēṟpak kālantōṟum paḻiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ aḻintu pōtalum putiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ pukuntu poruntu talum iyaṟkiyām. itu paṟṟiyaṉṟō paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum, vaḻuvalakāla vakiyiṉāṉē eṉṉum āṉṟōr moḻiyum eḻuntatu.
ākavē, cāti vēṟṟumi paḻiyakālantoṭṭu iruttal paṟṟiyē ataṉi viṭātu kippaṟṟi yoḻukal vēṇṭu meṉpatu ciṟitum poruttamillāta pōli yuriyām.
iṉic cāti vēṟṟumi paḻamitoṭṭē uḷḷa teṉpatum ārāycci aṟivillār kūṟṟām. mikap paḻiya nūlākiya irukkum vētattiṉ mutal oṉpatu maṇṭiṅ kaḷiṉum cāti vēṟṟumiyip paṟṟiya kuṟippu oṉṟumē kāṇappaṭavilli. irukkuvētam mutal oṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉum `pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉuñ col `vēḷvi vēṭkum muṉivaṉiyum, `patikaṅkaḷ pāṭum pulavaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatē yallāmaṟ, piṟkālattil vaḻakkum pārppaṉa vakuppiṉaṉik kuṟikkavilli. ēṉi mūṉṟu vakuppiṉariṉ peyarkaḷāṉa kṣattiriyaṉ, viciyaṉ, cūttiraṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷuṅkūṭa avvoṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉuṅ kāṇap paṭavilli. itaṉāl, avvoṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷum eḻutappaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ ellārum orē vakuppiṉa rāyiruntaṉarē yallāmal, nālvēṟu vakuppiṉarāyp pirintilareṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? makkaḷ mutaṉ mutal malikaḷiluṅ kaṭalōraṅkaḷilum uyirvāḻntaṉa reṉṟum, akkālaṅkaḷil avarkaṭku vīṭukaḷ illi yeṉṟum, attakiya kiruta ūḻiyiṟ cātivēṟṟumi ciṟitumē illi yeṉṟum vāyu purāṇamum (8, 53, 62) pukalā niṟkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa paṇṭikkālattē makkaḷuḷ ētoru vēṟṟumiyum, uyarvu tāḻvukāṭṭum ētorupirivum iruntatilli yeṉpatum pulaṉām.
iṉi, avvirukkuvētattiṉ mutal oṉpatu maṇṭilaṅ kaḷukkuppiṉ, pattām maṇṭilam eḻutappaṭṭa kālattilē tāṉ mutaṉ mutal nālvaki makkaṭpirivu tōṉṟuva tāyiṟṟu. aṅṅaṉan tōṉṟiyaviṭattum, makkaḷuḷ iv vakuppiṉar uyarntōr, iv vakuppiṉar tāḻntōr eṉṉum vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟilli. uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum nālvakit toḻil kaḷic ceyvōr nālvēṟu vakuppiṉarākac collap paṭṭaṉa rāyiṉum, annālvakuppiṉarum ellāmvalla iṟivaṉukkup putalvarkaḷē yāvar eṉpatuṅ kūṭavēvittut teḷit turikkappaṭṭatu. yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: irukku vētattiṉ iṟutikkaṇṇatākiya pattām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa puruṭacūtta mantiramāṉatu (12), pirāmaṇaṉ avaṉatu vāy āyiṉaṉ; rājanyaṉ avaṉuṭiya tōḷkaḷ āyiṉaṉ; viciyaṉ eṉappaṭṭōṉ avaṉuṭiya toṭikaḷ āyiṉāṉ; cūttiraṉ avaṉuṭiya aṭikaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṟu ōtumāṟṟāl, nālvakippaṭuttuc collappaṭṭa makkaḷ aṉivarum ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉa reṉṉum uṇmi iṉitu pulappaṭāniṟkum. aṟṟēṟ, pārppaṉaṉ iṟivaṉatu mēluṟuppākiya vāyiliruntum, maṟṟiyōr maṟṟik kīḻ uṟuppukaḷākiya tōḷ toṭi aṭi eṉpavaṟṟiliruntum tōṉṟiṉar eṉa ammantiram urittatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ; an nālvaki vakuppiṉariṉ toḻil vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṟivittaṟku aḵtu aṅṅaṉam uruvakavakiyāṟ kūṟiṉatē yaṉṟi, avarkaḷuḷ uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpittaṟku aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṟṟilli. nūl ōtutal vāyiṉtoḻil ātalāl, ōtutaṟṟoḻiliyē tamakkuc ciṟappākakkoṇṭa pārppaṉar iṟivaṉatu vāyiṉiṉṟum piṟantavarākac collappaṭṭār; vilaṅku kaḷālum pakivarkaḷāluṅ kuṭimakkaḷukkut tīṅkunērāmal villuṅ kaṇiyum piṭittu vēṭṭamāṭutalum pōrpuritaluṅ tōḷkaḷiṉ toḻil kaḷātalāl at toḻilkaḷic ceyyum rājanyar iṟivaṉuṭiya tōḷkaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantavarākac collap paṭṭār; ōriṭattuṇṭākiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi vilikoṇṭu piṟitōriṭattiṟ cērppittum, piṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭākiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi ōriṭattiṟ koṇarntu tokup pittum viṟṟu makkaḷ ellārum vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi avarkaḷ eḷitiṟpeṟat tiraṭṭittantu peritu payaṉpaṭuṅ koṇṭuviṟṟaṟ ṟoḻilic ceytal kālkaḷiṉāl ākun toḻilākaliṉ ataṉip puriyum viciyar iṟivaṉuṭiya toṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantavarākac collappaṭṭār; iṉi immūvakit toḻilpurivārum ēviya piṟatoḻil kaḷi virintuceṉṟu muṭittu avarkku mikavum payaṉpaṭuṅ kuṟṟēvaṟṟoḻilic ceytal perumpāluṅ kālaṭi kaḷiṉāl ākaṟpālatākaliṉ ataṉic ceyyum cūttirar iṟivaṉuṭiya tiruvaṭikaḷi ṉiṉṟum piṟantavarākac collappaṭṭaṉar. ākavē, ippakuppukaḷ toḻilpaṟṟi vantaṉavēyallāmal vēṟalla veṉpatu collāmē iṉitu viḷaṅkum. aṟṟaṉṟu, iṟivaṉatuvāy mēluṟuppum, avaṉṟaṉ aṭi kīḻuṟuppum, ēṉit tōḷun toṭiyum iṭippaṭṭa uṟuppukkaḷu mākaliṉ, ammuṟiyē mēluṟuppukkaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟiya pirāmaṇar kṣattiriyar viciyar eṉṉum mūvakuppiṉarum mēlōreṉavum, kīḻ uṟuppiṟṟōṉṟiya cūttirar kīḻōreṉavuṅ koḷḷutalē poruttamuṭitteṉiṉ; ikkūṟṟum pakuttuṇarcci iṉmiyāl nikaḻvatoṉṟām; eṉṉi? evvakip paṭṭa uyirum uṭampōṭu kūṭip piṟattal eṉpatu, tanti tāyimaruva veḷippaṭum pāṟṟuḷiyāṉatu tāyiṉ vayiṟṟuḷḷē karuppiyiṟpukuntu taṅkik karuvāyc camintu ki kāl mutalāṉa ellā uṟuppukaḷum poruntip paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarntu muṟṟip, piṉ akkaruppiyi viṭṭakaṉṟu veḷippaṭutalākavē eṅkum ekkālattum nikaḻntu varutalik kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum aṟintum varukiṉṟōmākalāṉum, ivvāṟu eṅkum māṟāmal nikaḻa amitta iṟivaṉatu amippukkumāṟāka vāyiṟ piṟattalum tōḷ toṭi aṭi eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷiṟ piṟattalum eññāṉṟum evviṭattum nikaḻakkāṇō mākalāṉum eṉpatu. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟu, makkaḷukkuttāṉ makavukaḷi vayiṟṟil amittup piṟakkavittaṉaṉē yallāmal, ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ taṉṉiṉiṉṟu makkaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṟku attakiya amippi vēṇṭuvāṉ allaṉ; avaṉ taṉatu uṟuppil enta iṭattiṉiṉṟum avarit tōṟṟuvikka vallaṉ eṉiṉ; aṅṅaṉam ellām valla iṟivaṉ makkaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṟku avaṉatu niṉivoṉṟē pōtumāyirukka, vāy tōḷ toṭi aṭi mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷum ataṟku vēṇṭumō eṉak kūṟimaṟukka. mēlum, iṟivaṉ taṉṉuṟuppukkaḷiṉiṉṟu makkaḷip paṭittataṉi ammakkaḷuḷ orupālārākiya kaṟṟavar kaṇṭaṟivateṅṅaṉam? evarum tāyvayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu piṟakkiṉṟa ñāṉṟu tām aṅṅaṉam piṟattali aṟinta varallar. tām piṟantu vaḷarntu aṟivuṭiya rāṉapiṉ, kuḻavikaḷ tāyvayiṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟattalip palakāluṅ kaṇṭuvittē tāmum aṅṅaṉam piṟantiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa ellārum uyttuṇarkiṉṟār. immuṟi yōṭu oppa, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷum ippōtu evviṭattāyiṉum tammuṭiya vāy tōḷ toṭi aṭi mutalāṉa uṟuppukaḷiliruntu pirāmaṇar kṣattiriyar viciyar cūttirar eṉpārit tōṟṟuvikka nām kāṇiṉaṉṟō, mutaṉmutal avar an nāṟpiriviṉariyum paṭitta kālattil avarkaḷi aṅṅaṉamē tōṟṟuvittiruppāreṉa uṇaralām. avvāṟu kaṭavuḷ tammuṭiya nālvēṟu uṟuppuk kaḷiṉiṉṟu nālvēṟu vakuppiṉarit tōṟṟuvittal eṅkum nikaḻakkāṇāmi yāṉum, iyaṅkum uyirkaḷ attaṉiyum tāytantiyariṉ cērkkiyāṟ ṟāyiṉ karuppiyiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟak kāṇṭalāṉum, ivvāṟu naṭipeṟum uyirkaḷiṉ piṟappu ulakam uṇṭāṉa kālamutaṟ piṟaḻāmal nikaḻntuvarutal muṉṉuḷḷa uṇmiyaṟiviṉar toṉṟutoṭṭu varaṉ muṟiyāka eḻutivittirukkum uṇmi nūlkaḷāl aṟiyak kiṭattalāṉum, tāytantiyariṉ cērkkiyiṉāl tāyiṉ karuppiyiṉiṉṟē pārppaṉar aracar vaṇikar aṭiyōr eṉṉum ellāmakkaḷum piṟattalik kaṇkūṭāka yāvaruṅ kaṇṭuvarutalāṉum, immuṟiyiyē teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvanāyaṉār piṟappu okkum ellā uyirkkum eṉṟu iraṇṭāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē aruḷic ceytiruttalāṉum, iṅṅaṉam piṟaviyeṭukkum amippuk kaṭavuḷ vakuttatēyallāmaṟ piṟarevarum vakutta tallāmiyāṉum, tāṉ vakutta ivvamippukku māṟāka iṟivaṉ vēṟoruvāṟṟāl makkaḷit tōṟṟuvittā ṉeṉṟāl piṟaḻā iyaṟkiyaṉākiya avaṉatu iṟimit taṉmikku iḻukkātalāṉum, maṟṟu avvāṟu māṟāṉa tōṟṟaṅ kūṟutal ellārkkum pulaṉāy nikaḻum uṇmi nikaḻccikkum paṇṭutoṭṭu uṇmiyaṟiviṉar kūṟivarum ellā uṇmiyurikkum muṟṟum muraṇāy muṭitalāṉum aṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭavuḷ vāy mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiṉiṉṟē nālvaki makkaṭpiriviṉariyun tōṟṟuvittāṉeṉa vari yaṟikaṭantu pēcutal peritum ētamāmeṉak kūṟiviṭukka. ataṉāl, annālvaki makkaṭkuriya nālvēṟu toḻilkaḷi uṇarttutaṟkē uruvaka vakiyāl iṟivaṉṟaṉ vāy mutaliya uṟuppukaḷiṉiṉṟu an nālvarun tōṉṟiṉāreṉa ap puruṭacūttavuri moḻiyalāyiṟṟeṉa ataṟkup poruntu māṟu paṟṟip poruḷurittuk koḷka.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, mēlkīḻ eṉṉum uyarvutāḻvukaḷi uṇarttutaṟkē, aḵtu aṅṅaṉam uruvaka vakiyāl mēluṟuppākiya vāyiṉiṉṟu pārppaṉarum ēṉik kīḻ uṟuppukaḷākiya tōḷ toṭi aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu ēṉi yōrun tōṉṟiṉar eṉak kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟāṟ paṭum iḻukkeṉṉiyeṉiṟ; pulāl nāṟṟam vīcum makkaḷiṉ uṭampukaḷ viṉikaḷi nukarutaṟporuṭṭu māyiyil amittuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉavākaliṉ, ivvuṭampukaḷiṟ poruntiya mēluṟuppukaḷi uyarntaṉaveṉṟum, malak kaḻivukaḷukku iṭamāṉa kīḻ uṟuppukaḷit tāḻntaṉa veṉṟuṅ kūṟutal okkum. maṟṟu ellām valla iṟivaṉatu tiruvuruvamō aṅṅaṉam piṟaroruvarāl māyiyiṉiṉṟu ākkappaṭuvatu allāmiyiṉālum, avaṉ taṉ aṉparkkup pulappaṭṭuttōṉṟi aruḷceytaṟku niṉintavaḷavāṉē aḵtu avvaruḷniṉiviṟ ṟōṉṟi miṉṉoḷiyiṉum mikka pēroḷiyum nuṇmiyun tūymiyum vāyntatāy maṟivatoṉṟākalāṉum, appeṟṟittākiya avaṉatu tūya aruḷuṭampil ōr uṟuppiṉi uyarntateṉṟalum maṟṟi ōr uṟuppiṉit tāḻntateṉṟalum oruvāṟṟāṉum ovvā. pimpoṉṉiṉāṟ camikkappaṭṭa orupāvi mēlkīḻ iṭameṅkuñ cuṭarviṭu poṉvaṭivāyē viḷaṅ kutalpōlavum, tūya kaṉṉaṟpākiṉāl vārttamikkap paṭṭa oru pāviyiṉ uṭampeṅkun tittikkum iyal piṟṟākavē iruttal pōlavum iṟivaṉatu uṭampu muḻutum orē tūymittāy viḷaṅkumallāmal, atu taṉṉilē uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ uṭittāy iruppataṉṟeṉa uṇarntu koḷka. ākavē, iṟivaṉatu mēluṟuppiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉāreṉpatu paṟṟip pārppaṉari mēlōr eṉṟalum, kīḻuṟuppiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉāreṉpatu paṟṟi ēṉi mūvarik kīḻōr eṉṟalum ciṟitum ovvā, annālvarum iṟivaṉuṭiya nālvēṟu uṟuppukaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉār eṉṟatu, an nālvakuppārkkun taṉittaṉiyē ciṟappāka uriya nālvakit toḻilkaḷi uṇarttiyapaṭiyām; ituvē ap puruṭacūtta uriyiṉ uṇmip poruḷeṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka. mēlum, in nālvakuppiṉarum iṟivaṉ oruvaṉiṭattē niṉṟu tōṉṟiṉavareṉpatu ataṉuḷ viḷakkamākac collap paṭṭiruttaliṉ, avarkaḷ oru tantikkup piṟanta makkaḷēyāva reṉpatum, ataṉāl avartamakkuḷ uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpittal ciṟitum ēlāteṉpatum karuttiṟ patikkaṟpālaṉavākum. innālvaki makkaṭpiriviṉariṉ peyari avvavar toḻil vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi eṭuttōtum patikam ippuruṭacūtta mantirattit tavira vēṟētum irukkuvētattil eṅkuṅ kāṇappaṭāmi yāṉum, oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷil irukku ecur cāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟuvētaṅkaḷiṉ peyari eṭuttuc collum ippuruṭa cūttam ammūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷun tōṉṟiya piṉṉaṉṟi muṉṉiruntatu ākāmiyāṉum, nālvēṟu makkaṭ pākupāṭum ataṉiyeṭuttuk kūṟum puruṭacūttamum irukku ecur cāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷum iyaṟṟap paṭṭa kālattil iruntaṉa alla eṉpatum uṇarntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, māpāratappōril muṉintuniṉṟa kurukula pāñcāla aracarkaḷiyum, avar vaḻiyilvanta jaṉakaṉ, ajātacatturu, jaṉamē jayaparikṣit, ivaṟku āciriyaṉākiya vicampāyaṉaṉ mutaliyōriyum eṭuttōtum kiruṣṇa cuklayajur vētamum ataṉōṭu oru kālattatākiya cāmavētamum  am māpāratappōr nikaḻntataṟkuppiṉ ākkappaṭṭaṉavātal teḷippaṭum. ivviraṇṭu vētaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kālatti yaṭuttē, av vētaṅkaḷukku viḷakkurikaḷāy eḻunta `pirāmaṇaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟi yaṭuttē vēḷvi vēṭṭali maṟuttu muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷiṉ meymmiyip pulappaṭuttavum paṉṉiraṇṭu paḻiya `upaniṭataṅkaḷum ākkappaṭṭaṉa. ivikaḷuṇṭāṉa kālattiluṅkūṭap, piṟappaḷavil cātivēṟupaṭu collap paṭavilli. cukkilayacurvētattiṉ muppatām iyaliṟ palavēṟu toḻilkaḷicceyyum palavēṟu makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉ peyarkaḷ maṭṭumē eṭutturikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉamē tittirīya pirāmaṇattiluñ collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl, avaṟṟuṭ cātivēṟupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ uyarpu iḻipukaḷum pēcappaṭavilli. ataṟku orucāṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭutum: muṉṉorukāl caracuvati yāṟṟaṅkariyilē iruṭikaḷ oruvēḷvi vēṭkalāyiṉar. appōtu `illūṣi eṉṉum ōr aṭimip peṇṇiṉ putalvarāṉa `kavaṣā eṉpavar avvēḷvikku vantiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ avari ōr aṭimiyiṉ piḷḷi eṉpatupaṟṟi ikaḻntu, avvēḷvikkaḷattiṉiṉṟun turatta, avar ceṉṟa iṭattiṟkē av yāṟṟu nīr cellumāṟu kaṭavuḷ aruḷceytaṉar; ataṉi yuṇarnta iruṭikaḷ avari yikaḻnta tamatu aṟiyāmikku varuntip piṉṉar avari oru ciṟanta iruṭiyāka ēṟṟu vaṇaṅkiṉar; eṉṟu aitarēyapirā maṇam (2, 19) pukalukiṉṟatu. ik `kavaṣā eṉpavarē irukku vētattiṉ pattām maṇṭilattiṟ pala patikaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟṟāṟ pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa kālattilum cāti vēṟṟumi nilipeṟavilli yeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉṉum, pala vēḷvikaḷic ceytu muṭittavaṉum, avvēḷvikaḷil muṉivararkkum piṟarkkum ērāḷamāṉa naṉkoṭi vaḻaṅkiṉavaṉum, vicuvāmittira muṉivarip pātukāttavaṉum, irukkuvēta ēḻām maṇṭilattiṉ 18 ām patikattiṟ pārāṭṭappaṭṭavaṉumāṉa `pijavaṉaṉ eṉpāṉ oru cūttiraṉē eṉṟu māpāratam cāntiparvam (2304 -ām ceyyuḷiliruntu) nuvalkiṉṟatu.
iṉṉum, irukkuvētap patikaṅkaḷi ākkiya muṉivarar palarum araca vakuppiṉiyum vaṇika vakuppiṉiyuñ cērntōr āvareṉṟu maṟcapurāṇattiṉ 132-ām iyal vakutturikkiṉṟatu. irukkuvētattiṉ mūṉṟām maṇṭilatti ākkiya vicuvāmittirarum, maṟṟi maṇṭilaṅ kaḷiṟ patikaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi ākkiya vivacuvatamaṉu, iṭaṉ, purūravar mutalāyiṉārum aracavakuppiṉarē āvar; palantar, vantiyar, caṅkīrtti mutaliyōr viciya vakuppiṉarāvar. irukkuvētattiṉ, mutal maṇṭilattuḷḷa 100 ām patikatti iyaṟṟiya rijrācuvar, amparīṣar, cakatēvar, payamānar, curātar mutalāṉa iruṭikaḷ aivarum viruṣākir aracaṉ putalvarāvar eṉṟu irukkuvētattiṉ anukkira maṇikiyē pukalkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē, avvirukkuvētattiṉ 6 vatu maṇṭilattiluḷḷa 15-ām patikatti ākkiya amparīṭar makaṉāṉa cintutvīparum, ataṉ 75- ām patikatti iyaṟṟiya piriyamētar makaṉāṉa cintukṣittarum, ataṉ 179-ām patikattippāṭiya ucīnarariṉ makaṉ cipiyum, kācimaṉṉaṉ tivōtācaṉ makaṉ piratarttaṉarum, ataṉ 98- ām patikattip pukaṉṟa cantanuviṉ makaṉ tēvāpiyum ellām araca iṉattic cērntōrē yāvar. ivvāṟē iṉṉum palar uḷar.
mēṟcolliya irukkuvētap patikaṅkaḷi ākkiya kṣattiriya viciyar eṉpār tamiḻa vēḷāḷa vakuppiṉarē yāvar. yāṅṅaṉa meṉiṟ kūṟutum: mēṟkāṭṭiya rijrācuvar mutalāṉa araca muṉivar aivarum cērntiyaṟṟiya patikattiṉ 12 ām ceyyuḷil `cōmaṉ eṉṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ aintu iṉattārik kāppavarākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ic `cōmaṉ eṉṉuñ col `ca umā eṉṉum irumoḻip puṇarcciyāṟṟōṉṟi umiyōṭu kūṭiṉavar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum eṉa atarvacira upaniṭata uriyilē viḷakkappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, accol civapirāṉ mēṟṟātalum, `cōmanātam eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭuc civapirāṉ tirukkōyil ap peyarpeṟṟiruttaliṉ aḵtu ataṟkup piṉṉum orucāṉṟātalum naṉku viḷaṅkum. iṉic civapirāṉāṟ kākkappaṭum aivaki iṉattiṉar āvār turuvācarkaḷum yatukkaḷum aṇukkaḷum turukiyarkaḷum pūrukkaḷum eṉa aivar āvareṉṟu kiripit eṉṉum āciriyar naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟār.1 iv vivaki iṉattiṉarum tamiḻ makkaṭ piriviṉarē yāvareṉṟu rākociṉ eṉṉum varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyar tām āḻntārāyn teḻutiya vēta intiyā eṉṉum nūlil virittu viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟār2. vaṭakkiruntuvantu intiyanāṭṭuḷ nuḻinta āriya makkaḷi etirtta pattu aracarkaḷukkut talivarāy niṉṟōr pūrukkaḷum, avarkkut tuṇivarāy añcā neñciṉarāy nilaviya paratarkaḷum āvar. it tamiḻa aracar patiṉmaruṭ civar (civar) viṣāniyar (viṣṇavar) eṉpār iruvarum cērttuccollappaṭukiṉṟaṉar3. ākavē, attuṇip paḻiyakālattilēyē tamiḻaruṭ civapirāṉi vaṇaṅkuñ civarum, viṣṇuvi vaṇaṅkum viṣṇavarum valiyarāyiruntu āriyari etirttu niṉṟami pulaṉākum. paḻiya tamiḻmakkaḷuṭ pōr valimiyil mikac ciṟantuniṉṟa paratar tam kuṭiyiṟṟōṉṟiya aracarkaḷē iv intiyanāṭu muḻumiyum āṇṭuvantaṉar; atupaṟṟiyē, avarkaḷiṉ ceṅkōl nīḻalilvantu vikiya āriyamakkaḷ iv intiyanāṭṭip `pāratavarṣam eṉṟu aḻippārāyiṉar. kumarināṭu kaṭalkoṇṭapiṉ aṅkirunta   tamiḻarkaḷē vaṭanāṭuvariyiṟ ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟip pattuvaki araciyali nāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ vaṭamaturiyil aracāṇṭa yatukulatta varkaḷē piṉṉark kaṇṇaṉit talivaṉāyk koṇṭu tuvārakiyil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. av yatukulattavaril oru pakutiyārē peyarttun tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pōntu erumiyūriṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar1. erumiyūriṟ kuṭiyēṟiya yatukulattavarākiya vēḷirkkut talivaṉē puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭum iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum maṉṉaṉ āvaṉ. ivvāṟu ivarpōṟ ṟamiḻnāṭṭiṉum piṟanāṭukaḷiṉum annāḷiṟ paraviyirunta vēḷāḷa maṉṉarkaḷ varalāṟukaḷi urikkappukiṉ itu mika viriyum. ittamiḻ vēḷāḷa aracarkaḷiyē paḻiya vaṭamoḻi vētaṅkaḷum upaniṭataṅ kaḷum `rājanyar, `kṣattiriyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟippavāyiṉa eṉpataṟku, maṉu tirāviṭarkaḷi `kṣattiriyar eṉṟu kūṟutalē (10, 43, 44) cāṉṟām2. tamiḻa vēḷāḷaril uḻavutoḻil ceytārum, uḻavutoḻilāṟ peṟṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuviṟṟārumē vaṭa nūlkaḷil `viciyar eṉṟu nuvalappaṭuvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟu kāṭṭap paṭṭa kṣattiriyarum viciyarumākiya tamiḻa vēḷāḷarē toṉṟu toṭṭuc civapirāṉi vaṇaṅki varupavarāy iruttaliṉ, ammakkaṭ piriviṉar aivariyuṅ kāppavar `cōmaṉ eṉṉum civapirāṉāka irukkuvētam pukaluva tāyiṟṟeṉka. eṉavē, irukkuvētattiṟ palappala patikaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟiya kṣattiriya viciyarkaḷ aṉivarum tamiḻa vēḷāḷarkaḷē yātalit teṟṟeṉa  uṇarntu koḷka.
ivvāṟu kṣattiriya viciyarākiya vēḷāḷarkaḷum, ivar tamakkuk kuṟṟēvaṟṟoḻil purivārāṉa cūttirarum, maṟṟi āriyarum kalantu vētaṅkaḷiyum pirāmaṇaṅ kaḷiyum paṉṉiraṇṭu upaniṭataṅkaḷiyum ākkivitta kālattiṟ, piṟappaḷavil tamakkuḷ uyarvutāḻvukāṇātu, uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta nālvēṟu toḻil kaḷiyuñ ceytukoṇṭu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷil ētoru vēṟu pāṭumiṉṟi orumittu vāḻntaṉarākaliṉ, ak kālattiṟ piṟappaḷavil uyarvutāḻvu koḷḷum cātivēṟṟumi ciṟitumilliyeṉṟu teḷika. itaṉālaṉ ṟō vāyu purāṇamum (8, 890 - 193) tīyavaṉukku, maṉat tūymi iḻan tavaṉukku vētaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ caṭaṅku kaḷum vēḷvikaḷum paṭṭiṉikiṭattalum teṉpulattār kaṭaṉiṟuttal mutalāṉa viṉikaḷum payaṉit tarukiṉṟila; akattē iḻinta iyaṟkiyuṭiyavaṉā yiruppōṉ puṟattē ettuṇi muyaṟciyōṭu caṭaṅkukaḷic ceytā ṉāyiṉum, avi ciṟitum payaṉpaṭā. oruvaṉ aḻukku neñcattōṭu taṉakkuḷḷa poruḷ muḻutuṅ koṭuttā ṉēṉum, ataṉāl naṉmi eytamāṭṭāṉ; ātalāl, nalla taṉmiyē uyarccikku ētuvākum. eṉṟu kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, mūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷukkum piṟpaṭṭatākiya puruṭa cūttattilum, ataṟkum piṟpaṭṭatākiya pirukatāraṇiyaka upaniṭattilum (1, 4, 15) nālvakic cātippeyar kāṇap paṭiṉum, muṉṉiyatil an nāṟcātiyiṉarum oru muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ piḷḷikaḷātalum, piṉṉiyatil an nāṟcāti yiṉariṭattum iṟivaṉ niṟintu viḷaṅkutalum naṉku vaṟpuṟuttu urikkappaṭutaliṉ, in nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattil nālvakiccātikaḷ iruntami peṟappaṭiṉum, avaruḷ uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ iruntami pulaṉākavilli.
pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟku muṟpaṭṭa vēta kālattil pūṇūl aṇiyum vaḻakkaṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyāl, atupaṟṟi yuṇṭāṉa cātivēṟṟumiyum  aññāṉṟu iruntatilli. catapatapirāmaṇattilum (2, 4, 2), kuṣītaki upaniṭatattilumē (2, 7) pūṇūl aṇiyuṅkuṟippu mutaṉmutaṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭiluṅkūṭap pakalavaṉ kīḻpāl eḻumpōtu avaṉi vaṇaṅkuṅ kālippoḻutilum, vēḷvivēṭkum poḻutilumē māntar pūṇūl aṇintu maṟṟik kālaṅkaḷil ataṉik kaḻaṟṟiviṭum vaḻakkam naṉku terittōtap paṭṭamiyāl, in nūlkaḷ uṇṭāṉa kālattilum cātivēṟṟumiyiṉ poruṭṭup pūṇūl aṇiyappaṭavilli yeṉpatum, iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭun toḻiliṟ pukuntiruppōr attoḻiliṟ pukātār tammi aṇukāmal vilakip pōtaṟku ōr aṭiyāḷamākavē ataṉi attoḻil iyaṟṟum nēraṅkaḷil maṭṭum aṇintiruntaṉareṉpatum iṉitu viḷaṅkum. maṟṟu ikkālattilō iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkāmal, ulakiyaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷiyē allum pakalum purintu tamatu vayiṟṟukkum tammic cērntāratu vayiṟṟukkumē pāṭupaṭṭu uḻaṉṟu tīviṉikaḷi īṭṭuvōr ennēramum appūṇūli aṇintukoṇṭu tammi uyarnta cātiyārāka eṇṇi iṟumāntu aiyakō! mēṉmēṟ ṟīviṉikaḷip perukki varukiṉṟaṉarē! atu kiṭakka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	See R.T.H. Griffith’S English translation of the Rig Veda, Vol. I. p. 10, foot note.
2.	Vedic India by Zenide A. Ragozin, pp. 323-329.
3.	irukkuvētam, 7, 18.
4.	See A History of Civilisation in Ancient India, by R.C. Dutt, Vol. I . 219.
5.	Ibid. VIl. II. p. 86.
iyal - 2
paḻiya upaniṭataṅkaḷ 
itikācaṅkaḷil cāti illi

iṉi, īcakēṉa kaṭam mutalāṉa paḻiya paṉṉīrupa niṭataṅkaḷiṟ pala tōṉṟiya kālattum nālvakic cāti vakuppu mikutiyāyk kāṇappaṭāmal, pirāmaṇar rājanyar eṉṉam iraṇṭu pirivumaṭṭumē aṭuttaṭuttuk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭu pirivum avvavar toḻil paṟṟi vantaṉavē yallāmal, avar tamakkuḷ evvakiyāṉa vēṟupāṭēṉum iruntamipaṟṟi vantaṉa alla. vēḷvi vēṭṭalum nūl ōtutalumākiya toḻilkaḷicceypavar pirāmaṇar eṉṟum, pōr puritalum nāṭu kāttalumākiya toḻilkaḷic ceypavar rājnyar allatu cattiriyar eṉṟum toḻilkaḷiyē ceyya vēṇṭu meṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭu ciṟitum iruntatilli. turōṇarum avar āciriyar akkiṉivēcarum avar putalvar acuvattāmarum avar mittuṉar kiruparum pōṉṟa pirāmaṇar pōrttoḻil payiṉṟamiyum, piriyamētar ciṉi kārkkiyar tiriyāruṇi mutalāṉa kṣattiriyar nūlkaḷ ōtip pirāmaṇarāṉamiyum (viṣṇu purāṇam, 4, 19, 9, 10) mēṟkūṟiyataṉi nāṭṭutaṟkup pōtu māṉa cāṉṟām. ivi maṭṭum alla. iv viruvakuppārum ētum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi oṉṟucērntu uṇavaruntiyum, peṇ koṇṭu koṭuttum orumiyāy vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. caryāta aracaṉ putalvi cukaṉyiyic cayavanar maṇantami catapatapirāmaṇattiṟ collappaṭṭatu; ratavīti aracaṉ putalviyic ciyāvācuvar maṇantami irukkuvētattil (5, 61) collappaṭṭatu. vitarppaṉ eṉṉum kṣattiriyaṉ putalviyi akattiyar maṇantukoṇṭār; ulōmapātaṉ putalviyi iruciya ciruṅkar maṇantukoṇṭār;  (māpāratam); iṅṅaṉamē rājanyar putalvikaḷip pirāmaṇar maṇantukoṇṭa varalāṟukaḷ iṉṉum eṇṇiṟantaṉa irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉip pirāmaṇar putalvikaḷi maṟṟi vakuppārāṉa rājanyar maṇantukoṇṭa varalāṟukaḷum pala irukkiṉṟaṉa. cukkirāccāriyār eṉṉum pirāmaṇar tēvayānī eṉṉum tam putalviyi yayāti eṉṉum aracaṉukkuk koṭuttār; maṟṟoru peṇṇi aṉūkaṉukkuk koṭuttār (māpāratam). iḻikuṭippiṟanta akṣamāli eṉṉum peṇṇi vaciṭṭarum cāraṅkiyi mantapālaruṅ kūṭiṉami maṉumiruti (9, 22) iṟ collappaṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉamē ñāṉōpatēcaṅkaḷum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟic ceyyappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. vitēkamaṉṉaṉākiya jaṉakaṉ cuvētakētu aruṇēyar, cōmacuṣmacātyayaññi, yāññavalkiyar mutalāṉa pārppaṉa muṉivar palarkku ñāṉōpatēcañ ceytaṉaṉ eṉṟu catapatapirāmaṇam (10, 6, 2, 1) pukalkiṉṟatu; kācimaṉṉaṉākiya ajātacatturu eṉṉum aracaṉāl pālāki eṉṉum  pirāmaṇariṣi ñāṉōpatēcañ ceyyappaṭṭa varalāṟu paḻiya kuṣītakī pirāmaṇa upaniṭatattiṟ (4,1) kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāñcāla maṉṉaṉākiya piravākanajivali eṉpāṉ cuvētakētu aruṇēyarkku meypporuḷ aṟivuṟut tiṉami, catapatapirāmaṇam (14, 9, 1, 1), pirukatāraṇiyaka upaniṭatam (6, 2,1), cāntōkkiya upaniṭatam (5, 3, 1) mutaliya vaṟṟil naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. ivvuṇmikaḷāl irukkuvētattiṟkup piṟ paḻiya upaniṭataṅkaḷ eḻunta kālattilum pirāmaṇar kṣattiriyar eṉṉum iraṇṭu vakuppē iruntaṉa veṉpatum, avviraṇṭu vakuppun toḻil paṟṟi uṇṭāyiṉavē yallāmaṟ piṟkālattiṟpōlap piṟappaḷavāl uṇṭāyiṉa valla veṉpatum, avviru vakuppārum tamakkuḷ ētum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi uṇṇal kalattalkaḷāl orumittu vāḻntaṉar eṉpatum iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
iṉip purāṇaṅkaḷ itikācaṅkaḷ piṟpaṭṭa upaniṭataṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattilētāṉ pirāmaṇar kṣattiriyar viciyar cūttirar eṉṉum nāṉku vakuppukaḷ pirintaṉa. in nālvakic cātiyārum orē tantikkup piḷḷikaḷāyp piṟantu nālvēṟu toḻilāl nālvakippaṭṭārkaḷē yallāmal avarkaḷuḷḷum uṇṇal kalattalkaḷil ētum vēṟṟumi ēṟpaṭṭatilli. cūttiraṉ camiyal ceyta uṇavip pārppaṉaṉ aruntum muṟi paḻiya kaṟpacūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu.1 vāyupurāṇattum2 piṉ varumāṟu collappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. kirutayukattiṉkaṇ cātikaḷ ētumē illi; ataṉpiṉṉar nāṉmukaṉ avvavar toḻilkaḷukku ēṟpa makkaḷuḷ nāṉku vakuppukaḷ ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ. piṟari āḷutaṟkēṟṟa āṇmicceya luṭiyavarkaḷip piṟarik kākkum poruṭṭu kṣattiriyareṉṟu niyamittāṉ. tannayam pārāṭṭāmaṟ piṟar nalaṅ karuti uṇmiyē pēci vētattic cevviyāka ōtiṉavarip pirāmaṇar eṉa vittāṉ. muṉṉē valiyaṟṟavarkaḷāy nilatti uḻuvatilum kittoḻil purivatilun taliyiṭṭu uḻaviṉāl uyir vāḻkkikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi eṭuttut tantavarkaḷi viciyar eṉa vittāṉ. tuppuravu ceypavarkaḷāy ēviya toḻilmaṭṭuñ ceyyum valiyaṟṟavarkaḷic cūttirar eṉṟu vittāṉ. ivvāyupurāṇa uriyuḷḷum cātiyāṉatu toḻilpaṟṟi vantateṉṟu collappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatē yallāmaṟ, piṟappiṉāl vantateṉṟu ciṟitum collappaṭavilli.  iṉi, innālvakic cātiyā ruḷḷum evar uyarntōr evar tāḻntōr eṉṟu viṉāvuṅkāl, oḻukkattāl mikkavarē uyarnta cātiyā reṉavum, oḻukkamillātavarē tāḻnta cātiyā reṉavum, makāpāratam cānti paruvattiṉ kaṇ (6925) uṟutippaṭut turikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. appakuti varumāṟu:
pārattuvāca muṉivar piruku muṉivari viṉāvu kiṉṟār:
nālvakic cātivēṟṟumikkum niṟantāṉ ētuvāku meṉṟu coṉṉāl ellāc cātiyuḷḷum niṟaṅkaḷ pala tiṟappaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
viruppum vekuḷiyum accamum pēravāvum kalakkamum kavaliyum paciyum kaḷippum nammellāriṭattum mikuntu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉamāyiṉ cātippakuppu evvāṟu poruntum?
viyarviyum ciṟunīrum malamum caḷiyum pittamum irattamum ellārkkum potuvāy uḷḷaṉa; ellāruṭiya uṭampukaḷum aḻintupaṭukiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉamāyiṉ etaṉikkoṇṭu cāti vakukkappaṭum?
aciyum poruḷum aciyāp poruḷumākiya vakikaḷum eṇṇiṟantaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa; ip pakuppukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum cātivakuppuc ceytal eṅṅaṉam?
ivaṟṟiṟkup piruku maṟumoḻi kūṟukiṉṟār:
cātivēṟṟumi eṉpatu oṉṟilli. ulakamuḻutum nāṉ mukaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭamiyāl toṭakkattil eṅkum pirāmaṇar maṭṭumē iruntaṉar. toḻilkaḷi ṉālēyē cātikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
aimpoṟi yiṉpanukarcciyil viḻivuḷḷavarkaḷum, koṭumiyum ericcalum niṟintavarkaḷum, tām vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷip peṟuvatil ūkkam vāyntavarkaḷum, tām ceytaṟkuriya uṭamikaḷi viṭṭavarkaḷum, cenniṟam vāyntavarkaḷum āy irupiṟappāḷarāṉa pirāmaṇar kṣattiriyaruṭiya nilimiyi aṭintārkaḷ.
āṭu māṭu mēykkun toḻili mēṟkoṇṭavar kaḷum, mañcaḷ niṟam vāyntavarkaḷum, uḻavutoḻilāṟ piḻippavarkaḷum, tammuṭiya kaṭamikaḷil nillāta varkaḷum āy irupiṟappāḷarāṉa pirāmaṇar viciya ruṭiya niliyi aṭintārkaḷ.
kuṟumpu ceyvatilum poy kūṟuvatilum viruppa muḷḷavarḷum, pēravāp piṭiyuṇṭavarkaḷum, ellāvaki yāṉatoḻilkaḷiyum ceytu avaṟṟāṟ piḻippavar kaḷum, kariyaniṟam vāyntavarkaḷum, tūymi tuppuravu illātavar kaḷuma āy irupiṟappāḷarāṉa pirāmaṇar cūttiraruṭiya nilimiyi aṭintārkaḷ.
ittakiya toḻilkaḷāṟ pirikkappaṭṭu irupiṟap pāḷarē cātikaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭaṉar. aṟamum vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷum ivarkaḷil evarkkum eppōtum vilakkappaṭṭaṉavalla.
aintām vētamākiya pāratattilē pārattuvācarum pirukuvum uriyāṭiyatākap peṟappaṭṭa intap pakuti yiṉāṟ cātivakuppu oḻukkattiṉāl vakukkappaṭṭatē yallāmaṟ piṟappiṉāl aṉṟeṉpatu iṉitu viḷakkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
akkālaṅkaḷil, uṇmi pēcutalē mikac ciṟanta oḻukkamākavum, ataṉiyuṭiyōrē pirāmaṇarākavum karutappaṭṭaṉar. piṟappiṉāl iḻintavarāyiruppiṉum, avar uṇmipēcutaliṟ piṟaḻātavarāyiṉ avarē pārppaṉarākap pārāṭṭappaṭuvārāyiṉar. itu cāntōkkiya upaniṭatattiṟ (4,4, 1-5) pōnta cattiyakāmajāpālaṉ katiyiṉāl naṉkuviḷaṅkum; atu varumāṟu:
japālā eṉpavariṉ makaṉākiya cattiyakāmaṉ eṉpāṉ taṉ aṉṉiyi nōkki, `ammā, nāṉ piramacāriya oḻukkatti mēṟkoḷḷa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. nāṉ entak kuṭumpattiṟ kuriyavaṉ? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku avaḷ: `kuḻantāy, nī entak kuṭumpattiṟku uriyi eṉpati yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. eṉ iḷamikkālattil yāṉ ōr ūḻiyakkāriyāy aṅkumiṅkumāy irukkiyil niṉṉik karukkoṇṭēṉ. ātalāl, nī entak kuṭumpattiṟku uriyiyeṉpati yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. eṉpeyar japālā; uṉ peyarō cattiyakāmaṉ. ākavē, niṉṉic cattiyakāmajāpālaṉ eṉṟu terivittukkoḷ eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ.
ataṉpiṉ avaṉ hāriturumata kutamariṭampōy, `aiya nāṉ, oru piramacariyattavaṉāyt taṅkaḷiṭam irukka virumpukiṉṟēṉ. ataṉālētāṉ aiya, yāṉ taṅkaḷpāl vantēṉ eṉṟāṉ:
“avar avaṉinōkki, `ō naṇpaṉē, nī entak kuṭumpattiṟ,ku uriyi?    eṉa viṉaviṉār. ataṟku avaṉ, aiyā, yāṉ entakkuṭikku uriyēṉ eṉpati aṟiyēṉ. yāṉ eṉ aṉṉiyik kēṭṭa pōtu avaḷ, `eṉatu iḷamik kālattil yāṉ ōr ūḻiyakkāriyāy aṅku miṅkumāy irukkiyil niṉṉik karukkoṇṭēṉ. ataṉāl nī ekkuṭikku uriyi eṉpati yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. eṉpeyar japālā, niṉpeyarō cattiyakāmaṉ eṉa maṟumoḻi tantāḷ. ākiyāl, aiya, yāṉ cattiyakāma jāpālaṉ eṉappaṭuvēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
ataṉpiṉ avar avaṉinōkkip, `pirāmaṇaṉ allātavaṉ iṅṅaṉam uṇmipēcun takutiyuṭiyavaṉ ākāṉ. kuḻantāy, ōmaviṟaku koṇṭuvā, nī uṇmi pēcutaliṟ piṟaḻāmiyāl, yāṉ niṉakku meypporuḷi aṟivuṟuttuvēṉ eṉṟār.
paḻiya viḻumiya aṟivunūlākiya cāntōkkiya upaniṭattiṟ pōnta ivvariya katiyikkoṇṭu, piṟappiṉāl ettakiyavarāyiruppiṉum uṇmi kūṟutalil vaḻuvātavarō pārppaṉarāy uyarkuṭip piṟappiṉarāyp pārāṭṭappaṭṭataṉi aṟikiṉṟaṉam allamō?
iṉṉum, uyarnta cātiyāṉ evaṉ eṉṟu ārāyntu muṭivu kaṭṭiya vajracūci upaniṭata uri perituṅ karuttiṟ patikkaṟpālatoṉṟāy iruttaliṉ ataṉiyum iṅkē moḻipeyarttuk kāṭṭuvām:
aṟiyāmiyip pōḻuvatum, aṟivilāri iḻittut teyvavuṇarcci yuṭiyōṉi uyarttuvatum ākiya vajracūci eṉṉuṅ karuvi īteṉat teritturikka ippōtu pukukiṉṟēṉ.
pirāmaṇarum kṣattiriyarum viciyarum cūttirarum eṉa nālvakic cātiyiṉar uḷar. avarkaḷuṭ pirāmaṇarē mikac ciṟantōrāvareṉṟu vētavurikaḷukkup porutta mākavē mirutikaḷum pukalukiṉṟaṉa.
ātalāl iḵtu ārāyaṟpālatā yirukkiṉṟatu. pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉuñ collāl nuvalappaṭuvatu yātu? aḵtu ōr uyirā? allatu aḵtu ōr uṭampā? aḵtu oru vakuppā? allatu aḵtu oru ñāṉamā? (aṟivā?). aṉṟi aḵtu oru karmamā? (viṉiyā?) allatu aḵtu oru tarumatti (aṟattip) purivōṉā?
mutaliṟ, pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpatu uyirā? aṉṟu. kaḻintu pōṉa pala uṭampukaḷilum, iṉi varakkaṭava pala uṭampu kaḷilum pukuntum pukuvatāyum varuvatu ōr uyirēyāka lāṉum, viṉikkīṭākakkiṭitta palatiṟa uṭampu kaḷiliruntu varum uyir oṉṟēyākalāṉum uyir pirāmaṇaṉ āka māṭṭātu.
aṟṟēṟ, pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpatu uṭampā? aṉṟu. aivakip poruḷkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭa uṭampāṉa caṇṭāḷaṉ īṟāka ellā māntarkkum orē taṉmittāy iruttalāṉum, mutumiyum iṟappum aṟamum (tarmamum) maṟamum (pāvamum) avarkaḷellārmaṭṭum potumiyil nikaḻtalāṉum, pārppaṉa rellārum veṇṇiṟat tiṉarāyum araca rellārum cenniṟattiṉarāyum vaṇika rellārum mañcaḷ niṟattiṉarāyum aṭiyōrellārum karuniṟat tiṉarāyumē iruppareṉa variyaṟutta vēṟupāṭu yāṇṭum illāmi yāṉum, taṉ ṟanti mutalāyiṉār piṇattic cuṭukiyil oru pārppaṉaṉik koṉṟa paḻi putalvar mutalāyiṉārkkuc cērumātalāṉum uṭampu pirāmaṇaṉ ākamāṭṭātu.
iṉip, pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpatu oru vakuppā? aṉṟu. makaruṣikaḷ (periya muṉivarar) palar paṭippiṉkaṇ uḷḷa maṟṟi vakiyiṉariliruntuñ cātiyiṉariliruntun tōṉṟiṉar. ruṣiyaciruṅkar oru māṉvayiṟṟi piṟantār; kucikar kucippulliṟ piṟantār; jāmpukar oru narivayiṟṟiṟ piṟantār; vālmīki oru  puṟṟiliruntu uṇṭāṉār; viyācar oru cempaṭavaṉ makaḷvayiṟṟiṟ piṟantār; kutamar orumuyaliṉ piṭṭattiliruntu uṇṭāṉār; vaciṭṭar ūrvaci eṉṉun tācivayiṟṟiṟ piṟantār; akattiyar oru kumpattil (kuṭattil) uṇṭāṉār; ivvāṟu yāṅkaḷ collakkēṭṭōm. ivarkaḷuṭ cātikkup puṟampāṉāruṅkūṭap, piramañāṉattik (kaṭavuḷuṇarcciyi) aṟivuṟukkum āciriyaril mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟārkaḷ; ātalāl, oru vakuppum pirāmaṇaṉāka māṭṭātu.
aṟṟēṟ, pirāmaṇaṉeṉpatu ñāṉamā? (aṟivā?). aṉṟu. kaṭavuḷuṇmiyiṉi aṟivatil nirampat tērcci peṟṟa kṣattiriyarum maṟṟiyōrum palar uḷarākaliṉ, ñāṉam pirāmaṇaṉ ākamāṭṭātu.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṟ, karmam (viṉi) pirāmaṇaṉā? aṉṟu. pirāraptam (kaḻintaviṉi), cañcitam (eccaviṉi), ākāmiyam (ēṟumviṉi) eṉṉuṅ karmaṅkaḷ ellā uyirkaḷukkum orē taṉmiyavāy iruttalāṉum, karmattiṉāl ēvap paṭṭē ellāmāntarkaḷum, tattam viṉikaḷic ceytalāṉum karmam pirāmaṇaṉākamāṭṭātu.
aṟṟēl, aṟattit (tarmattic) ceypavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉā? allaṉ. poṉṉi vaḻaṅkiṉōr kṣattiri yōrilum maṟṟiyōrilum palar uḷarākaliṉ, aṟattic ceyvōṉ pirāmaṇaṉ ākāṉ.
avvāṟāyiṉ uṇmiyiṟ pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpōṉ yāvaṉ? avaṉ evaṉāy iruppiṉum, taṉ uyirkku uyirāyirukkum iṟivaṉi uṇarntōṉē, taṉṉōṭu otta iraṇṭāva toru poruḷ illātatāyc cātiyuñ ceyalkaḷum aṟṟatāy aṟuvaki māṟutalkaḷum aṟuvakik kaṟikaḷākiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷum nīṅkiyatāy uṇmiyum aṟivum iṉpamum eṉṟumuḷḷa taṉmiyum uṭiyatāyt taṉṉil ētoru māṟutalum illatāy ellāk kaṟpaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyāy ellāp poruḷ kaḷiyum ūṭuruvik koṇṭu iruppatāy vāṉveḷi pōl ellāpporuḷ kaḷiṉ uḷḷumpuṟampum niṟintu niṟpatāyp pakukkappaṭāta iṉpa uruviṉatāy vaḻiyaḷaviyiṉ vittu aṟiyappaṭātatāy uyiriṉāl nērē uṇarap paṭuvatāyt taṉṉuḷ viḷaṅkum paramāṉvi (muḻumutaṟ poruḷi)t taṉ uḷḷaṅkiyiṉ nellikkaṉipōl nērāka uṇarntōṉē, tāṉ viḻin tatip peṟṟamiyāl ulakattup poruḷkaḷiyum iṉpaṅkaḷiyum viḻiyum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟṟōṉē, iruviṉi yoppu mutalāṉa uyarnta taṉmikaḷ vāyntōṉē, maṉaacivu poṟāmi ulakattup poruḷkaḷil avā viruppu mayakkaṅkaḷil nīṅkiṉōṉē, taṟperumiyālum taṟcerukkālum tīṇṭappeṟāta uḷḷattōṉē, motta māy ivvellā iyalpukaḷum avaṟṟi yaṭitaṟkuriya vaḻikaḷum oruṅku uṭiyōṉē pirāmaṇaṉ āvaṉ.
vētaṅkaḷ, mirutikaḷ, itikācaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ karuttu ippaṭittāṉ irukkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟaṉṟi oruvaṉ pirāmaṇaṉatu niliyi aṭital muṭiyātu. oruvaṉ taṉṉuḷḷirukkum paramāṉmāvic caccitāṉanta (uṇmi yaṟiviṉpa) uruviṟṟāka iraṇṭaṟṟa piramamāka vittu niṉittalvēṇṭum. ām, taṉṉuḷḷirukkum paramāṉvic caccitāṉanta piramamāka vittē niṉittal vēṇṭum. ittakiyatutāṉ ivvupaniṭatam.
cāmavētattiṉ pālatākiya iv vajracūci upaniṭatamāṉatu piṟappaḷaviṉāl evaṉum uyarnta cātiyāṉ ākamāṭṭāṉ eṉavum, puṉitattaṉmiyum muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷi yuṇarntu ataṉi vaḻipaṭum viḻumiya oḻukkamumākiya ceyaṟki vakiyiṉālēyē ekkuṭik piṟantavaṉāyiruppiṉum avaṉ uyarnta cātiyāṉ ākiṉṟāṉ eṉavum ārāyntu variyaṟuttu ellā nūlkaḷiṉ karuttum atuvēyeṉa muṭinta muṭipi valiyuṟuttic collutaliṉ piṟappaḷaviṟ cātiyuyarvu koḷḷutal vētam mutalākiya entanūlukkum uṭaṉpāṭu aṉṟeṉpatu naṉkupeṟṟām.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	āpatampa tarmacūttiram, 2, 2, 3, 4-9
2.	vāyupurāṇam, 8, 161- 165.
iyal - 3
mirutikaḷiṟ cātippirivum 
oḻukkam paṟṟiyatē

iṉi, āpatampar, kutamar, vaciṣṭar, pōtāyanar, manu mutalāyiṉār tarumacāttiraṅkaḷum mirutinūlkaḷum ākkiya kālantoṭṭē nālvēṟu cātivakuppum, avvavark kuriya toḻilkaḷum variyaṟuttu urikkap paṭṭaṉa. avvāṟu avi vakuttu urikkappaṭṭa viṭattum uyarnta toḻilkaḷic ceyvōrē uyarnta cātiyārākavum, iḻinta toḻilkaḷāka avarkaḷāṟ karutappaṭṭavikaḷic ceyvōrē iḻinta cāti yārākavum vikkappaṭṭaṉa rallāmaṟ, piṟappaḷaviṟ cātivēṟṟumi mēṟkūṟiya taruma cūttiraṅkaḷ miruti nūlkaḷuḷ yāṇṭuñ collappaṭa villi. uyarnta oḻukkaṅkaḷik kaṭippiṭittuc ceytaṟ kuriyōr, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvuvarāyiṉ avar iḻinta cāti yiṉarākavē avaṟṟuḷ aiyantiripukku iṭaṉiṉṟic collappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. vāciṣṭa taruma cūttiram,
pirāmaṇaṉāvatu kṣattiriyaṉāvatu kaṟkaḷ, uppu, caṇal, paṭṭu, caṇaṟṟuṇi, tōl mutaliyavikaḷi viṟṟal ākātu; cāyam ēṟṟiya puṭivikaḷum viṟṟal kūṭātu; camiyaṟceyta vuṇavum, pūkkaḷ paḻaṅkaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷ maṇppaṇṭaṅkaḷ uṇavukku naṟumaṇaccuvi ēṟṟum poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum, taṇṇīr pūṇṭukaḷi liruntu iṟakkiya cāṟu mutaliyaṉavum viṟṟal kūṭātu; cōmap pūṇṭu paṭikkalaṅkaḷ naccuppaṇṭaṅkaḷum viṟṟal ākātu; iṟicciyāvatu pālāvatu avaṟṟāl amikkap paṭṭa uṇavāvatu, takaram cemmeḻuku īyam mutaliyaṉa vāvatu viṟṟal kūṭātu (2,24-27) eṉavum, vētatti ōtātavarum ataṉip piṟarkkuk kaṟpiyātavarum aḻal ōmpātavarkaḷum āṉa pirāmaṇarkaḷ cūttiri ōttavarā kiṉṟaṉar; vēta uṇarcci yillātavaṉip pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṟu aḻittal ākātu; viyāpārañceytu piḻippa vaṉiyum, nāṭakam āṭuvōṉiyum, cūttirarkku ēval ceyvōṉiyum kaḷvaṉippōṟ piṟarporuḷik kavarvōṉiyum, maruttuvañceytu piḻippōṉiyum aṅṅaṉamē pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal ākātu; tamakkuriya tūya kaṭamikaḷic ceyyāmalum, vētam aṟiyāmalum aiyam ēṟṟup piḻikkum pārppaṉarkaḷ irukkum ūri aracaṉ oṟuttal (taṇṭittal) vēṇṭum; ēṉeṉil attakiya ūr koḷḷikkārarukku uṇṭikoṭuk kiṉṟatu. tammuṭiya puṉitak kaṭaṉkaḷi niṟivēṟṟāmalum, vētatti aṟiyāmalum, pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉuñ cātippeyar oṉṟimaṭṭuṅ koṇṭu piḻippittēṭum pirāmaṇar pallāyirampēr oruṅkukūṭiṉālum, avarkaḷ aṟaṅkūṟum aviyattār ākamāṭṭārkaḷ (3, 1-5) eṉavum vaṟpuṟutturikkiṉṟatu. itaṟku iciyavē kutama tarumacūttiramum (7, 8-21), manu mirutiyum (12, 114) pukalāniṟkiṉṟaṉa.
nālvakic cātikaḷum avaṟṟiṟkuriya kaṭamikaḷum variyaṟuttup, pārppaṉari varikaṭantu uyartti, ēṉi mūvariyum avaruṭ kaṭippaṭṭa cūttirariyum iḻittup, perun tokiyiṉarāṉa intumakkaḷi iḻinta cūttira vakuppiṟ cērttu, avarkaḷik kalviyilum nākarikattilum taliyeṭukka voṭṭāmal nacukkip, paṉṟi nāy kaḻuti mutalāṉa vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷilum avarkaḷit tāḻtti, niṉippiṉum neñcam naṭuṅkuṅ koṭun taṇṭaṉikaḷi (oṟuttalkaḷi)c ciṟitum irakkamum īra neñcamum iṉṟi ac cūttirarkku maṭṭumē ēṟpaṭuttic, cātippirivukaḷukku aḻuttamāṉa aṭippaṭikōliya tarumacūttiraṅkaḷ mirutikaḷuṅkūṭap piṟappaḷavil oruvaṉip pirāmaṇaṉākak kūṟutaṟku oruppaṭātirukkat, tām antaṇa araca vaṇika vakuppukaḷukku uriyavarkaḷāyiruntum avvuṇmiyi aṟintukoḷḷutaṟku ēṟṟa kalviyaṟivum ārāycci yuṇarvum iṉṟik, kiṟituvar `aññāṉi eṉṟaḻittāl ataṉ iḻanta poruḷi yaṟiyāmal ataṉit taṉakkuriya uyarnta paṭṭappeyarākak karutit taṉṉittāṉē `aññāṉi eṉṟu collikkoḷḷum puliyaṉip pōlat, tammip pārppaṉar `cūttirar eṉṟaḻittāl accolliṉ iḻintaporuḷi yaṟiyāmal tāmun tammic `cūttirar eṉavuñ `caṟcūttirar eṉavuñ collik koḷḷum pōliccivarkaḷ maṭṭum piṟappaḷavil uyarntavar ātal yāṅṅaṉam? nālvaki makkaṭpiriviṉaril mika uyarntōrākak karutappaṭṭa pārppaṉarukkē piṟappaḷavil uyarcci kikūṭātāyiṉ, an nālvaki yiṉariluṅ kaṭippaṭṭa cūttira vakuppiṉarākat tammittāmē oppukkoṇṭa pōlic civarkaḷmaṭṭum piṟappaḷavil uyarntavarātal yāṅṅaṉam? maṉumiruti eṭṭām attiyāyattil (413 - 415).
oru cūttiraṉ vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭālum vilikku vāṅkap paṭāviṭṭālum, avaṉi aṭimivēli ceyyumpaṭi oru pārppaṉaṉ kaṭṭāyappaṭuttalām; ēṉeṉṟāl, avaṉ pārppaṉaṉukku aṭimiyā yirukkum paṭi kaṭavuḷāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.
taṉ talivaṉāl viṭuvikkappaṭiṉum oru cūttiraṉ taṉ aṭimit taṉmiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuvikkappaṭṭavaṉ allaṉ; aḵtu avaṉukku iyaṟkiyāy iruppataṉāl, avaṉi ataṉiṉiṉṟum yārtām viṭuvikkakkūṭum?
caṇṭiyil veṉṟu ciṟiyākappiṭittuk koṇarap paṭṭavaṉ, taṉ aṟṟic cōṟṟukkāka ūḻiyañceypavaṉ, taṉ vīṭṭil taṉ vēcikkup piṟantavaṉ, vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭavaṉ, piṟarāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭavaṉ, taṉ muṉṉōr kālantoṭṭut taṉakku aṭimip poruḷāy varupavaṉ, kuṟṟattiṟkāka aṭimippaṭuttappaṭṭavaṉ eṉac cūttirar eḻuvakippaṭuvar. eṉṟu cūttirar iṉṉā reṉpatu teḷivākak kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. tammic cūttirar eṉṟu veḷippaṭiyākac collikkoḷḷum pōlic civarkaḷ, mēlē maṉu colliya eḻuvakic cūttiraril enta vakiyiṟ cērntavarkaḷō ataṉi avarkaḷē eṭuttuc collak kaṭavar. avvēḻuvakiyil avarkaḷ etiṟcērntavarā yiruntālum irukkaṭṭum avarkaḷ tammic cūttirar eṉṟu tāmē oppukkoṇṭa maṭṭil avarkaḷ pārppaṉar vīṭṭuk kaṭivāyiliṟ kāttiruntu aṭimivēli ceyyum ūḻiyakkārarē yāvarallāmal, uyarnta cātiyārātal yāṅṅaṉam?
aṟṟaṉ, `yāṅkaḷ kolipulitavirntu civavaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟi uḻavum vāṇikamum naṭāttic celvāḻkki yuṭiyēmāy iruttaliṉ emmi maṟṟic cūttira vakuppār ellārōṭum cērttal ākātu, yām cūttirariṟ ciṟanta caṟcūttirarē āvēmeṉiṉ; mēleṭuttukkāṭṭiya maṉu mutalāṉa paḻiya miruti nūlkaḷiṟ cūttiraril oruvakiyē collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟa tallāmaṟ, caṟcūttirar eṉṉum maṟṟoruvaki avaṟṟuḷ yāṇṭum collappaṭavilli; cūttirar eṉpōr pārppaṉar aracar vaṇikar eṉṉum ēṉi mūṉṟu  vakuppiṉarkkum aṭimikaḷāy ūḻiyam oṉṟē ceytaṟkuriya rallāmal, uḻavu vāṇikam naṭāttic celvan tokukkavāvatu, kalvi kaṟkavāvatu, civavaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟavāvatu urimi vāyntavarkaḷ allar. ātalāṟ `caṟcūttirar eṉṉuñ colvaḻakkup paḻiya vaṭamoḻi miruti nūlkaḷukku muṟṟum māṟāvatoṉṟā meṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka.
aṟṟēṟ, `caṟcūttirar eṉṉum peyarum avarkkuriya kaṭamikaḷum civākamaṅkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉavē yeṉiṉ; `civākamaṅkaḷ eṉṉumpeyarāl ippōtu naṭipeṟum cila vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷ, kōyilil vaḻipāṭu āṟṟum muṟikaḷiyuṅ kōyil amikkum vakikaḷiyun terittaṟporuṭṭu irunūṟu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷākak kōyiṟ kurukkaṇmārkaḷāl eḻutivikkappaṭṭaṉa vākum. kōyilvaḻipāṭukaḷum kōyiṟkurukkaṇmār piḻippum celvarkaḷāyuḷḷa civavēḷāḷarkaḷiṉ poruḷutaviyik koṇṭē naṭipeṟa vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, avarkaḷic cūttiravakuppā rellārōṭum potuvākac cērttuk kūṟutaṟku, añciyē, annūlkaḷi eḻutiya ak kurukkaṇmārkaḷ avarkaḷic ciṟitu uyarttivittaṟkākac `caṟcūttirar eṉṉuñ collip putitākappaṭittu avaṟṟuḷ nuḻippārāyiṉar. civapirāṉ ṟirukkōyiṟ kurukkaṇ mārkaḷ uṇmiyiṟ civavēḷāḷa marapiṉic cērntavar kaḷāyiruntum, tammi ammarapiṟ kuriyavarkaḷākac collik koḷvatu iḻiveṉa niṉintu, vaṭakkiruntuvantu kuṭiyēṟiya āriyappārppaṉa iṉattil tammiyum mella mellac cērttukkoḷvāṉ viḻintu, tammavarāṉa civavēḷāḷaric cūttirareṉavun tammip pirāmaṇar eṉavum vēṟupakuttu, aṅṅaṉam pakuppiṉun tamatu piḻippukkup palavakiyil utaviyāḷarāy niṟkum civavēḷāḷari āriyappārppaṉar ceytavāṟu pōl tāmum muḻuc cūttiravakuppiṟ cērttuviṭutaṟku iyalāmal, avarkaḷic ciṟitu uyarttic `caṟcūttirar eṉṉum oru putuppeyarippaṭittu ataṉit tām civākamap peyarāl iyaṟṟiya annūlkaḷil eḻutivippārāyiṉar. `caṟcūttirar eṉṉum ip peyarvaḻakku, ellārum oppuk koṇṭa paḻiya vaṭanūlkaḷil eṅkuṅ kāṇap paṭāmiyiṉ itaṉi āriyappārppaṉar evarum taḻuvik kūṟukiṉṟilar; iṉṉōraṉṉa putuvaḻakkukaḷ viravik kāṇappaṭum ivvākama nūlkaḷippaṟṟiya kuṟippum paḻiya vaṭa nūlkaḷil eṅkuṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ, vaṭanūl ārāycciyuṭiyār evarum ivvākama nūlkaḷi eṅkum mēṟkōḷākavum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟilar. aṟṟēl, vētānta cūttirattiṟkuc civa pāṭiyam eḻutiya nīlakaṇṭa civācāriyār yām vēta civākamaṅkaḷukku vēṟṟumi kāṇkiṉṟilam eṉat tamaturiyuḷ uritta teṉṉi yeṉiṉ; avarāṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa civākamaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa vaṭamoḻi yiluḷḷa upaniṭataṅkaḷippōṟ kaṭavuḷ uyir malam (patipacupācam) eṉṉum muttiṟap poruḷiyalpukaḷ terittu vīṭupēṟu payakkum uṇarviṉit tōṟṟuvikkum vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi aṟivunūlkaḷēyām. tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ uḷḷa tirumantiram ap peṟṟittāṉa toru viḻumiya civākama nūlākum. it tirumantirampōṉṟa civākama nūlkaḷ pala tamiḻilum vaṭamoḻiyilum ak kālattiruntamiyiṉ avi tammiyē nīlakaṇṭa civācāriyār tamatu pāṭiyavuriyiṟ kuṟippārāyiṉareṉka. paṇṭināḷilirunta tamiḻ vaṭamoḻic civākama nūlkaḷippaṟṟiya ārāycciyiṉi ematu māṇikka vācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum nūluḷ virittukkāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēmākaliṉ ataṉi aṅkē kaṇṭukoḷka; īṇṭurippiṟ perukum.
iṉi, it teṉṟamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa civa vēḷāḷarkaḷ uṇmiyil āriyappārppaṉarkku mēlāṉa vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷiyuṭiya uyarnta cātiyārāyiruntum, tamakku uriya aruḷ oḻukkattiyum kalvippayiṟciyiyum ārāycciyuṇar viṉiyum aṟavē kiviṭṭu, iṅkē piḻikkavanta avāriyap pārppaṉar kaṭṭic colliya poyk katikaḷiṟ cikkuṇṭu, tamakkuḷḷa celvatti yellām avarkaṭkum avarkaḷ kāṭṭiya ulakap poymmāya iṉpaṅkaṭkum aḷaviṉṟic celaviṭṭut, tamakkup palavakiyāṉa payaṉpaṭuṅ kittoḻilkaḷic ceytu koṭuttut tammic celvattiṟ puraḷavitta patiṉeṇ toḻilpuriyum arumit tamiḻmakkaḷiyellān tammiṉun tāḻntacātiyārāka iḻipupaṭuttit, tammi avarellāriṉum uyarnta cātiyārākak karuti iṟumāntu, avvāriyap pārppaṉari maṭṭun tammiṉum mēlāṉa cātiyārākap piḻipaṭa niṉintu civa vēḷāḷarkaḷ tam muṉṉōratu aruḷ oḻukkattiṟku muṟṟum māṟāka naṭakkappukunta kālantoṭṭē iḻinta cūttirarāyiṉār kaṇṭīr! tamakku utaviceyyun toḻilāḷarit tām iḻinta cātiyārāka niṉintu tarukki avarkaṭkup pala koṭumikaḷic ceytamiyā laṉṟō civa vēḷāḷarākiya tāmum āriyap pārppaṉarāl iḻipu paṭuttappaṭṭu iḻinta cūttirarāyiṉār!
piṟarkkiṉṉā muṟpakaṟ ceyyiṟ ṟamakkiṉṉā
piṟpakaṟ ṟāmē varum
eṉṉum poyyāmoḻi orukālum poypaṭātaṉṟō!
patiṉeṇvakit toḻilkaḷic ceytu taruvārāṉa kikkōḷar, taccar, kollar, kammāḷar, taṭṭār, kaṉṉār, cekkār, maruttuvar, kuyavar, vaṇṇār, tuṉṉar, ōviyar, pāṇar, kūttar, nāvitar, caṅkaṟuppār, pākar, paṟiyar eṉpōr tattan toḻilkaḷic cevvaṉē ceytu taruta lālaṉṟō vēḷāḷarum aracarum antaṇarum iṉituyir vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta ik kittoḻilkaḷic ceytu tantu nām tuṉpamiṉṟi iṉpamāy naṉkuvāḻa utavipuriyum aruntamiḻ makkaḷākiya nam uṭaṉpiṟappiṉariyum avar iyaṟṟum at toḻilkaḷiyum iḻipupaṭutti niṉittaluṅ kūṟutalun teyvattiṟku aṭukkumā? ivarkaḷ tāntāñ ceyyun toḻilkaḷic ceyyātu kiviṭṭu vaṟitēyiruntāl, tammic civa vēḷāḷar eṉṟum, kṣattiriya reṉṟum pirāmaṇa reṉṟuñ colli iṟumāntu tirivōrtam iṟumāppu evvāṟākum! ivar tam cātiyuyarvellām pañcāyp paṟantupōmaṉṟō! irukkuvēta kālattil oru kuṭumpattārilēyē paṟpala toḻilkaḷic ceyvārirun taṉareṉpatu naṉku kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. irukkuvēta oṉpatām maṇṭilattil (112, 3)
