mēlē nāṉkām iyalil, uḻavutoḻiliyaṟinta vēḷāḷarkaḷ attoḻilāṟ pala uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷip perukki, ataṟkumuṉ tām uṇṭuvanta iṟicciyuṇavi viṭṭuc civavuṇavē koḷḷat talippaṭamiyiṉiyum, atumutal tammil ūṉuṇavu koḷvārāṉa ēṉi yōrit tammiṉ iḻintōrāka vilakkivittut tām avariṉ uyarntōrāṉamiyiṉiyum, aṅṅaṉañ civarāṉa  tammuḷḷuṅ kalviyiluṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum akan tūymi puṟantūymiyilum mēmpaṭṭu niṉṟōr `antaṇar, `pārppaṉar eṉa mēṟkulattavar āṉamiyiṉiyum, anneṟiyil iyaṉṟamaṭṭum niṉṟu kuṭikaḷip pātu kāttalilum, celvam perukki uḻavutoḻili naṭappittalilum, uḻavutoḻilāṟ peṟṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuviṟṟalilum niṉṟōr `aracar, `vaṇikar eṉa vēṟiraṇṭu iṉaṅkaḷāyp pirintamiyiṉiyum, im muttiṟattārkkum iṉṟiyamiyāta patiṉeṇ ṭoḻil kaḷiyum ēvaṟṟoḻiliyum purintōr `aṭiyōr, `viṉivalar eṉṉuñ cūttirarāṉamiyiṉiyum, in nāṟperum piriviṉarilun tattam makaḷiratu oḻukkattāl mēlum paṟpala pirivukaḷ uṇṭāṉamiyiṉiyum virittu viḷakkippōntām. eṉavē, itteṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṭ cātivēṟṟumikaḷip piṟappittaṟku aṭippaṭi mūlaṅkaḷāy niṉṟavi: ūṉ uṇṇāmi, kalvi, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu, akantūymi, puṟantūymi, makaḷiratu oḻukkam eṉpavi kaḷēyāmeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟa taṉṟō? cāti vēṟṟumikaḷip palkuvittaṟku ētuvāy niṉṟa im mūlaṅkaḷikkoṇṭē cātivēṟṟumikaḷi oḻittaṟku vaḻitēṭal vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka. aḵtu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, aṭivē viḷakkikkāṭṭutum.
mutalāvatu `pulāluṇṇā oḻukkamē taṉṉik kikkoṇṭu oḻukiya makkaḷi uyarnta cātiyārāka ākkiṉamiyāṟ, pulāl uṇṇātavar evarāyiruppiṉum avari `antaṇar, `pārppaṉar, `civavēḷāḷar eṉṟu kūṟutalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam pulāl uṇṇā oḻukkattik kikkoṇṭōri, avar piṟanta iḻikulap peyarāl aḻittalum, muṉṉarē civarāy uḷḷār avarit tammuṭaṉ cērttu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceyyāmal avari avvaḻikulattilēyē irukkaviṭutalum perun tīviṉiyām. eṅṅaṉameṉil, ūṉ uṇpār oruvar avvūṉ uṇavu koḷḷum peruntīviṉip paṭukuḻiyi ṉiṉṟum mēlēṟuvarāyiṉ, ūṉuṇṇā mēl niliyil niṟpōr avarik kikoṭuttut tūkkit tammōṭu uṭaṉ cērttuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum; avvāṟu ceyyiṉ aṅṅaṉañ cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭōr kollā aṟattil vaḻuvātu niṟpar; ataṉāl ettaṉiyō mīṉkaḷ, kōḻikaḷ, āṭukaḷ, māṭukaḷ mutaliya uyirkaḷ uyir piḻikkum! avvāṟu am mēlōr avari mēlē tūkki viṭāmal appāḻṅ kuḻiyilēyē avari mīṇṭum viḻa aḻuttuvarāyiṉ, mēlnilikku varuvōrik keṭutta perun tīviṉikkum, ūṉ uṇpār kūṭṭattil mīṇṭuñ cērppikkappaṭṭa avar mīṇṭum mīṉ mutalāṉa uyirkaḷiṉ iṟicciyi uṇavu koḷḷavēṇṭi varutalāl ataṉāl uṇṭākum avvuyirkaḷiṉ koli viṉikkum ammēlōr uḷḷākic ciṟitu kālattil tāmun tammiṉattavarum ippiṟaviyilēṉum allatu varumpiṟavi yilēṉum puliyarāvar. aṟṟaṉṟu, yāṅkaḷ paṇṭu toṭṭup pulāl uṇṇāk kollā aṟattil vaḻuvātu niṟṟalāṟ, putitākap pulāl uṇavu nīkki varuvāri emmuṭaṉ cērttal ākātu eṉṟu uripparāyiṉ, avaruri aruḷoḻukkattiṟkumāṟāṉa tīviṉi uriyēyākum. uyirk koliyum, ataṉāl varum ūṉuṇavum nīkki aruḷoḻukkattik kikkoṇṭavar, aṅṅaṉamē kolipuli nīkkivaruvārit tammuṭaṉ cērttu, ūṉuṇṇār tokiyip perukkivantā laṉṟi ulakiṟ `kollā aṟam paravamāṭṭātu; kollā aṟam paravāta variyil, eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷ kollappaṭutalāl varuntu  viṉi civariyuñ cārātu oḻiyātu. ūṉiṉ poruṭṭut tām uyirkaḷik kollāviṭiṉum, ataṟkākak kolvārit taṭiceyyāmaluṅ, kolipuli tavirntu varuvārit tammuṭaṉ cērāmalum viṭutalāṟ, koli puli mēṉmēṟ perukutaṟkut tāmum ētuvāyiruttal paṟṟic, civarkaḷukkum ak kolit tīviṉiyiṟ perumpaṅku uṇṭu. civarākiya tammik koṭiyār cilar kollavarukiyil, aṅṅaṉaṅ kollavaruvārip piṟar vilakkātiruntāl ac civaratu nili eppaṭippaṭṭatāy irukkum! vilakkātiruntāri accivar kuṟṟañ collā tirupparō! aṅṅaṉamē uyirkkoliyi vilakkāta civarkkum peruntīviṉiyiṟ paṅku uṇṭeṉṟu aṟintu koḷka. uyirkkoliyit taṭiceyyāmiyāl varun tīviṉi peritāyiṉāṟpōlavē, ataṉit taṭiceytu koli puli tavirntārit tammuṭaṉ cērttuk koḷvatāṟ civarkku varum naṉmiyum aḷappilatākum; putiya civarkaḷit tammuṭaṉ cērttalāṟ civarkaḷiṉ toki mēṉmēṟperukum; iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa eṇṇiṟanta ciṟṟuyirkaḷ kattiyāl veṭṭappaṭṭut tuṭituṭikkum peruntuṉpattiṉiṉṟu tappippiḻikkum; nāṭeṅkum aruḷoḻukkam paravum; atu paravavē, poyyum, puṉicuruṭṭum, oruvar maṟṟoruvarkkut tīṅkiḻikkum eṇṇiṟanta pollāvaḻikaḷun toliyum. iṅṅaṉa mellāṅ, koli puli tavirntuvaruvāric civarkaḷ tammuṭaṉ cērttukkoḷvatāṟ pōtarum naṉmikaḷic collappukuntāl, avi aḷavilavāy viriyum. aṅṅaṉamē, putitu civarāvāric cērttuk koḷḷāmiyāṟ kiḷikkun tīṅkukaḷuñ collukku aṭaṅkā. ākiyāl, nāṉ peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivviyakam (tirumantiram, 95) eṉṟu civacittānta mutalāciriyarāṉa tirumūlanāyaṉār aruḷicceytirukkum viḻumiya aṟavuriyik kaṭippiṭit toḻukavēṇṭiya varāṉa civarkaḷ, tām kikkoṇṭa cīriya civa voḻukkattip piṟaruṅ kaṭip piṭikkumāṟu ceytu, aṅṅaṉam ataṉip putitu kaṭippiṭittārit tammōṭu uṭaṉvittu aḷavaḷāvutalē iṉṟiyamiyātu ceyaṟ pālatām; avvāṟu ceyyārāyiṉ tam āciriyar kaṟpitta colvaḻi naṭavāta peruṅ kuṟṟattiṟku āḷākic, civa voḻukkattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta aruḷirakkam illāmiyiṉ taṅkulamum varavara māyntu oḻiyappeṟuvar.
tamatu civa oḻukkattip parapputaliṟ karuttillā malum, putitu civavoḻukkam pūṇṭāriyum piṟariyun tammāṭṭu aṇukaviṭāmalun tamatu ūṉuṭampiṉ piṟappiyē mēlatākak karuti iṟumāntoḻukum pōlicciva vakuppiṉariṉ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ kālvaḻiyiṉṟi nāḷukkunāṭ kuṉṟi māyntupōtalip pala iṭaṅkaḷil em kaṇṇetirē kaṇṭu varukiṉṟēm, avvakuppiṉariṟ paṟpalar ataṉic collakkēṭṭum varukiṉṟōm. itaṟku ētu eṉṉeṉṟāl, tampōṉṟa ēṉi makkaḷiṉ naṉmiyik karutāmiyum, putu irattak kalappu iṉmiyu mēyām. ovvoru pōlic civavakuppiṉarum nāṅkaḷ muppatu vīṭṭukkārarkaḷ; eṅkaḷukkuḷḷētāṉ uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceykiṉṟōm; inta muppatu vīṭukaḷit tāṇṭi vēṟoru vīṭṭiṟ kiyikkūṭa naṉikka māṭṭōm eṉṟu mikunta taliyeṭuppōṭum pēcukiṉṟārkaḷ. ippaṭit tammiṟ cilarkkuḷ maṭṭum uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceyvatāl, tamakkāvatu piṟarkkāvatu ivarkaḷ ceyta naṉmiyātu? ataṉāl ivarkaḷ tēvarkaḷāy viṭṭārkaḷā? allatu ivarkaḷiṉ uṭampu evarkkum illāta poṉṉuṭampu āyviṭṭatā? allatu aṟiviṟ periyavarākik kaṭavuḷi nērē kaṇṭu viṭṭārkaḷā? appaṭiyoṉṟum illiyē. maṟṟi ellāriyum pōlc cilanēraṅkaḷil maṟṟi yōriviṭap pōliccivarkaḷ mika iḻinta ceyalkaḷiyuñ ceyyak kāṇkiṉṟōmē. tammic civareṉak karuti iṟumāp pōril ettaṉi pēr potumakaḷir vīṭṭiṟ pulit toḻil ceytu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr cīmic cārāyamum vaṟutta āṭṭiṟicciyuṅ kōḻi muṭṭiyum maṟivil uṭkoḷkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr cūtāṭṭat tiṟku kālaṅ kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr piṟar poruḷi ēmāṟṟip paṟikka maṉṟip paṭupoypēci aḻivaḻākkāṭu kiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉi pēr tamakku ākātavarkaḷi āḷviṭṭu aṭittuk kolkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr nōyvāyp paṭṭup puḻutta aḻuki nāṟik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr kalviyaṟivu ciṟitum illātavarkaḷāy aṟiyāmic cēṟṟil amiḻntik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉipēr kaṭavuḷ illiyeṉa `nāttikam pēci nāttaḻumpēṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉar! ettaṉi pēr ecakki, māri, vīraṉ, kaṟuppaṉ mutalāṉa pēykaḷi vaṇaṅki avaṟṟiṟku eṇṇiṟanta āṭu, kōḻikaḷi veṭṭukiṉṟaṉar! iṅṅaṉamē iṉṉum pōlic civarkaḷiṉ iṭiyē naṭipeṟum aṭāta ceyalkaḷiyellām eṭutturikkappaṭiṉ ivvēṭu iṭaṅkāṇātu. ittakiyayōrāṉa pōliccivar tammittāmē uyarnta cātiyārākac collikkoṇṭu tammil iṟumāppuṟṟut, tāntāmē kūṭikkaḷittāl, ataṉik kaṇṭa uyarnta aṟiviṉar avariyum avar colliyum oruporuṭṭāka matipparō! `civar eṉṉuñ collukku ēṟpat tām aruḷoḻukkattilum, civanēyattilum, aṭiyār paṇiyilun taliciṟantu niṟpatōṭu, tammōṭotta ēṉi makkaḷiyun tamatu aruḷoḻukkat tiṟṟiruppi avarōṭu aḷavaḷāvi, avvāṟṟāṟ civavoḻukkatti eṅkum paravac ceyyiṉaṉṟō, aṟivāṉmikka āṉṟōr avari uṇmic civareṉa uyarttup pārāṭṭuvar!
mēlum, muppatu nāṟpatu nūṟu irunūṟu vīṭṭuk kārarkaḷukkuḷḷētāṉ yāṅkaḷ uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceyvōm eṉat tarukkip pēci avvāṟu naṭantuvarum pōliccivariṉ kuṭumpattāruṭampukaḷiṟ paḻiya irattamē varavara valiviḻantu ōṭivarutalāl, avarkaṭkup piḷḷippēṟu vāyāmaṟ pōkiṉṟatu; piḷḷippēṟu vāyppiṉum uyarnta aṟivum, tiṭpamāṉa maṉamum, muṟukkāṉa yākkiyum, ciṟanta taṉmiyum vāynta piḷḷikaḷip peṟutal avarkaḷuḷ aritiṉum aritāyp pōkiṉṟatu. putunīr varattiṉṟip paḻiya kaṭṭukkiṭit taṇṇīrēyuḷḷa oru kuḷam nāṟṟameṭuttu, nōyppuḻukkaḷi yuṇṭākki yārkkum payaṉpaṭāmal, varavara vaṟṟivaṟaṇṭu muṭivil nīraṟṟuppōtal pōla, akaluḷḷa kuṭikaḷil aṟiñarāy valiyarāy nallarāyp piṟantār tam putiya irattamāṉatu, tamatu paḻaṅkuṭip piṟantārtam uṭampukaḷil vantu kalattaṟku iṭaṅkoṭāta pōliccivak kuṭumpattiṉarun tamatu valiviḻanta paḻiya irattaṅkeṭṭu akattum puṟattum nōykaḷukku iriyāki vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu pōkat tāmum irunta iṭan teriyāmaṟ cilakālattil māyntupōkiṉṟaṉar.
pārmiṉkaḷ! nam intiyanāṭṭavar allāta veḷḷikkāraruḷḷum avarōṭottār piṟaruḷḷum ittakiya pollāp pōlic cātik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavā? illiyē; avi illāmiyiṉ, avarkaḷ tammil āṇmakkaḷum peṇmakkaḷum tattam iyaṟkikku icintāruṭaṉ iṉitukūṭi vāḻa  iṭantantu, avvāṟṟāl aṟivilum āṇmiyiluñ ciṟanta makkaḷi aḷaviṉṟip peṟṟu, ulakameṅkun tāmākavē paravi, ellā nalaṅkaḷum eyti, annalaṅkaḷ illāta nam nāṭṭavarkaḷit tam aṭikkīḻppaṭuttu, nammaṉōr tammic cārntu aṇṭip piḻikka nammi āṇṭu namakku utaviyum purintuvarukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟirukka, aḷaviṟanta cātik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷuṭiya nammaṉōr, ac cātikkaṭṭup pāṭukaḷāṟ peṟṟa payaṉ ētēṉum uṇṭā? ētumēyilli. avaṟṟāl uyarnta payaṉkaḷ uṇṭākāviṭiṉum viṭuka; tīmikaḷēṉum viḷiyāmalirukkiṉṟaṉavā veṉṟāl appaṭiyumilli; avi naṉmiyit tarātamaṭṭil amiyāmal, aṭuttaṭuttup pala tīmikaḷiyum viḷi vikkiṉṟaṉa! tammi uyarntacātiyārāka niṉippavarkaḷ tammōṭotta makkaḷi evvaḷavu iḻivāka niṉittu iṟumākkiṉṟārkaḷ! iḻintōrāka otukkappaṭṭavarkaḷ evvaḷavu tuṉpatti aṭintālum avarkaḷi uyarnta cātiyār eṉpōr kaṇṇeṭuttum pārkkiṉṟār kaḷilliyē! tāḻnta cātiyāriṭattil ellāvakiyāṉa ēvaṟṟoḻili yum vāṅkikkoṇṭu avvāṟṟāl mikka celvamuṭiyarāy vāḻ kiṉṟavarkaḷ, avvēḻi ēvalar orunāḷikku oruvēḷi kūḻuṇavu kūṭak kiṭiyāmaṟ kaḷittukkiṭakka, avarkaṭku orupiṭicōṟāvatu koṭuk kiṉṟārkaḷā, illiyē! iḻinta cātiyār kaṭuveyilil nāḷ muḻutum niṉṟu ki eriyak kāl ōya mutukunōva veṭṭiyeṭuttut tanta kiṇaṟṟunīrit tamatu vīṭṭu niḻaliliruntu veṭṭivēr iṭṭu maṇam ūṭṭip parukukiṉṟa mēṟcātiyār, ak kīḻccātiyārkaḷ veyilāl nāvaṟaṇṭu uṭaltaḷarntu oru kuṭaṅki nīrāvatu mukantu kuṭikkalām eṉṟu akkiṇaṟ ṟaṇṭi vantāl avarkaḷi evvaḷavu vaṉṉeñcattōṭun turattukiṉṟaṉar!
mēṟcātiyāreṉpōrāvatu tamakkuḷ aṉpum oṟṟumiyum pūṇṭu oḻukukiṉṟārkaḷāveṉṟāl appaṭiyum illi. avarkaḷḷum paṟpala cātivēṟṟumikaḷ! oruciṟu vakuppiṉar maṟṟoru ciṟuvakup piṉarit tammiṟ ṟāḻntavar eṉak karuti avarōṭu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceyyāmal iṟumākkiṉṟaṉar! ennēramuñ cātiyuyarvu tāḻvukaḷip paṟṟiyē pēcukiṉṟaṉarallāmal, vēṟu uyarnta poruḷ kaḷippaṟṟi eṭṭuṇiyum pēcukiṉṟivar. iṉṉakālattil aḻiyum eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭāta ūṭaṉumpiṉ piṟappiyē ennēramum niṉintu aṟiyāmiyil māḻkkiṭakkum ip pētimakkaṭku uyarnta nōkkaṅkaḷum uyarnta poruḷ kaḷippaṟṟiya pēccukkaḷum eṅkē yiruntu eppaṭivarum? iv intumakkaḷippōla oṟṟumi keṭṭa nellikkāy mūṭṭikaḷi innilavulakattil vēṟeṅkuṅ kāṇṭal iyalātu. tamakkuḷ oruvari yoruvar tāḻvāka niṉintu, pōli mēlōr pōlik kīḻōrik koṭumiyāka naṭattutalukkut taṇṭaṉiyāka vaṉṟō, ellārkkun tantiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ nam intu makkaḷic ceṉṟa eḻunūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukkumēlāka ayal nāṭṭavar paṭiyeṭuppiṟ paṭuttut tuṉpuṟuttiyum, avaratu āṭciyiṉ kīḻ vittut tiruttiyum varukiṉṟatu. taṉ putalvarkaḷuḷ eḷiyāri valiyār koṭumip paṭuttutal kaṇṭu, pērirakka muṭiyaṉākiya iṟivaṉ, nam intumakkaḷi ayalavarāl evvaḷavō tirutti vantum, nammavarkaṭku iṉṉum nalluṇarvum aṉpum oṟṟumiyum vanta pāṭilliyē! eḻunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟṟoṭṭut tulukkarkaḷ paṭiyeṭuttuvantu nammaṉōric ciṉṉa piṉṉamāy veṭṭi vīḻttiyum, poruḷkaḷic cūṟiyāṭiyum, peṇmakkaḷik kaṟpaḻittum, ūrkaḷik koḷuttiyum, tirukkōyilkaḷi iṭittum varutti vantakkāl nammaṉōrtam cātivēṟṟumikaḷ eṅkiruntaṉa? avi ellām pañcāyp paṟantoḻintaṉavallavō? `timūr amīr eṉṉun tulukkamaṉṉaṉ kipi 1398 il vaṭanāṭṭiṟ paṭiyeṭuttu vanta ñāṉṟu `tulampā eṉṉum ūrilirunta nam intuk kuṭimakkaḷic ciṉṉapiṉṉamāy veṭṭi vīḻtti, eñciṉōri aṭimippaṭuttiṉāṉ! piṉṉum orukāl nammaṉōril nūṟāyiram pērik kurutiyoḻukak koṉṟāṉ; ṭilli nakari aintunāḷ variyiṟ koḷḷiyiṭṭāṉ; appōtu eṇṇiṟanta nam peṇmakkaḷic ciṟi piṭittāṉ1. ak kālammutal nammaṉōril ettaṉikōṭi pēr tulukkarāyiṉar! ettaṉi yāyirampēr vaṟumippaṭṭuk kīḻccātiyārā yiṉar! aṭittavaṉukku āmuṭiyāḷ, piṭittavaṉukkup peṇṭāṭṭi eṉṟapaṭi nam peṇmakkaḷil ettaṉi yāyirampēr tulukkar kiyiṟ cikkimīṇṭavarkaḷ! attulukka aracarkaḷālnērnta kuḻappaṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyē, nam intu aracarkaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ kīḻt talimi celuttiṉavarkaḷum, ēṉic celvarkaḷum, āḷvalimi paṭittavarkaḷum ceytuvanta takutiyaṟṟa koṭuñ ceyalkaḷuñ collukku aṭaṅkā; avarkaḷāl tuṉpuṟṟut tamatu niṟi citinta  nam intup peṇmakkaḷum eṇṇiṟantavarkaḷ! aṟam vaḻuvāta nam āṅkilavēntariṉ āṭci innāṭṭil nilipeṟṟapiṉṉaraṉṟō, nāmum nam peṇmakkaḷum accamiṉṟi iṉitu vāḻa iṭampeṟṟōm. intac cīriya araciyaliluṅkūṭa, otuṅkiyuḷḷa nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil iṭam poruḷ ēvalkaḷāl valiyarāy uḷḷār tammic cūḻntirukkuṅ kuṭimakkaṭkup perun tīṅkukaḷ ceytuvarukiṉṟārkaḷ; at tīyār irukkum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṟpuṭiya makaḷirum irupparō eṉpatu aiyuṟaṟ pālatākavē yirukkiṉṟatu. cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ maliyāḷa nāṭṭil nikaḻnta koṭiyanikaḻccikaḷ iṉṉum namatu niṉiviviṭṭu nīṅkiṟṟila, aṅkuḷḷa nampūrip pārppaṉarum nāyarkaḷum nīḷa iḻittuvanta cātivēṟṟumik koṭumikaḷip poṟukka ēlāmiyālaṉṟō, aṅkuḷḷa tulukkarkaḷ avarkaḷi uyirōṭuvittut tōlurittum, avarkaḷiṉ makaḷiric ciṟipiṭittuc ceṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷit tīyiṭṭuk koḷuttiyum, avarkaḷ toḻuṅ kōyilkaḷi iṭittup pāḻākkiyum niṉippiṉum neñcamnaṭuṅkum vakiyā lellām avarkaḷit tuṉpuṟuttiṉārkaḷ! aṅkuḷḷa nampūrikaḷum nāyarkaḷum tammōṭotta makkaḷāṉa `tīyar, `īḻuvar, `puliyar mutaliyōrik koṭumippaṭuttāmal aṉpāka naṭattiyiruntāṟ, peruntokiyiṉarāṉa avarkaḷellārum oruṅkucērntu, ciṟutokiyiṉarāṉa  nampūrikaḷiyum nāyarkaḷiyum pātukāttut, tulukkarkaḷi eḷitil aṭakkiyirupparaṉṟō! nam āṅkila araciṉar tam paṭikaḷi viṭuttu at tulukkarkaḷi aṭakkiyirā viṭṭāl, nampūri nāyarkaḷiṉ cātikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷellān tukaḷāyp piṟantu, maliyāḷam muṟṟun tulukkanāṭāyp pōyirukku maṉṟō? ivvintiya nāṭeṅkum pārppaṉa rālum pōliccivarkaḷālum vērūṉṟi niṟkum cātivēṟṟumiyāl, ellā makkaḷum eṇṇiyaṭaṅkāp paṟpala piriviṉarāki oṟṟumikeṭṭu, oruvariṭattoruvar aṉpilarāyt, tammittāmē periyarākaniṉintu iṟumāntu naṭakkiṉṟaṉar; ivarkaḷit tulukkarkaḷ maṟṟorumuṟi etirkka nēram vāykkumāṉāl, intukkaḷ aṉivarum vērōṭu māykkappaṭutal tiṇṇamāy muṭiyum. cila kiḻamikaḷukkumuṉ kalkattāvil naṭanta `intu mulīm kalakamē itaṟkoru cāṉṟām. araciyal neṟivaḻukuvāta nam āṅkila turittaṉattār ik kalakatti uṭaṉē aṭakkātiruntāl, ikkalakam evvaḷavu añcattakkatāy muṭintirukkum. cātivēṟṟumi eṉṉum perum puyaṟkāṟṟāṟ ciṉṉa piṉṉap paṭuttappaṭṭuc citaṟik kiṭakkum nam intumakkaḷil, talivareṉat tammik kūṟikkoḷvōr in nilimikaḷic ciṟitāyiṉum ārāyntupārāmal, nam āṅkila araci innāṭṭi ṉiṉṟun turattappārk kiṉṟārkaḷē!
 mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpuṅ
  kāvalaṉ kāval iṉṟit taṅkā
eṉṟu nam paḻiya āṉṟōrākiya iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ `cilappatikārattil aruḷicceytirukkum aṟavuriyi avarkaḷ aṟiyār kollō! āṅkila araciṉar innāṭṭiviṭṭu akalum aṉṟikkē itu piṇak kāṭām eṉpati avarkaḷ uṇarārkollō! nam intukkaḷukku ellāvakiyāṉun tīrāp peruma pakivarāyt, tammuḷ oṟṟumiyum ataṉāl valimiyum tamakku utavipuriyun tulukka aracarkaḷum naṉku vāykkap peṟṟārāy, ivvintu nāṭeṅkaṇum peruntoki yiṉarāyk kuṭikoṇṭirukkun tulukkarkaḷip pārttāyiṉum nammavarkaḷ cātivēṟṟumi yiṉi yoḻittut tamakkuḷ oṟṟumi eytalākātā! ōr eḻunūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka ivvintiyanāṭeṅkum nikaḻntuvarum māṟutalkaḷi naṭuvunilimiyōṭu niṉṟu ārāyntu kāṇpavarkaḷukku, iṅkuḷḷa entac cātiyārēṉum piṟacātik kalappiṉṟit taṉiccātiyārāy vāḻntirukkalām eṉṟu aiyamiṉṟic colvataṟkuc ciṟitum iṭam illāmiyālum, vevvēṟiṉamāy niṟkum makkaḷ oṉṟu kalattalāṟ piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷ aṟivu mikutiyum uṭal valimiyum peṟṟu ulakiṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭutali nāḷuṅ kāṇkiṉṟō mākaliṟ cātikkalappu mikavum vēṇṭaṟ pālatāyt tōṉṟalālum, immi maṟumip payaṉkaḷi viḻiyum uṇmic civarkaḷ piṟacātiyāriliruntu pulāluṇṇāp puṉitarāy varuvārit tammoṭu cērttu valimiyilum aṟivilun tokiyilum aruḷoḻukkat tilum mikuntu nalampeṟak kaṭavarāka!
iṉi, maṟṟit tāḻnta vakuppiṉariṟ kolipuli tavirntāri uṇmic civarkaḷ tammiṉattiṟ cērttuk koḷvatu oṉṟiṉ maṭṭum amiyātu, kalviyil mikkāriyuṅ, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟ ciṟantāriyum, akan tūymi puṟantūymi vāyntāriyum piṟappiḻipu nōkkātu tammoṭucērttu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceytal vēṇṭum. ellā makkaḷiṭattum ellā nalaṅkaḷum amital aritākiyāl, evvevariṭattu evven nalaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō, avvan nalaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyē avvavarip pārāṭṭutal vēṇṭum; avariṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭāta nalaṅkaḷukkāka avarik kuṟicollal ākātu. cila nalaṅkaḷ uṭiyarāy vēṟu cila nalaṅkaḷ ilarāyiṉāriyun tammoṭu cērttu aḷavaḷāvivariṉ, avar uṇmic civariṉ cērkkiyāl varavara ellā nalaṅkaḷum nirampappeṟuvar. tamakkuḷḷēyē civa voḻukkattiṉ vaḻuvip pulāluṇavu koṇṭuṅ kuṭittum parattiyari maruviyum ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytum varuvār palar irukka, uṇmic civarkaḷ avarkaḷi nīkka māṭṭāmaṟ ciṟappunāṭkaḷil avarkaḷōṭu uṭaṉiruntu uṇṭum, avarkaḷpāṟ peṇkoṇṭu koṭuttum aḷavaḷāvi vāḻavilliyā? aṅṅaṉamē maṟṟit tāḻnta vakuppārilun tiruntattakkārāyc, cila nalaṅkaḷēṉum uṭiyār iruntāl, avarkaḷi uṇmic civarkaḷ tammiṉattil uṭaṉē cērttal iyalātāyiṉum, avarkaḷōṭu mutalil neruṅkip paḻakavēṇṭu maḷavukkup paḻaki, ap paḻakkattāl avarkaḷ muṟṟun tiruntakkāṇiṉ, piṉṉar avarkaḷit tammuṭaṉ cērttukkoḷḷal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
piṟa cātiyārkaḷ kalviyuṅ kaṭavuḷvaḻipāṭun tūymiyum ilarātalpaṟṟi, avari vilakkivittu, avarkaḷ tiruntutaṟkuriya vaḻivakikaḷic ceyyāmal nammavar iṉṉum parāmukamāyirupparāṉāl, avar kaḷellāruṅ kiṟituvar, tulukkar, puttar mutalāṉa piṟacamayattavarāl eḷitil ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭu, nam intucamayap pakivarākic, cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ivvintiya nāṭeṅkum niṟintu, nammaṉōrum nam civaviṇava camayaṅkaḷum iruntaviṭamun teriyāmal vērōṭu aṟṟuppōkac ceykuvar. nammaṉōr ceyta koṭiya cātikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷiṉ tuṉpam poṟukkamāṭṭāmi yālaṉṟō, kaṇakkiṟanta nam intumakkaḷ kiṟituva rāyviṭṭaṉar! nammaṉōril nāḷoṉṟukku munnūṟupēr viḻukkāṭu kiṟituvarāy viṭukiṉṟārkaḷeṉṟu, ataṉi ārāynta naṇpar oruvar collak kēṭṭēm. nāḷoṉṟukku munnūṟupēr kiṟituvarāyviṭṭāl, oru tiṅkaḷukku oṉpatiṉāyiram ākac cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil intiyanāṭu muḻutuṅ kiṟituvarālēyē nirappappaṭum. kiṟituvarkaḷ taṅkaḷatu camayattip parappumporuṭṭu ovvorunāḷum eṇṇiṟanta nūlkaḷip parappi varukiṉṟārkaḷ; tammiṟ kaṟṟavarāyuḷḷa kurumārkaḷukku mikunta campaḷaṅkaḷ koṭuttu, avarkaḷi āyirak kaṇakkākap palappala iṭaṅkaḷukkum pōkkit, tammuṭiya camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷi ellārkkum eṭuttuccolli, virumpiṉōrit tamatu camayattil ēṟṟukkoḷkiṉ ṟārkaḷ. nammaṉōrāṟ ciṟitēṉum pārāṭṭappaṭāmal otukki vikkappaṭṭirukkum ēḻiyeḷiya cātiyārkaḷ irukkum iṭaṅkaḷ eṅkuṅ kiṟituvak kurukkaḷmār pōy āṅkāṅkup paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ tiṟappittu, avarkaṭku ūṇkoṭuttu uṭikoṭuttuk kalviyaṟivu pukaṭṭi, avarkaḷ tuppuravāyirukkak kaṟpittu, avarkaḷic ciṟanta aluvalkaḷilum amarac ceytu iṅṅaṉamellām avarkaṭkup paṟpala naṉmikaḷip purintuvarutalāl, annaṉmikaḷip peṟṟu uyarum avveḷiya cātiyārkaḷ ak kurumārkaḷpāl mikka naṉṟiyaṟi vuṭiyavarkaḷāyt, tamakku ētōr utaviyuñ ceyyāmal tampāl ellā utavikaḷum peṟṟuk koṇṭu tammip paṭṭiṉiyilum paciyilum mikat tāḻntaniliyiluṅ kiṭakkaviṭṭut tammi mīḷāt tuṉpattiṟku āḷākkivarum nammaṉōriyum nammuṭiya civaviṇava camayaṅkaḷiyum nīṅki akkiṟituvak kurukkaḷmār kāṭṭum kiṟituvacamayam pukukiṉṟārkaḷ. eḷiya cātiyārkaṭku utavipurivār illāta kālaṅkaḷilē, mēṟcātiyāreṉak kūṟik koḷḷum ō irakkamaṟṟa koṭiya intumakkaḷē, nīṅkaḷ avarkaḷi āṭu māṭu kaḻuti kutiri paṉṟi nāy mutalāṉa vilaṅkiṉaṅ kaḷiṉuṅ kaṭippaṭṭavarāka naṭattiyum, avarkaḷukku arivayiṟṟuk kūḻuṇavukūṭak kiṭiyāmaṟ ceytum, avarkaḷil āṇ makkaḷāyiṉār kōvaṇattiṟkumēl oruciṟu kantittuṇi kūṭa uṭukka viṭāmalum, avarkaḷiṟ peṇmakkaḷāyiṉār tamatu mārpiṉi maṟittu mēlāṭi uṭuppataṟkuṅkūṭa maṉampoṟāmaṟ ciṉantum, avarkaḷ tūymiyāyirukkak kalviyaṟivutāṉum pukaṭṭāmalum nummōṭoppa iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa ammakkaḷip peruntuṉpattilum aṟiyāmiyilum irutti, avarkaḷ pāl ellāvakiyāṉa vēli kaḷiyum vāṅkivantīrkaḷ! nuṅkaḷuṭiya īramaṟṟa vaṉṉeñcattiyum irakkamaṟṟa koṭuñceyalkaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭaṉṟō, avvēḻi makkaṭku utavipuritaṟ poruṭṭu aruṭkaṭalākiya āṇṭavaṉ āṅkilanaṉ makkaḷiyum avar vaḻiyē kiṟituvak kurumārkaḷiyum innāṭṭukku varumpaṭi aruḷpurintāṉ! avarkaḷ varātiruntāl pala kōṭik kaṇakkāyuḷḷa at tāḻttappaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ nilimi iṉṉum evvaḷavu tuṉpattiṟku uḷḷāki yirukkum! ivvāṟu ellā irakkamum vāynta iṟivaṉāl ēvappaṭṭu āṅkila aracuṅ kiṟituvak kurumārum iṅkuvantu av vēḻimakkaṭkum piṟarkkum ellāvaki yāṉa nalaṅkaḷum purintuvaruvatip pārttum, aiyō! intumakkaḷē, ō pōliccivarkaḷē, iṉṉum nuṅkaṭku irakkamum nallaṟivum vantapāṭilliyē! nuṅkaḷi nuṅkaḷ kālvaḻiyaṟṟuppōka vērōṭu veṭṭi māyttu varum pollāta kōṭāriyāyc cātivēṟṟumi iruppa tuṇarātu, ataṉi numakkuc ciṟapput taruvatāka eṇṇi nīṅkaḷ makiḻvatu evvaḷavu pētimi! ūriṉ naṭuvē veṭimaruntuk kottaḷattiṉ mēliruntu koḷḷik kaṭṭiyic cuḻaṟṟi makiḻvōṉukkum nuṅkaṭkum yām vēṟṟumi kāṇkilēm. oru tīppoṟiyāṉatu akkot taḷatti veṭikkacceytu avaṉiyum, avvūriluḷḷā raṉivariyuñ ciṟitu nērattiṟ paṭucāmparākki viṭutal pōlap, pāḻuñ cātivēṟṟumiyāl iṉi yuṇṭāvataṟku mummarittu niṟkum oruciṟu kalakamāṉatu nuṅkaḷiyum nuṅkaḷiṟumāppiṉiyum nuṅkaḷ cātik kaṭṭuppāṭu kaḷiyum eḷitil māyttoḻikkumēyeṉa añcu kiṉṟōm! veḷḷam varuvataṟku muṉṉarē aṇikōlivittal aṟivuṭiyār ceyalātalpōlap, perun tīmikku ētuvāṉa kalakam varutaṟkumuṉṉamē ataṉi varuvikkuñ cātivēṟṟumi yiṉit tolittuviṭumiṉkaḷ!
āṭu māṭu kutirikaḷippōlac civavuṇavu maṭṭuṅ koḷḷutalāl nīṅkaḷ nuṅkaḷic `civar eṉac collikkoḷḷukiṉṟīrkaḷ; nīṅkaḷēṉum maruttuvar collik kēṭṭuc cilakālaṅkaḷil ūṉuṇaviyum uṭkoḷvīrkaḷ; āṉāl, āṭu māṭu kutirikaḷō ekkālattum ūṉuvukoḷḷā; kaṭavuḷ illiyeṉa nāttikam pēcum camaṇarkaḷ kollā aṟattil nuṅkaḷi viṭap paṉmaṭaṅku ciṟantavarkaḷ ūṉ uṇṇāmiyē `civam eṉa nuvaluvīrkaḷāyiṉ, āṭu māṭu kutiri kaḷiyum camaṇariyumaṉṟō nuṅkaḷiviṭac ciṟanta civarākac collutal vēṇṭum? aṟṟaṉṟu, nāṅkaḷ civaperumāṉi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟōm, tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷ ōtukiṉṟōm, tirunīṟum civamaṇiyum aṇikiṉṟōm, ainteḻuttu mantiram uruvēṟṟukiṉṟōm, aṭiyār tiruttoṇṭiṉ perumi kaḷiyum `civañāṉapōtam mutalāṉa civacittāntat taṉipperu nūlkaḷiyum payiṉṟaṟikiṉṟōm; ivikaḷē civarākiya emakkuc ciṟappaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ eṉṟu urippīr kaḷāyiṉ, iv vaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ uṭiyavar nuṅkaḷil ettaṉipēr uḷar? patiṉāyiravaril oruvarēṉum uḷarā? tuṇintu colvīr kaḷā? civaperumāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉpataṟkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi nuṅkaḷil naṉku uṇarntavar evvaḷavu pēr? tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷukkup poruḷum avaṟṟip piḻiyaṟa ōtutalun terintōr evvaḷaviṉar? tirunīṟu, civamaṇi, ainteḻuttu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ uṇmiyi yārāyntaṟintōr ettaṉipēr? `periya purāṇam, `civañāṉapōtam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷiyēṉun terintōr nuṅkaḷil āyirattil oruvarēṉum uḷarā? uṇmic civarkkuriya iv vaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷuḷ oru cila tāmum vāykkappeṟātār veṟum peyaraḷavil tammic civareṉa iṟumāppuṭaṉ collikkoḷḷā niṟkiyil, maṟṟic cātiyāriliruntu iv vaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ ellām muṟṟum uṭiyarāy varum puṉitari `uṇmic civar eṉakkoṇṭu avaruṭaṉ uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceytalāṟ pōtaruṅ kuṟṟam eṉṉi? piṟacātiyāriṟ civanalaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟōṭu peruñcelvamum uṭiyār iruntāl, avaruṭaṉ ētoru vēṟṟumiyum pārāmal uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceyyum civarkaḷip pārātār yār? piṟa cātiyārkaḷiṟ celvam mikuntōr ettaṉiyō peyar tañcātiyi viṭuttuc civa kulattil eḷitākap pukuntu kalantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. paṇam pantiyilē kulaṅ kuppiyilē eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum ivvāṟu nēruñ cātikkalappukaḷiyē uṇarttāniṟkiṉṟatu. civakulattil maṭṭumē ittakiya kalappukaḷ nērkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu niṉittal vēṇṭām. ellāc cātiyārilum ivi pōṉṟa kalappukaḷ nikaḻntukoṇṭē pōkiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu ellāk kulattiṉarilum mēṟpaṭṭa kulattiṉarākak karutappaṭum pārppaṉaruṅkūṭat, tammiṉum mikat tāḻnata varākak karutappaṭuñ cātiyāriṟ celvamuṅ kalviyum mutalāṉa nalaṅkaḷuṭiyār virumpiṉāl avarkaṭkut tam peṇ makkaḷi maṉiviyarāka vāḻkkippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷ pāṟ peṇmakkaḷiruntāl avarkaḷit tāmum maṇam muṭittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamellām piṟacātiyāriṟ celvam mikkārpāl oruvakiyākavum, celvaṅ kuṟintārpāṟ, piṟitoru vakiyākavum naṭakkum pōlic civariṉ vañcavoḻukkan tīyiṉuṅ koṭiyatā meṉka.
iṉṉum, piṟacātiyāriṟ ṟāḻntakulattavarākat tammāṟ karutappaṭṭa vakuppiṉar cilarit teṉṉāṭṭut tirukkōyil kaḷiṉuḷ nuḻiyaviṭāta pōliccivariṉ pulliya ceyki yāl, ettuṇip peruntīṅkukaḷ nikaḻavirukkiṉṟaṉavō eṉpati niṉikka emneñcam naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu! ittaṭiyāl ituvariyil nikaḻnta tīmikaṭkē aḷavilli. ellā makkaḷiyun tōṟṟuvitta iṟivaṉākiya tam appaṉ amarntirukkum potu iṭamākiya kōyiliṟ ceṉṟu makkaḷ ellārum maṉaṅ karintu vaṇaṅkavēṇṭiyavarā yirukka, appotu iṭattilum pollāta cātivēṟṟumiyi nuḻittu, iṟivaṉ paṭitta makkaḷuṭ perumpālārit tāḻnta cātiyārākki, avarkaḷik kōyiliṉuḷ nuḻiyaviṭāta koṭuñceyki yiṉuṅ koṭiyatu vēṟu yātirukkiṉṟatu! tāḻntacātiyārkaḷ kōyiliṉuṭ nuḻiyaviṭāta koṭuñceykiyiṉuṅ koṭiyatu vēṟu yātirukkiṉṟatu! tāḻntacātiyārkaḷ kōyiliṉuṭ pukuntu tammi vaṇaṅkalākāteṉṟu kaṭavuḷ taṭiceytiruk kiṉṟārā? appaṭiyoṉṟu milli yeṉpatum, cāti iṟumāppuk koṇṭōrkkuk kaṭavuḷ aruḷpuriyāmal, tāḻttap paṭṭa cātiyārkkē avar mikutiyum aruḷpurintiruk kiṉṟā reṉpatum uṇmi varalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟāl mēlē viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉa maṉṟō? allatu kaṭavuḷi nērēkaṇṭu avaṉataruḷip peṟṟa uṇmiyaṭiyār kaḷāvatu tāḻttappaṭṭa cātiyārkaḷi uḷḷē viṭutalākāteṉṟu vilakkiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷā? appaṭiyum illiyē. avarkaḷ, īḻak kulaccāṉṟārākiya ēṉāti nāyaṉāriyum, paṟik kuṭiyiṉarāṉa nantaṉāriyum, pāṇar kuṭiyiṉarāṉa tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇariyum, vaṇṇār kuṭippiṟanta tirukkuṟipput toṇṭariyum, eṇṇey viṟkum vāṇikar kuṭippiṟanta kaliyaṉāriyum vaṇaṅki avarkaḷik kōyiliṉuḷ nuḻiya iṭaṅkoṭutta maṭṭil amiyātu, avarkaḷiyellām ēṉi nāyaṉmār tiruvuruvaṅkaḷōṭu uṭaṉ vittu, aṟupattu mūvarāka ovvoru civapirāṉ tirukkōyilkaḷilum ovvoru nāḷum vaḻipāṭuceytu varukaveṉavuṅ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa rallarō? ak kaṭṭaḷippaṭiyē an nāyaṉ mārkaṭku nāḷ vaḻipāṭum āṇṭuviḻāvum ovvoru tirukkōyilkaḷiṉun toṉṟu toṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭu varutalip pōlic civarkaḷ pārttatilliyā? iḻinta cātiyārākak karutappaṭṭa nāyaṉmārkaḷ civapirāṉōṭu oppak kōyilkaṭōṟum vittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu varukiyil, annāyaṉmār marapiṟ piṟakkun tavamuṭiya cāṉṟār, paṟiyar, cempaṭavar, vaṇṇār, eṇṇey vāṇikar eṉpōrit teṉṉāṭṭuc civapirāṉ tirukkōyilkaḷil nuḻiyaviṭāta pōlic civariṉ pullaṟiviṉun taliyeṭuppiṉ koṭiyavi vēṟuṇṭō kūṟumiṉkaḷ! ivarkaḷuṭiya ik koṭumikku añci yaṉṟō tirunelvēli mākāṇat tiluḷḷa tāḻttappaṭṭa cātiyāril eṇṇiṟantōr kiṟitumatam pukuntaṉar! iṉṉum pukukiṉṟaṉar! ivvāṟu civacamayattiṟ piṟanta makkaḷellāruma nāḷukkunāṭ kiṟituvarāyviṭṭāṟ piṟaku civapirāṉ kōyilkaḷi vaṇaṅka varuvārtām yār? itutāṉum uṇarntu pārkkuñ ciṟiyatōr aṟivutāṉum iṉṟic, civacamayattavarāy irukkum variyil tāḻttap paṭṭa cātiyāri muṉṉēṟa voṭṭāmaṟ palavakiyāl īraneñcamiṉṟit tuṉpuṟuttit tāmē, avarkaḷik kiṟituvamatam pukumāṟuceytuviṭṭu, avarkaḷ kiṟituvam matam pukuntu mēlnilikku vanta piṉ, avarkaḷmuṉ aṭakkoṭukka vaṇakkattōṭu ceṉṟu, palli iḷittuk kāṭṭiyuṅ keñciyamukattōṭu talikuṉintu niṉṟum, avarkaṟpāṟ pala utavikaḷippeṟum pōliccivariṉ ceyal peritum aruvarukkattakkatā yirukkiṉṟataṉṟō? ellāc cātiyiṉariyuṅ kōyiliṉuḷ viṭuttu, avaruṭ civapirāṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ meyyaṉpu pūṇṭu oḻukiṉārkku, ak kōyilkaḷiṉuḷḷēyē tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ camittu niṟutti avari vaḻipaṭṭuvarum vaḻakkan toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭipeṟṟu varāniṟpavum, acciṟanta vaḻakkattiṟku māṟākap pōlic civarkaḷ tāḻnta vakuppiṉarik kōyil kaḷiṉuḷḷē viṭuvatilliyeṉap putiyatoru pōli vaḻakkatti ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, appōli vaḻakkattiṉ tuṇiyāl āṅkila aracarit tamakkut tuṇi kūṭṭi vittut, tāḻnta cātiyārik kōyiliṉuḷḷē nuḻiya viṭāmal, ammuyaṟciyil maṭṭum viṭāppiṭiyāy niṟkiṉṟārkaḷē! nam nāṭṭavarum, nam camayattavarum, nammuṭiya vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭum ellā utavikaḷiyuñ ceytu varupavarumāṉa paṟiyar, cāṉṟār, ēkāliyar, eṇṇey vāṇikar mutaliyōrkkuk koṭumi ceyvatākiya tīyavaḻiyiltāṉā ip pōlic civarḷukku ittuṇi muyaṟciyum viṭāppiṭiyum iruttalvēṇṭum! tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppiṉarip pōlic civarkaḷ ivvā ṟellām mika varunti avarkaḷiṉ akkoṭuñceyali aruvaruttuppēcāniṟpa, appōlikaḷ evaruṭiya matippip peṟalvēṇṭiyō av vēḻikaḷi iṅṅaṉamellāṅ koṭumippaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉar? aṟivuṭiyōr virumpātatic ceytaliṉ, appōlikaṭku immiyil ikaḻcciyē yallāmaṟ pukaḻcciyilli. iṟivaṉ tiruvuḷḷattiṟku ākātatic ceytaliṉ avarkaṭku maṟumiyilum pāvamēyallāṟ puṇṇiyamum illi. ivvāṟu immi maṟumi yiraṇṭilun tamakkat tīmiyētarum ikkoṭuñ ceyalip pōliccivar uṭaṉēnīkki, aṉivariyuñ cāti vēṟṟumi pārāmaṟ kōyiliṉuṭ pōkaviṭṭu, aṟivuṭi yōr naṉku matippiyum iṟivaṉatu aruḷiyum peṟakkaṭavarāka!
ivarkaḷ avvāṟu ceyyātoḻiyiṉ nam aruḷāḷa rāṉa āṅkila aracarē aṉivarun tirukkōyiliṉuṭ puka viṭitarumnāḷ virivilvarum. appōtu ippōlic civarkaḷ tammāl āvatoṉṟumiṉṟic ceyalaṟṟu ellārālum iḻikkappaṭṭu niṟpar. pāruṅkaḷ! maliyāḷat tiluḷḷa vikkattil tāḻnta cātiyārit teruviṟ cellaviṭōmeṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa pōli mēṟcātiyār evvaḷavu viṭāppiṭiyāy niṉṟaṉar! maliyāḷa aracar vāḷā irukkum variyil, appōli mēlōr evvaḷavu iṟumāppuṭaṉ aṟivuṭiyōr collik kēḷāmal ēḻivakuppiṉarit turattit talivirittāṭiṉar! āṉāl, ippōtu maliyāḷa araci ēṟṟa aruḷ araciyar tamatu tiruvuḷamiraṅkit tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppiṉar ellārun tamatu nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa terukkaḷellāvaṟṟilun taṭiyiṉṟic cellalām eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi piṟappittapiṉṉar ammēṟ cātip pōlikaḷ tam ceyalavintu ippōtu evvaḷavu iḻikkattakka niliyi lirukkiṉṟaṉar! ittakiya nili varutaṟku muṉṉamē avarkaḷ tāmākavē ellāriyun tāmirukkun terukkaḷiṟ cellaviṭṭirupparkaḷāṉāl, avarkaḷatu naṟceyali ulakam evvaḷavu pukaḻntu koṇṭāṭiyirukkum! tirunelvēliyiluḷḷa civarum pārppaṉarum ippōtē ellāvakuppiṉariyum uyarvu tāḻvu karutātu tirukkōyil kaḷiṉuṭ cellaviṭukuva rāyiṉ, avarkaḷatu avvirakkac ceyali ulakam mīkkūṟik koṇṭāṭum. avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉañceyyātu tamatu koṭuñceyaliṟ kāḻppēṟi niṟparāyiṉ, āṅkila araciṉarē tiruvuḷamiraṅki ellā vakuppiṉaruṅkōyiliṉ uṭcellak kaṭṭaḷiyiṭum pōtu appōliccivarum piṟarun tammāṟ ceyyalāva tiṉṟi iḻipuṟṟu ellārāṉum ēḷaṉañ ceyyappaṭṭu māḻki niṟpareṉka.
iṉi, iccātivēṟṟumikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷāl nam tamiḻmātarkaḷ āḷāki varun tuṉpaṅkaḷ mikak koṭiyaṉa vāyiruttalāl, avaṟṟi oḻittaṟkum ellāruma virivil muyalal vēṇṭum. `namakkuppaṭippum vēṇṭām, paṇamum vēṇṭām, aḻakum vēṇṭām, nallācātiyāṉāy maṭṭum iruntāṟpōtum, avaṉ ēḻiyāyiruntāluṅ kalviyillāta vaṉāyiruntāluṅ kiḻavaṉāyiruntāluṅ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟu colli aḻakiya aṟivuḷḷa nalla iḷampeṇṇi oru maṭiyaṉukkāvatu allatu oru kiḻavaṉukkāvatu kaṭṭi viṭukiṉṟaṉar! āṇmakkaḷip pōlavē peṇmakkaḷukkum aṟivuṇṭu, iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷ uṇṭu, viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ uṇṭu, paci kāmaṅkaḷ uṇṭu eṉpati nammaṉōr ciṟitāyiṉum uṇarntu pārkkiṉṟārkaḷilli. makaḷiri aṟivum uṇarvum aṟṟa iyantiraṅkaḷākavē niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷanāḷ uḷḷamaṭṭum āṭavartamakkuk kuṟṟēvalceytu vayiṟukaḻuvi avarkku iṉpantantu tām tuṉpattil vikum eḷiya vilaṅkukaḷākavē mātarkaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iciyātavaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭa ōr ēḻic ciṟumi avaṉāṟ paṭuntuṉpaṅkaḷip poṟukkamāṭṭāmal avaṉiviṭṭu nīṅkuvaḷāyiṉ, avaḷukkuc ciṟitum iraṅkāmal avaḷmēṟ paḻikūṟuvārē eṅkum uḷar! oru kiḻavaṉukku vāḻkkip paṭuttappaṭṭavaḷ cila nāṭkaḷil avaṉ iṟantupōka aḻutakaṇṇuñ cintiya mūkkumāy uyiraṟṟa piṇampōṟ kālaṅkaḻikkumpaṭi taṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻutuṅ kaṭṭāyappaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟāḷ; avaḷ ak kaṭuṅkaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟ  ciṟitu picakuvaḷāyiṉum avaṭku varum paḻiccollun tuṉpamum aḷaviṭappaṭā! ivvāṟu nampeṇ maṇikaḷit tuṉpuṟuttum āṭavarkaḷ tāmāvatu picakupaṭātu naṭakkiṉṟārkaḷā? eṉil, atuvumilli. oru keṭṭavaṉ ettaṉimuṟi vēṇṭumāṉālun tāṉ cākumaṭṭum makaḷir palari maṇantukoḷḷalām; vēcivīṭṭilēyē kuṭiyāyirukkalām; taṉakkuḷḷa poruḷi yellāṅ kuṭikkuṅ kūttukkuñ cūtukkum vampuvaḻakkukkum celavaḻittuviṭṭut, tam maṉivi makkaḷic cōṟṟukkun tuṇikkum alintu tiriyaviṭalām; tāṉ eḻupatu āṇṭu āṉālum patiṉintāṇṭuḷḷa oru ciṟumiyi maṇantu koḷḷalām. ivikaḷik kēṭpārilli. āṉāl, ivaṉukkup piṇikkappaṭṭa ēḻicciṟumiyō ikkoṭiya cātikkiḻavaṉiviṭṭu vilakuvaḷāyiṉ, avaḷ uṭaṉē kaṟpoḻukkattil vaḻuviyavaḷ āyviṭuvaḷām! aiyakō! avaṉukkut tāliyaṟutta avaḷ piṟaku vēṟoruvaṉi maṇantu koḷḷalākātām! atu cātikkuṟivām! peṇmakkaḷi ī eṟumpu kocukupōl nacukkum ittaṉi cātik koṭumikaḷ ōrālili mūkkuppōlirukkum icciṟiya teṉṉāṭṭi laṉṟi, ālilipōṟ paranta vēṟenta nāṭṭiluṅ kāṇap paṭuvatilli. maṟṟi nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa peṇpālār āṇpālārōṭoppa ellā urimikaḷum peṟṟu iṉitu vāḻntuvarutalāl, avarkaḷ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum aṟivilum aḻakilum āṇmiyilum piṟa nalaṅkiḷiluñ ciṟantu ulakiṟ payaṉ paṭṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. tuṉpattil uḻalum nam intumātar kaḷiṉ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum piḷkiḷō aṟivillā maṭiyarāy aḻakillā mucukkaḷāy avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa tīya taṉmiyun tīya ceyalumuṭiyarāyt tuṉpavāḻviṟpaṭṭu virivil uyirmāḷukiṉṟaṉar! ivvaḷāvuñ cāti vēṟṟumiyāl viḷinta tuviṉiyākum! oru peṇṇukku ēṟṟa iḷiñaṉ enta marapiṟ piṟantavaṉā yiruntālum, avaḷ avaṉi virumpuvāḷāyiṉ avaṉukkē avaḷi vāḻkkippaṭuttutal vēṇṭum. appōtutāṉ makaḷirkkuk kaṟpoḻukkam nilipeṟum. ēlātavaṉ oruvaṉukkup piṇikkappaṭṭavaḷ taṉakkēṟṟavaṉik kāṇa neruṅkāl avaṉi virumpātu irāḷ. iḵtu ellā makkaḷiṭattum māṟṟamuṭiyāta iyaṟkiyāka iṟivaṉāl amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. muṟṟuntuṟanta muṉivarkaḷum, makkaḷiṉuñ ciṟanta tēvarkaḷum ivviyaṟkiyik kaṭakkamāṭṭātu. tattaḷittaṉareṉṟāl, eḷiya makkaṭ piṟappiṉarāṉa ēḻi iḷampeṇkaḷik kuṟṟañ collutal aṭukkumō! ōr iḷiñaṉ taṉakku iciyātavaḷit, taṉakkumēl āṇṭiṉ mūtta oru kiḻaviyi maṇakkumpaṭi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭṭāl, avaṉ uḷḷam eppaṭik kotikkum! avaṉ avvēṟpāṭṭukku iṇaṅki naṭappaṉā? appaṭittāṉē ēḻi yiḷam peṇkaḷ maṉamum irukkum! ātalāṟ peṇmakkaḷi avar tamakku ēṟaṟa āṇmakkaḷōṭu maṇañ ceyvittalē uṭaṉē ceyaṟpālatu. cātik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷāl makaḷir oḻukkap piḻipaṭutalum, ataṉāṟ pala tīṅkukaḷ viḷitalum nāḷukkunāṭ perukukiṉṟaṉa! oru ciṟu cātikkuḷḷēyē nalla maṇavāḷaṉ kiṭitta laritu; oruvaṉ iruntāṟ peṇkaḷippeṟṟōriṟ celvarāy uḷḷōr pōṭṭipōṭṭukkoṇṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tokikaḷik koṭuttu avaṉi vilikku vāṅki viṭukiṉṟārkaḷ! aṅṅaṉam vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭa maṇamakaṉ mēlumēlun taṉ maṉiviyiṉ vaḻiyē poruḷ paṟikkum pēravāvuṭiyaṉāyp pōtaliṉ, avaṉtaṉ maṉiyāḷi iṭiyaṟātu tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉāl avviruvark kuḷḷum uyarnta kātalaṉpu illātākiṉṟatu. koḻunaṉum maṉiviyum aṉpilarāṉāl ilvāḻkki veṟum pāḻākiṉṟatu. iṉip poruḷ koṭuttu maṇamakaṉip peṟa vaḻi ilātavarō tamatu cātiyik kaṭantu tāṇṭa māṭṭārāy iciyātavaṉukkuṅ kiḻavaṉukkum peṇṇik koṭuttut  tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar! taṉ kaḻuttiṟkuk kayiṟu tāṉēyiṭṭukkoṇṭatupōla uṇmiyil illāta cātivēṟṟumiyiṉit tāmākavē vakuttukkoṇṭu ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉamellān tuṉpuṟu vatēṉ! nallarāyt tūyarāy iruppār ekkuṭip piṟantavarāyiruppiṉum avarkku makaḷik koṭuttalum avarpāl makaṭkōṭalum ellārun tuṇintu ceykuva rāyiṉ, maṉivikkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉum, kaṇavarkēṟṟa maṉiviyum vāyppar. ataṉāl, illaṟa vāḻkki uyarnta tūya iṉpavāḻkkiyām. ippōtuḷḷa tuṉpaṅkaḷum, maṇamakkaḷi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkutalum oḻiyum. makaḷiratu kaṟpoḻukkamum evaruñ colla vēṇṭāmalē iṉitu nilipeṟum.
iṉip, piṟacātiyāriliruntu tūyarāy varuvār kaṭippiṭiyāyc ceyaṟpālatum oṉṟuṇṭu. iḻintakuṭi oṉṟiṟ piṟantōr ataṟkuriya iḻinta taṉmikaḷi viṭṭut tūyarāy varumpōtu, tammi mīṇṭum avviḻinta kuṭikku uriya peyarāṟkūṟit tammittāmē tāḻvu paṭuttikkoḷḷal ākātu. oru tūyavar tammittāmē uyarttikkoḷḷa vaḻitēṭavēṇṭumē yallāmaṟ, piṟar tammi uyarttuvār eṉṟu nampiyiruttalākātu. ēṉeṉil, nammaṉōr cakkiyip piṭittuc cāṟṟi oḻukaviṭum paṉṉāṭiyip pōlvar; uyarntār oruvar pāl uḷḷa kuṟṟaṅkaḷi ārāyntu avaṟṟiyē pēcum nīrar allāmal, avarpāl uḷḷa uyarnta nalaṅkip pēcum iyalpiṉar allar. ātalāl, ittakiya tīya makkaḷiṭiyē tūyarāy uyarvār tamatu iḻikuṭip piṟappiyuriyātu, tammic civar eṉavum pārppaṉar eṉavuṅ kūṟi yoḻukutalvēṇṭum; tammōṭu uṭaṉiruntu uṇavukoḷḷātār vīṭṭil tāmum uṇaveṭuttal ākātu, immuṟiyi viṭāppiṭiyāyk koṇṭu oḻukiṉāṟṟāṉ kīḻōril tūyarāy varuvōr uyarakkūṭum ivvāṟu nāḷaṭiviṟ ceytē pārppaṉaruñ civarum uyarntaṉar. im muṟiyik kippaṟṟit tūyarāvār ellārun tāmum orumiyuṟṟup, piṟar ellāriyun tūyarākki orumippaṭuttic, cātivēṟṟumikaḷi aṟavē yoḻittu ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya tantikku iṉiya putalvarāy iṉitu vāḻvārāka!

cāti vēṟṟumiyum pōliccivarum
- muṟṟum -
mutaṟ patippu mukavuri
ki.pi. 1921 ām āṇṭu mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷil yām yāḻppāṇattil uḷḷa aṉparkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭu, aṅkē virivuri nikaḻttac ceṉṟu, yāḻppāṇa nakarattum ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa pala ūrkaḷiluñ civacittāntap poruḷkaḷiyun tamiḻcciṟappukaḷiyum eṭuttup pala virivurikaḷ nikaḻttiṉēm. av virivurikaḷi yellām peruntiraḷāṉa aṉparkaḷ āṅkāṅku vantukēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟaṉar. kaṭi muṟiyāka yām yāḻppāṇatti viṭṭup puṟappaṭutaṟkuc cila nāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ, aḵtāvatu ki.pi. 1922 ām āṇṭu tittiṅkaḷ mutalnāḷ aṉṟu yāḻppāṇa nakaramaṇṭapattil māli 6 maṇikkut tuvaṅkit tamiḻar nākarikam eṉṉum poruḷi virittup pēciṉēm. tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ varalāṟukaḷi avvirivuri putumuṟi ārāycciyāl naṉkeṭuttu viḷakkiṉamiyāl, avaṟṟi āṇṭuk kuḻumiyiruntu kēṭṭa aṟivuṭi aviyattār ellārum av virivuriyi mika viyantu pārāṭṭiṉar. av virivuriyiṉ arumi ilaṅkiyilum, yāḻppāṇattu naṉmakkaḷ mikutiyāyc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi vāḻum malāy nāṭukaḷilum virintu paravalāyiṟṟu. yāḻppāṇam, kantarōṭiyiliruntu ceṉṟu malāy nāṭṭil ireṅkāṉ eṉṉum ūriṟ pukivaṇṭinili utavit talivarāy (Assistant station Master) aluvalil amarntirunta tiruvāḷar na.ci. kantiyā avarkaḷ civacittānta uṇmikaḷiyun tamiḻiṉ arumperuñ ciṟappukaḷiyum nāṭeṅkum paravac ceyvatil nirampak karuttūṉṟiṉavarkaḷātaliṉ tamiḻar nākarikam eṉṉum av virivuriyi oru nūlāka acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭumpaṭi emmik kēṭṭuk koṇṭatōṭu, cekamat eṉṉum ūril araciṉar maruttuva viṭutiyil (Government Hospital) aluval pārkkun tiruvāḷar kumāracāmi avarkaḷi ūkki, avarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ naṇpar cilarumāka oruṅku cērntu annūṟ patippuc celavukku irunūṟu rūpā poruḷutavi ceyyumāṟum purintiṭṭārkaḷ. tamiḻil ittakiya putumuṟi ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutaṟku utavi ceytaliṉuñ ciṟanta aṟam vēṟu illi. ivvuyarnta aṟattic ceytuvarun tirukkantiyā avarkaṭkum avarkaḷiṉ naṇpar tiruk kumāracāmi avarkaṭkum avarkaḷōṭu ottu utavi ceyta maṟṟa naṇparkaṭkum yām peritum naṉṟi celuttukiṉṟēm.
kalvivaḷarcciyiṉ poruṭṭup pala naṉmuyaṟcikaḷi yām iṭiviṭātu ceytuvarutalāl, tamiḻar nākarikam eṉṉum itaṉi eḻuti veḷiyiṭutaṟku amayam vāyātu ēṟakkuṟiya oṉṟari āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa. piṟaku, nāṭṭukkōṭṭic ceṭṭimār muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ poruṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭun taṉaviciya ūḻiyaṉ eṉṉum itaḻil (ki.pi. 1923 ām āṇṭu āṭittiṅkaḷ 12 ām nāḷ) ataṉ talivar vēḷāḷar uṇmi varalāṟukaḷi aṟiyāmal avaric cūttirar eṉṟu ikaḻnatu, tam marapiṉaril uḷḷa āṭavarkaṭku vēḷāḷa marapiṉariliruntu peṇkaḷ eṭuttu maṇañ ceyvittuk koḷḷutalippaṟṟip pēciyaviṭattut, tām viciya vakuppiṉarākalāṉ tammavar tammiṟṟāḻnta cūttira vakuppiṉarāṉa vēḷāḷariṭamiruntu peṇkaḷ eṭuttu maṇañ ceytu koḷḷal muṟiyēyām eṉṟu eḻutiya kaṭṭuri oṉṟik kaṇṭēm.
appōtu namakku naṇparāyuḷḷa civa vēḷāḷar cilar vēḷāḷariṉ paṇṭi uṇmi varalāṟukaḷi ārāyntu oru kaṭṭuri virinteḻutumpaṭi emmip perituvēṇṭiṉar. paṇṭikkālan toṭṭu nam centamiḻ makkaḷil nākarikattāṟ ciṟantu vāḻntu, tamiḻmoḻiyiyuñ civa vaḻipāṭṭiyum niliniṟutti āriyariyun tirutti nalvaḻip paṭuttiṉōr vēḷāḷarē eṉpatu ematu ārāycciyil naṉku pulap paṭṭamiyāl, ‘vēḷāḷar yāvar? eṉpataṉi viḷakkukiyil tamiḻaratu nākarikat tiyum uṭaṉ viḷakka vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatāyt tāṉē vantu kūṭiṟṟu. ākavē, yām muṉṉar eḻutak kuṟittirunta tamiḻar nākarikam eṉpataṉiyum itaṉkaṇ virittu viḷakka iṭam peṟṟēm. āriyar ivvintiya nāṭṭiṟ pukum muṉṉarē tamiḻar nākarika vāḻkkiyil mutirccipeṟṟu niṉṟu, piṉṉart tammoṭu vantu kalanta avvāriyarit tiruttiṉami itaṉkaṇ naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. paḻantamiḻ nūlārāycciyiṉṟi, vaṭa nūṟ payiṟci oṉṟēyuṭiyār āriyari uyarttut tamiḻari iḻittuk kūṟuvaṉavellām poruntāmiyun, tamiḻarē evvāṟṟāṉum uyarn tōrātalum ivitammi ārāyātārkkup putumiyāyt tōṉṟiṉum, yām ārāyntu cāṉṟukaḷōṭu urippaṉa vaṟṟi naṭunili vaḻātu niṉṟu kāṇpārkku em uriyiṉ meymmi tāṉē viḷaṅkutal tiṇṇam.
- maṟimali aṭikaḷ
iraṇṭām patippu mukavuri
ivviraṇṭām patippiṟ pala putiyakuṟippukaḷ viritteḻutic cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. muṟpatippil iruntavikaḷiṟ ciṟcila tiruttaṅkaḷuñ ceyyappaṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟaṟṟāl innūl muṉṉiyiṉum orupaṅku kūṭapperukiviṭṭatu.
iṉit tamiḻaruṭ ciṟanta vēḷāḷar nākarikatti viḷakkavē tamiḻaratu nākarikamun tāṉē peṟappaṭumākalāṉum, mutaṟ patippukku vitta `tamiḻar nākarikam’ allatu `vēḷāḷar yāvar?  eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutaṟku iṭar payattalāṉum ip patippukku avviraṇṭiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya `vēḷāḷar nākarikam eṉpataṉip peyarāka amittām.
pallāvaram							        maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
ki.pi. 1927

Preface to the Second Edition
The first edition of this work was published in November, 1923 and 500 copies of it were sold within four years - a period too short for the sale of a Tamil book of this kind which in contradistinction to the current fashion for a mixed kind of Tamil prose, is written in a scrupulously pure Tamil style, setting forth at the same time views of a revolutionary character in the sphere of social, religious and historical ideas of the Tamil people. In point of truth the views themselves are neither strange nor revolutionary, being the outcome of a comparative study carefully made of both Tamil and Sanscritr literature. If they appear strange surely they must appear so, to many it is becuse their knowledge is partial: and where that knowledge clims to be derived from the literatures of the two languages, it does not go beyond the pale of modern Puranic literature of the two.
No doubt, beneath the legendary accounts of Puranas critics of trenchant intellect - such are rarely to be met with in this country, may detect facts of great value for studying the history of ancient people, but others, who are untrined in the critical and historical methods of looking at things and events, take every bit of them as so many literal truths. People in every country, befopre they get the benefits of an excellent education, are moree credulous than children, since children cannot rest satisfied in a blind belief with respect to a certin thing or an occurrence, unless they receive satisfying answers to all they want to know about the one or the other. This, the self-interested persons know full well and such of them as are versed in the knowledge of a language know also how to work upon the people’s fancy by making that language as a medium for their artifices. This was how the huge body of Puranic literature came to be written first in Sanscrit and then translated into Tamil and other living languages in India. Sanscrit being the dead and not the spoken language understood by people, the authors of the Puranas adopted it is the means best suited to their purpose and sid in them all that was to be sid in their own interest and to the great disadvantage of the people. And as if to give a finishing and successful stroke to their artifices, not only did they uniformly conceal their human authorship of the Puranas, but they also attributed the composition of which invariably to Divine Beings such as Siva and Narayana. Being Divine utterances their contents were tught to be unquestionable under penalty of hell-fire to unbelievers. Having thus established the unquestionable authority of the Puranas, their authors, selecting for the basis a few cardinal facts, did their work excellently by exaggerating them so much out of all proportion, twisting them so much out of their shape, and combining them so much out of all hormony, that, to the degree they succeeded in the abnormal treatment of the subject, the legends appealed the more strongly to the minds of the credulous and led their belief to take deep root in them. As a consequence of this artifice people have become slaves to blind faith and ineradicable supersitition and they completely distrust that there can be anything as reason to question about their truth or untruth. Nay the very attitude of a person who dares to doubt the legend is repugnant to the people and they are sure to look upon him as their bitterest enemy and heretic. The hold which the Puranas thus have on the mind of the people and the intensity of the prejudice which they foster in their mind aginst a man who doubts them, one may best learn by nothing the strength of the Puranic belief even in those who have imbibed western education.
Under this deplorable condition it is no wonder that Tamil books written on modern critical and historical lines should seem strange and revolutionary to men of such mental calibre. Still the sale of 500 copies within four years betokens a brightening prospect and authors of my type need not despir of a better condition in future. For man cannot always rest in ignorance. He is ever prompted by an innate desire to learn the nature and constitution of things and ascertin the cuses that are at work within the heart of things. He is ever free to think on life and life -struggles and no power can restrin his thought nor can hold it eternally captive. To this freedom of thinking, the spread of western education is contributing its mighty and accelerating influence and we, on our part must do our utmost to bring the people to think for themselves and examine their beliefs in the lightr of reason before communicating them to their youngsters.
The object of this book is to examine some of the important beliefs that have clustered around the mind of the people and to rid it of such beliefs as are likely to mislead them or to hamper their progress in the path of right knowledge.
In the first place attention is directed to Velalas the civilized agricultural class of the Tamils, and to their origin, and organization. From references made to them, their occupation and social rank, in Tholkappiam the most ancient and existing Tamil work in all its entirety, the age of which goes back to 3500 B.C., it is shown that at a time when all the people except those who lived all along the equatorial regions, were leading the life of hunters or nomads, these Velalas attined perfection in the art of agriculture, built towns and strong forts, had been priests, kings and traders and by means of navigation occupied the whole of India settling in rich and fertile countries along the coasts and river - sides.
When the Aryan hordes came from the north-west of Punjab and poured forth into the interior, it was the ten velala kings then ruling in the north that stopped their advance (See Ragozin’s Vedic India.). When after a while, the intellectual section of the Aryan nomads found it impossible to get admission into the Tamilian territories by combating with their kings, they sought it by peaceful means and were thereafter accorded a cordial reception and admitted to a high rank in the Tamilian society. To the few stray hymns brought by the Aryans, the Tamil kings added a greater number of their own: and, in imitation of their four Tamil vedas which treated of practical ethics, political economy, love and heavenly bliss, they classified the hymns into four books and called them ‘Vedas’. That the word ‘Veda’ is a pure Tamil one, is etymologically proved in the body of this work, by grouping a number of pure Tamil words all having a single root ‘Ve’ and cognate in meaning, After a century of laborious research in linguistic matters, oriental scholars in Europe and America are just now coming to recognise that not only was the Taqmilian civilization prior to the Aryan but it was also much superior to it and was the great formative force that modelled it after its own.
(see prof. Rapson’s Ancient India).
In spite of the repeated efforts which the Taqmil kings made, as is manifest from the Chandogya, Kaushi taki and other upansihads, to divert the attention of the Aryan priests from costly rituals supposed to bring only transient benefits, to a contgemplation of eternal verities and problems of philosophy, the priests were persisting in the performance of bloddy sacrifices so much so that as time went on their conduct became more and more revolting to the delicate feelings of the humanitarian Velals.
So long as the Tamil kings and rich trading communities yielded to the wishes and devices of the Aryan priests and lavished their wealth on rituals, the latter pretended to treat them with utmost respect by designating them the Kshatriyas and Vaisyas, while in fact they were positing themselves openly at the zenith in the scale of castes and casting down others secretly much below. But from the moment the kings and nobles and others began to suspect the motives of the Aryan priests, these supercilious parasites gave up their bloody sacrifices but devised other effective means to such the wealth of the Tamilians. At first they brought all the Tamils under the three denomination of Kshatriya, Vaisya and Sudra, formulated rigid rules exclusively for each and ordined numerous ceremonies toeach, doing all these, byu means of a dead language Sanscrit, as if in the interest of the people’s welfare both here and hereafter, but in fact to establish their supremacy among them and make their priestly help seem indispensable to the people for consultin them on such rules and conducting the ceremonies prescribed for each. In this design the Aryan priests succeeded so well, that the Tamils whether kings or nobles, rich or poor, learned or ignorant, all have become thorough slaves no only to the Aryan priests but also to all who have joined the Aryan fold and bear the name of brahmin. After this the further work of vilifying the Tamils was made much easier, and all those who, in course of time, styled themselves brahmins discovered it, to their great benefit and glory, to efface the three grades of distinctions into which their predecessors classed the Tamils and to put them all together under the generic term ‘Sudra’ which means but the contemptuous menials as a whole. But in the Tamil country nobody will call himself a Sudra, or a Vaisya or a Kshatriya. The Tamils are either agriculturists or traders, artisans or labourers; every class of people follows a hereditary profession and calls themselves by the name of that profession. But quite recently a great mania has taken possession of some classes of people whose professions though much useful are looked upon as low by brahmins and their imitators, to bring themselves under the Aryan appellations of brahmin, kshatriya and visya and escape being called the Sudra. This mania is setting one class aginst the other and breeds enmity and arrogance in each. All this mischief and injustice of classifying the Tamils who are above such caste iniquities, are exposed in the body of this treatise and remedial measures suggested.
Unfortunately owing to the influfence of brahmins, European scholars devoted their attention exclusively to Sanscrit and have acquired the most amazing mastery in it. Had they spent half so much labour on ancient Tamil classics, the result would have been more fruitful, more accurate and more enlightening in the study of ancient Indian history, Philosophy and religion.My truth dispel falsehood and love overcome hate and God who is both truth and lvoe illumine our understanding!
The Sacred Order of love,	Vedachalam
Pallavaram 20, July, 1927

1. vēḷāḷar vāḻkki
ippōtirukkun tamiḻnūlkaḷil mikap paḻiyatākiya tolkāppiyattil vēḷāḷarkaḷum avarkaḷukkē ciṟappāka uriya uḻavutoḻilum oruṅkēvittuc collap paṭutal peritum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. tolkāppiyam aiyāyirattu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa nūlākiyāl, atiṟ kuṟippiṭṭa vēḷāḷarkaḷ annūlukku muṟpaṭṭa kālat tilēyē aḵtāvatu āṟāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyē uḻavu toḻilik kaṇṭaṟintu perukki, ataṉāl tāmum nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu, piṟariyuñ ciṟappuṟac ceytu vāḻntami pulaṉām. āṭu māṭu mēykkun toḻili mēṟkoṇṭu, ovvoru kālattil ovvōriṭattiṟ kuṭiyēṟik, kaṭiciyāka ip parata nāṭṭiṟ pukunta āriyar aññāṉṟu vaṭakkē vāḻnta vēḷāḷariṉ uḻavu toḻiṟ ciṟappum, ataṉāl avar peṟṟa nākarika vāḻkkiyuṅkaṇṭu viyantu av vēḷāḷari aṇṭip piḻikkalāyiṉar. vēḷāḷarun tamakkuḷḷa celvap perukkālum, irakka neñcattālun tampāl vantu tamatu utaviyi avāviya āriyarkku uṇṇa uṇavum uṭukka uṭiyum irukka iṭamun tantu palavāṟṟāmalum avarkaḷip pātukāttuvantaṉar. ākavē, vēḷāḷar eṉṉuñ col īkiyuṭiyār eṉṉum poruḷil avarkkē vaḻaṅki varalāyiṟṟu. vēḷāṇmi eṉṉuñcol iṅṅaṉam paṇṭutoṭṭu īkip poruḷil vaḻaṅkutal, vēḷāṇmi yupakāram īkiyum viḷampum eṉṉum paḻiya tivākarac cūttirattāl viḷaṅkum.
uḻavutoḻilō mikavum varuttamāṉa toṉṟu, uḻavu toḻilic ceypavarkaṭkē varuttam iṉṉateṉpatu teriyum; ataṉic cevviyāyc ceytu muṭippataṟkō muṉpiṉ ārāyum nuṇṇaṟivu vēṇṭum. ātalāl, ataṉic ceyvārkkē uyarnta aṟivum avvaṟiviṉip payaṉ
paṭuttum muṟikaḷum viḷaṅkum. ātaliṉāṟṟāṉ, vēḷāḷarkku irakkamum aṟivum īkiyun toṉṟutoṭṭu varum iyalpu kaḷākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tammiyotta makkaḷ vaṟumi yālum nōyālun tuṉpuṟak kaṇṭāl, avarkkuḷḷa attuṉpattiṉ koṭumiyiṉi niṉinturuki, avi tammip poruḷālum maruntālum nīkkavallavarkaḷ vēḷāḷarkaḷē yāvar. piṟar tarum poruḷip peṟṟut tam meyvaruntāmal illattiruntu iṉitu kālaṅkaḻippavarkkup, piṟar paṭuntuyar teriyātāki yāl aṉṉavar vaṟiyōrukkum nōyāḷarkkum piṟarkkuñ ciṟitum iraṅkār. ātaliṉāṟṟāṉ, vēḷāḷa rallāta piṟar piṟarkku utavi ceytal aritāyirukkiṉṟatu. īkiyum viruntōmpalum vēḷāḷarkkē ciṟantaṉavāka vittu nūlkaḷ ōtutalum piṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭum.
iṉit, tammōṭotta makkaḷiṉ tuyar kaḷitalēyaṉṟi, makkaḷiṉun tāḻnta āṭu māṭu kutiri kōḻi kokku mutalāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷ paṭun tuṉpattiyum naṉkuṇarntu, avikaṭkut tammālum piṟar tammālun tīṅku nērātapaṭi kollā aṟatti vaḷarkkavalla āṟṟalum vēḷāḷarkkē urittāyiṟṟu.
uyirōṭirukkum makkaḷukkum makkaḷiṉun tāḻnta ciṟṟuyirkaḷukkum utaviyāṟṟutalōṭu amiyātu, tammip peṟṟu vaḷarttup periyarākkiya tam mūtātikaḷiṉ pērutaviyiyum niṉintu, avarkaḷ ivvulaka vāḻvit tuṟantu maṟumiyulakaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟa piṉṉum, avarkaḷatu uyir tūya piṟaviyiyō piṟaviyoḻintu iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭiyiyō aṭiyumāṟu iṟivaṉi vēṇṭi vaḻuttutalum vēḷāḷarkkē urittāyiṟṟu.
iṉit, tamataṟivu vaḷarccikkum muyaṟciyiṉ īṭēṟṟattiṟkum iṉṟiyamiyāta vaḻivakikaḷik kāṭṭi avviraṇṭiṉuñ ciṟanta tammuṉṉōrkaḷ eḻutivitta nūlkaḷi ōti, avvāṟṟāṟ kalviyip paravacceytalum vēḷāḷarkkē ciṟantatōr aṟamāyiṟṟu.
ākavē, īkikkup peyarāṉa vēḷāṇmi eṉṉuñ
col, av vīkikkuk karuviyāṉa payirttoḻilukkum peyarāyiṟṟu. piṉṉar avviraṇṭu toḻilkaḷiyum āḷvārāṉa vēḷāḷarkkum atu peyarāyiṟṟu. iv vēḷāḷar ēṉi makkaḷi nōkkic ceyyum īkiyum viruntōmpalum makkaḷvēḷvi (mānuṭayākam) eṉavum, ēṉic ciṟṟuyirkaḷip pātukāttaṟ poruṭṭu  avar mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukuṅkollā aṟam uyirvēḷvi (pūtayākam) eṉavum, iṟantu paṭṭa tam muṉṉōri nōkkic ceyyum naṉṟikkaṭaṉ teṉ pulattār vēḷvi (pitiryākam) eṉavum, ivvāṟu piṟarkkāṟṟum utavi payaṉtaraṟ poruṭṭu iṟivaṉi nōkkic ceyyum vaḻipāṭu kaṭavuḷ vēḷvi (tēvayākam) eṉavun tamakkum piṟarkkum aṟivi viḷakki muyaṟciyip payaṉ peṟuvikkum nūlōtumuṟi kalivēḷvi (piramayākam) eṉavum āṉṟōrāl vakutturikkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē īkiyum īkikkuk karuviyāvaṉavum, īkiyip pārāṭṭum naṉṟikkaṭaṉum īkiyip payaṉpaṭuttumāṟu vēṇṭum vaḻipāṭum vēḷvi eṉṉum peyarkku iyipuṭiyavāy niṟṟal kaṇṭukoḷka. kollā aṟam, ciṟṟuyirkaḷ tattam uṭampukaḷil niṉṟu aṟivu viḷaṅkutaṟku utavi ceyvatākaliṉ, atuvum vēḷvi eṉṉum peyarkkup peritum urimi pūṇṭu niṟṟal kaṇṭu koḷka. iṅṅaṉamāka aivaki vēḷvikaḷiyum āḷpavarākaliṉ, paṇṭit tamiḻaril uḻavutoḻilāṟ ciṟantu nākarika vāḻkkiyi vakutta naṉmakkaḷ vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭuvārāyiṉa reṉpatu. 
uḻavutoḻili yaṟintu ataṉit tiṟamiyākac ceyyat teriyāta kālaṅkaḷil, makkaḷ uṇṇap pōtumāṉa uṇavum uṭukkac cevviyāka uṭiyum iṉṟi mikavum miṭippaṭṭu uyirvāḻntaṉar; kāṭukaḷilum malikaḷilum uḷḷa māṉ, mari, kaṭampi, muḷḷampaṉṟi, āṭu, māṭu mutalāṉa vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷi aḷaviṟanta varuttattōṭu vēṭṭamāṭik koṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyiyum aritiṟ kiṭitta kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum ayiṉṟu, taḻikaḷiyum tōliyum uṭuttu, nākarikam iṉṉateṉṟē teriyāmal malikkuki kaḷilum marap pontukaḷilum iruntu kālaṅkaḻittaṉar. evvaḷavō tēṭittirintum av vilaṅkukaḷum kāy mutaliyaṉavum akappaṭāta kālaṅkaḷiṟ paṭṭiṉiyum paciyumāyiruntu avarkaḷ poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpattiṟ kaḻitta nāṭkaḷum pala! vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyil ippōtum nākarikamiṉṟi uyirvāḻum mali vāṇaruṅ kāṉavarum nirampavum miṭippaṭṭa niliyiliruttali nām iññāṉṟum  nērē ceṉṟu kāṇalām, iṅṅaṉamākap paṇṭikkālattil vaṟumiyiṟ kiṭantuḻaṉṟa makkaḷiṉ koṭun tuṉpamellām, uḻavu toḻili mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭaṟinta aṟivuṭi naṉmakkaḷālētām nīṅkiṉa. tamiḻmakkaḷuḷ vēḷāḷar uḻavu toḻilik kaṇṭaṟinta paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil, ekupati, cālaṭi mutalāṉa ciṟcila nāṭukaḷil uṟinta makkaṭ pakutiyiṉar ciṟcilarit tavira, ēṉip perumpālār ellārum uḻavu toḻili aṟiyārāy vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyiṟ peritum vaṟumippaṭṭu vāḻnāṭ kaḻittaṉar. vēḷāḷar uḻavu toḻili naṭātti nākarikattip perukkac ceyta paṇṭi nāḷil, āriyar vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyilum āṭu māṭu mēykkum iṭiyar vāḻkkiyilumē iruntaṉar. ataṉālētāṉ, āriyarum avar vaḻippaṭṭāruñ ceyta nūlkaḷil uḻavu toḻil iḻitturikkappaṭṭiruppatōṭu, ataṉit tam miṉattavar evaruñ ceytalākāteṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuppāṭuṅ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.1
āṉāṟ, ṟamiḻa vēḷāḷārō avvuḻavu toḻilit tamatu nuṇṇaṟivāṟ kaṇṭuṇarntu ataṉic cevviyākac ceytu vantamiyāl, attoḻil vēḷāḷarkkē ciṟantatāka vittu vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyiluḷḷa nūlkaḷellām orumukamāy niṉṟu otturippavāyiṉa. ivvāṟu uḻavu toḻili vēḷāḷarkaḷ tōṟṟuvittu piṟaku tāṉ uṇavukkum uṭikkum uṟiviṭattiṟkum miṭippaṭṭa tuṉpa vāḻkki nīṅkiṟṟu. uṇavukkāka vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷik kolluṅ kolit toḻil nīṅkiṟṟu; aḷavukkumēl viḷinta nel tuvari mutalāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷit tampōṟ paciyāl varuntum ēṉimakkaṭkum pakuttuk koṭukkum īra neñcamum īkiyuṅ kiḷippavāyiṉa; viḷinta paṇṭaṅ kaḷip pātukāttu vaḻaṅkutaṟku aracaṉum nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷuñ celvamuṅ kalviyum iṉpavāḻkkiyum iṟivaṉ vaḻipāṭum oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟāyp perukālāyiṉa. iṉṉum mikuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭuvateṉ! iññāṉṟi makkaṭku vantaḷḷa nākarika vāḻvellām paṇṭum iṉṟum vēḷāḷar kaṇṭaṟintu naṭāttum uḻaviṉāl vantaṉavē yāmeṉṟuṇarntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. 
iṅṅaṉam ellāt toḻiluñ ciṟantatāy ellār uyirvāḻkkikkum iṉṟiyamiyātatāy uḷḷa uḻavu toḻilip paṇṭutoṭṭu naṭāttivarum vēḷāḷar koliyum puliyum nīkki nākarikattiṟ ciṟantārāy viḷaṅkutaliṉ avaratu perumi paḻiya nallāciriyar iyaṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ pārāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatōṭu, aḵtu iṉṟukāṟum maṅkātu ēṉi yellā vakuppiṉarkkuriya perumiyiṉum mikku viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷa aṟiviṉ ṟiṟattāṟ, kālamaṟintu nilattit tirutti vaḷampaṭutti nel mutalāṉa naṉceyppayirum tuvari mutalāṉa puṉceyp payirum viḷivittu, avaṟṟāl varum payaṉkaḷit tāmum uṇṭu piṟarum uṇṇak koṭuttu, yāṭu māṭu mīṉ mutalāṉa maṟṟi uyirkaḷik kollāmaluṅ, koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉit tiṉṉāmalum aruḷoḻukkattil iṉṟu kāṟun tali niṉṟu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷatu uṇmip perumiyiṉi yuḷḷavāṟuṇarntē teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
